**Confirmed Automatic Highlight profile:** *The Medicine Road* retains universal, hero-normalized **Perfect Combo** and **Two Threats One Move** legality, excluding protected cargo, objective props and repeated contacts on one enemy. **Guardian Intervention** recognizes stopping a committed Cargo Snatcher, warned Road Blocker action, Tow-Chain action, repair interruption or Luo Jian barrier attack before objective condition worsens. **Defensive Mastery** recognizes an Exact Block or Counter against a designated Blocker strike, dangerous Bike Harrier pass, or Luo Jian mounted/baton attack followed by a legal damage-free punish. **Team Harmony** recognizes one hero completing a repair, case recovery, route clearance or optional delivery while the partner interrupts a designated threat, followed by a secured objective state. **Environmental Ingenuity** recognizes a barrier, cable reel, tool cart, drainage pipe or throwable fragment interrupting an objective actor while meaningfully affecting another hostile.

Detection cannot reward deliberate cargo displacement, intentional vehicle damage, repeated repair interruption, warning delay or environmental farming. Screenshot selection favours successful intervention and may not depict patients or clinic staff as injured. The shared default-on toggle, maximum-three diversified selection, bounded candidate capture, localized explanations, replay reconstruction, export, Museum pin, privacy, performance and no-reward rules apply unchanged.

**Confirmed mount and movement — Bounded Medical-Convoy Pursuit Contract:** Solo receives one stage-local mid-class motorbike and co-op one per hero. Bikes use inherited depth-lane steering, capped acceleration, braking, hazard avoidance, one universal mounted strike and voluntary dismount. Players cannot steer or board the clinic vehicle, which follows an authored deterministic route between secure anchors. Each bike has separate Stability; zero causes a safe nonlethal dismount without explosion, lethal collision, cargo damage or civilian injury. Equipped weapons are secured during mounted transitions and restored at the next legal dismount, while mounted play adds no weapon, Special or Ultimate routes.

Every mounted section has a complete on-foot fallback: dismounted heroes evade passes, attack during readable low-speed turns, redirect riders into tagged barriers and advance on an authored roadside path. The clinic vehicle slows at the next secure anchor so no hero or partner is abandoned. Bike loss may modestly affect combat or efficiency results but never Medical Delivery Integrity, completion, cargo arrival or branch access. In co-op, one hero may intercept while the other protects or proceeds on foot; route boundaries prevent indefinite separation and force safe regrouping. Lost bikes do not respawn before the anchor, and restoration preserves their exact outcome. Higher difficulty adds coordinated passes, braking feints and staggered lane pressure rather than unreadable speed; reduced effects simplify presentation without changing lanes, timing or warnings.

**Confirmed bike handoff:** Each hero receives one persistent stage-local bike for the full run. A voluntarily parked intact bike is staged at the next secure anchor and returns for the next mounted section with exact remaining Stability. A bike reduced to zero Stability is permanently unavailable for the rest of that run; no anchor or checkpoint repairs or replaces it. Loss during Harrier Run therefore carries into later pursuits and Luo Jian's mounted phase, all of which retain complete on-foot fallback. Co-op bikes are independent, so one hero may remain mounted while the other proceeds on foot.

**Confirmed encounter baseline — Eight-Encounter Escort Rhythm:** **Dispatch Test** teaches clearance with Road Blockers and no cargo loss. **First Bend** combines moving defense, Blockers and Bike Harriers. **Cedar Rest Raid** introduces one visible Cargo Snatcher channel with Ridge Lookout support. **Harrier Run** provides bounded pursuit and on-foot fallback before CP1. **Tunnel Breakdown** protects the wheel repair against Blockers, Lookouts and one controlled cargo threat. **Tunnel Exit Blockade** mixes route clearance after repair before CP2. **Viaduct Seizure** is the strongest multi-lane cargo-removal attempt and includes displaced-case recovery. **Turnout Repair Siege** applies mixed pressure during the cargo-restraint repair before CP3 and Luo Jian. Optional-branch battles do not count among the eight mandatory encounters.

Initial active-hostile caps are Easy 3 solo/5 co-op, Medium 4/6, Hard 5/7, Extreme 5/7 and Ultimate 6/8. Reserves enter through readable authored positions and cannot begin an objective channel before becoming visible and interruptible. Co-op adds tactical coverage rather than doubling Health; solo permits one approaching objective action and co-op two staggered actions against different targets. Extreme replaces one ordinary Viaduct slot with the Tow-Chain Enforcer. Ultimate retains that challenge and replaces a Turnout support slot with the Dispatcher; they cannot create simultaneous unavoidable objective actions. `QUIETCROWD` reduces ordinary bodies but retains selected advanced roles and objective logic. Difficulty changes combinations, coordination, feints, recovery discipline and threat timing rather than simply multiplying durability. Exact Health, damage, spawn frames and attack-token values remain balancing data within the validated browser-performance budget.

**Confirmed rewards — Care over Profit Reward Set:** Every valid first clear grants an itemized difficulty-adjusted Credit award, the Medicine Road Canon-Compatible Side Story Chronicle entry, the regional mobile-clinic network Museum exhibit, the mission background-music track for Museum listening, and **The Delivery Arrives / 送药抵达 / Die Lieferung kommt an**. First A rank grants a text-free Six-Case Convoy profile emblem. First S rank grants the cosmetic-only **Clinic Route Patrol / 医疗路线巡护装 / Patrouillenfarbset der Medizinroute** colourway to all four eligible heroes; it changes no statistic, resource, objective or scoring property and occupies no combat-style outfit slot. First Ultimate clear grants the profile title **Road of Care / 仁心之路 / Weg der Fürsorge**.

The achievement set is **The Delivery Arrives** for a valid clear; **Care Reaches Every Road** for both optional branches in one eligible run; **Every Seal Intact / 封签完好 / Jedes Siegel unversehrt** for six Intact seals, both repairs completed, and vehicle Roadworthy or Stabilized; and **Guardian of the Medicine Road / 送药之路守护者 / Wächter der Medizinroute** for S on Hard or above. Locked achievements and Museum rewards show localized requirement clues. None requires a particular hero, purchase, co-op partner or combo route. First-clear, rank-improvement and Ultimate bonuses are profile-once transactions; replays grant only bounded anti-farm Credits. Cheat-Assisted clears retain completion, Chronicle and base Museum access but grant no Credits, rank rewards, achievements, Mastery, emblem, colourway or title.

**Confirmed presentation — Road Reaches Them Presentation Arc:** Skippable opening **Before the Long Road** shows six sealed cases checked at the Council Medical Dispatch Yard, the clinic coordinator explaining that remote communities have exhausted essential supplies, and the selected heroes accepting protective responsibility before dawn departure. The mission briefing presents route, cargo categories, controls, loadouts, difficulty and Automatic Highlight toggle. Hero selection may alter short reactions but never premise or outcome. Secure-anchor exchanges report current case/vehicle condition, repairs and optional-branch acknowledgements without interrupting active threats.

At the Remote Clinic Approach, Luo Jian openly demands the shipment for resale and the heroes reject converting remote patients' needs into gang profit; the mounted fight begins only after player control and warnings return. After defeat he is restrained, medically assessed and confirms the independent resale motive. Skippable ending **The Road Reaches Them** shows all six cases received and orderly distribution beginning. Vehicle condition, Scuffed seals, repairs and optional branches receive state-aware acknowledgements, but essential delivery always succeeds. The final image shows the clinic vehicle preparing for another route. Sheng Guo's Chronicle commentary states that prosperity becomes peace only when its benefits travel beyond the village.

English, Simplified Chinese and German subtitles/transcripts are complete, with text and installed voice languages independently selectable. Dialogue is subtitle-first; Windows TTS remains prototype/accessibility fallback unless deployment rights are independently established. Any generated footage is text-free, low-motion and based on validated start/end frames, with localization added in-game. Every scene supports skip, Museum replay and a localized still-panel fallback.

**Confirmed audio — Roads of Care Audio Suite:** Seven original cues are **Dawn Dispatch / 黎明启程 / Aufbruch im Morgengrauen**, **Wheels Along Changling / 长岭行车 / Fahrt über Changling**, **Blockade at the Bend / 弯道封锁 / Blockade an der Kurve**, **Light in the Service Tunnel / 隧道灯火 / Licht im Versorgungstunnel**, **Cases on the Viaduct / 高架桥上的药箱 / Die Kisten auf dem Viadukt**, **The Roadmaster's Demand / 路霸的索求 / Die Forderung des Straßenherrn**, and **The Road Reaches Them / 送药抵达远方 / Die Hilfe erreicht ihr Ziel**. Plucked/bowed acoustic strings, bamboo flute, warm low winds, hand percussion, wooden blocks and a subtle modern pulse create the identity; travelling rhythm evokes turning wheels without literal engines as percussion. Music has no lyrics, generated voices, recognizable borrowed melody or artist imitation.

Gameplay cues provide loopable exploration, pressure, pursuit, repair, boss and resolution stems controlled only by stable presentation events. SFX distinguish clinic vehicle, hero bikes and Harrier bikes; case latch/seal/displacement/recovery/lock; both repairs; Blocker, sling, Tow-Chain and Dispatcher warnings; tunnel lighting, route beacon, barriers, pipes and viaduct surfaces; and Luo Jian mounted, baton, disarm and restraint actions. Cargo threats, repair interruption, boss telegraphs, Exact Blocks, Counters and Combo Breakers receive mix priority, with music/ambience ducking. Meaningful sounds have localized captions and visual equivalents; reduced audio may simplify engines, crowds and debris but not warnings. Generated candidates require editing, provenance, normalization and commercial-rights clearance before use; approved tracks enter the Museum under established rewards.

**Confirmed archive — Delivery-to-Archive Result Ledger:** Five expandable result sections cover **Mission Record** (difficulty, configuration, heroes, loadouts, ruleset, continues, assistance, cheats, network and version), **Delivery Report** (vehicle, all six case categories/custody/seals/history, both repairs, route incidents and fallbacks), **Protective Duty** (mechanic, clinic/civilian safety, collateral, surrender/restraint and Luo phases), **Performance Evaluation** (four grade components, points, rank, caps, branches, rewards and noncompetitive co-op attribution), and **Highlights and Archive** (up to three localized Highlight cards, pin/export/reconstruction and compatible-category personal-best comparison).

The compact result is committed before Highlight reconstruction, so cancellation/failure cannot lose results or rewards. Shared tiered replay retention keeps full capsules for category bests, five latest Curated runs, manually pinned replays and Museum-pinned Highlights; older unprotected records retain facts and explanations as text-only entries. The Museum case contains the regional mobile-clinic overview, route/vehicle artwork, non-actionable six-case explanation, optional beacon lens and clinic-route token, Luo Jian's independent-gang summary, scenes, approved music/artwork and Sheng Guo's Chronicle commentary. Locked exhibits show localized clues. No fictional patient names, personal medical records or actionable pharmaceutical instructions appear. Cheat-Assisted runs may reveal the base case and Chronicle but retain an unranked/unrewarded label.

**Confirmed learning/access — Medical Escort Learning and Access Profile:** First mission access provides localized Guide card **Medical Escort Operations / 医疗护送行动 / Medizinischer Geleitschutz**, covering all four ledgers, case states, visible objective channels, normal/fallback clearance, bike/on-foot play, solo/co-op threat limits, grading, branches, Highlights and current bindings. It remains searchable during pause and requires no video, audio, pointer or Academy completion. Installing the mission adds optional **Medicine Road Convoy Drill / 送药护送训练 / Übung für die Medizinroute**, which teaches normal/fallback clearance, Snatcher interruption and case recovery, mechanic protection, riding/safe Stability loss/on-foot completion, and co-op repair-defense/partner-rescue. Practice cargo cannot be lost; the drill grants no Credits/rank, exclusive power or mission gate.

Cargo identity/state and threats use redundant shape, icon, pattern, localized text, audio, screen-reader, target-line, silhouette, caption and optional-vibration cues rather than colour alone. Repair, recovery, restraint and branch interactions support hold/toggle/reduced-input forms; riding provides steering sensitivity, camera centring and route guidance. Dust, reflection, shake, motion, particles, engine variation and debris reduce independently while preserving simulation. Non-simulation access keeps eligibility; simulation-changing timing, steering, pressure or damage help uses Assisted records.

No mission-specific cheat is added. Applicable hero codes plus `QUIETCROWD`, ordinary-provision-only `FULLPANTRY`, carried Baton/Tire-Iron integrity `TRUSTYTOOLS`, player-bike-only `STEADYRIDE`, and exact-snapshot `HOMECOMING` apply. `SAFEPASSAGE` displays Not Applicable because essential delivery already cannot enter completion-blocking permanent failure and the code cannot preserve grading condition. Any active code marks the run Cheat-Assisted under shared restrictions.

**Confirmed technical behavior — Authoritative Convoy and Scalable Presentation Contract:** In local-Wi-Fi play the host owns route/encounter activation, clinic-vehicle movement/condition, all case identity/custody/seal/carrier/recovery locks, repairs, clearance, branches, enemy AI, motorbike Stability, objective channels, Luo phases/restraint, score, caps, achievements, checkpoints, rewards and result transaction. Peers send frame-stamped abstract inputs; prediction may animate intent but cannot commit objective, boss, reward or achievement state. Simultaneous interactions resolve by authoritative frame and stable player identity, preventing duplicate case carriers, repair completion or restraint ownership. Rollback cannot duplicate SFX, warnings, rewards or consequences.

Reconnect/host recovery restores the latest validated authoritative state without improvement. Delay cannot change channels, warnings or schedules; version/package mismatch blocks readiness with localized correction. Automatic Highlights add no network traffic and reconstruct locally. Performance targets 60 FPS with supported 30-FPS presentation while simulation timing stays identical. Peak validation covers two heroes, Ultimate's eight-enemy cap, convoy/cases, player/hostile bikes, repairs, warnings, breakables, subtitles/captions/screen-reader and Highlight eligibility. Reduced effects may simplify dust, exhaust, reflections, debris, background activity, shake/motion, shadows/secondary light and nonessential audio, but not enemy count, AI, hitboxes, lanes, warnings, objective timing, collision, score, authority or outcomes. Cosmetic layers reduce first; Highlight collection may stop gracefully under shared policy without losing result facts or rewards.

**Confirmed release gate — Manifest-to-Archive Acceptance Matrix:** Validation covers all four solo heroes and all six unique pairs; same-device/local-Wi-Fi; all five difficulties; Standard, Open Loadout, Assisted and Cheat-Assisted records; keyboard including remapped German layout, gamepads, supported browsers, 60/30 presentation, EN/ZH-CN/DE, reconnect/rollback/host recovery, package update and Legacy migration. Content coverage includes every legal ledger transition; branches skipped/abandoned/completed singly/together; eight encounters and specialists; tools/fragments/provisions; mounted/on-foot paths; all Luo phases; snapshots/restores; every grade/cap/reward; all Highlight families; Museum/replay/storage; Guide/Academy/accessibility/cheats; subtitles/audio/reduced effects.

Negative proof forbids permanent cargo loss, off-screen/cosmetic/network objective damage, duplicate case/carrier/recovery, seal/repair improvement, repair/route/tool/branch softlocks, hero abandonment, lethal crash/fall/civilian or clinic-staff harm, unavoidable objective overlap, restore improvement/reroll/respawn/reward duplication, score/Highlight farming, mandatory co-op/branch/purchase, Tomas/sponsor/evidence contamination, and language/input/frame/effect/Highlight simulation differences. Release requires zero unresolved Critical or High defects. Exact frame/hitbox/damage/AI/spawn/rank/Credit values, final geometry/art/animation/dialogue/voice/Flow frames/music/SFX, provenance/rights and final encounter balance remain explicit production-data gates rather than feature gaps.

**Curated Mission 2 status:** Feature model and complete product/content/technical specification approved on 2026-11-02. The specification at `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-02-fight-for-peace-the-medicine-road-specification.md` passed fresh builder review. Implementation planning remains separate.

#### Curated Mission 3 The Vanishing Survey

**Approved localized title:** **The Vanishing Survey / 消失的勘测资料 / Die verschwundene Vermessung**.

**Confirmed:** **The Vanishing Survey** occurs after Stage 6 and before Stage 7. An independent salvage ring steals portable landslide sensors, historical hazard maps, and a sealed route-survey tablet from a regional civil-works depot. The equipment protects remote communities from floods and landslides. The thieves create decoy cases and move the genuine items through an abandoned cableway station for resale. The theft contains no Tomas or sponsor evidence.

The primary objective is **Recover and Secure**. The heroes search a tram-maintenance yard, terraced salvage market, and abandoned cableway station. Authentic cases remain identifiable through shapes, seals, serial fragments, and briefing evidence rather than color alone. Inspecting a decoy costs time but never causes an unrecoverable failure. Genuine mission items cannot be destroyed; if a carrier leaves one area, the item moves to a later authored interception. Item condition and recovery efficiency affect rank, while every essential item is recovered canonically.

**Approved route architecture — Five-Link Evidence Chase:** The mission targets approximately **35–43 minutes** for a first clear and **24–31 minutes** for an experienced replay, with **eight mandatory encounters plus the boss**. **Regional Civil-Works Depot** provides briefing, unique-hero/loadout selection and a safe authentic-versus-decoy identification exercise. **Tram Maintenance Yard** introduces Decoy Carriers and recovers the first genuine sensor among trams, pits, shutters and racks. **Terraced Salvage Market** uses layered legal stalls and concealed routes while requiring respect for legitimate traders and property. **Cableway Service Incline** uses Hook Runners, guards, cable carts, cranes and suspended cargo; missed genuine items advance to the next authored interception rather than becoming Lost. **Abandoned Cableway Station** secures the remaining tablet/maps and ends with the reinforced-case fence-broker's readable cable-lift escape attempt and live capture. The civil engineer coordinates safely and is never playable; only the four established village heroes are selectable. The mission remains independent of Tomas, sponsors, the Compact and main-campaign evidence.

**Approved opening and five-item manifest:** Shortly after a pre-dawn depot theft, the protected civil engineer explains the independent resale motive and the equipment's landslide/flood-route/community-safety role, then remains a radio coordinator rather than combatant. The five genuine items are three individually identified compact Landslide Sensor cases, the long flat twin-seal Historical Hazard-Map Archive, and the slim recessed-handle diagonal-seal Route-Survey Tablet. Every identity uses at least three non-color properties among silhouette/dimensions, seal geometry, partial serial, handle/latch and controlled reference image. A safe untimed exercise compares one reference match, one decoy and one unrelated container before unique-hero/loadout/difficulty/accessibility/Highlight setup. Priorities are recover all five, preserve condition/custody, protect legitimate people/property and avoid wasting force on proven decoys. Controlled localized UI contains all readable manifest data; control begins by following loading-area tracks toward the Yard.

**Approved unrelated-container classification:** Unrelated containers are non-candidate scenery, not a third evidence class. A clearly incompatible silhouette, ordinary-storage fixture and localized accessibility description such as “not a survey case” redundantly distinguish them. They have no inspection, marking, carrier, custody, condition or debit state. The Depot comparison uses one only to teach that not every container is a candidate; the Yard instance remains noninteractive ordinary scenery and cannot create objective pressure, penalties or farming.

**Approved identification contract:** Every candidate progresses Unknown → Inspected → Marked Genuine/Decoy → Verified Genuine/Decoy. Contextual inspection is interruptible without penalty and records silhouette/dimensions, seal, serial fragment, handle/latch and manifest comparison into a shared Evidence Tray. Genuine items match every required property; decoys have at least two non-color inconsistencies. Tray reading, screen reader, magnification and language changes do not count active time. First inspection/marking is authoritative; repeat inspection adds nothing. Wrong marking triggers remote correction plus one fixed operational debit, never item loss; correct decoy identification has a smaller fixed debit independent of reading speed. Unknown cases cannot be secured or weaponized. Attacks/physics/blind interaction cannot reveal identity, verified decoys remain noninteractive evidence scenery, and restore/co-op cannot duplicate penalties or score. In co-op either hero may inspect/mark, both receive facts, and host-authoritative first valid input resolves simultaneous conflict without pausing the partner.

**Approved marking-resolution clarification:** Each candidate accepts exactly one authoritative mark. A correct mark immediately verifies that class. A wrong mark produces localized corrective feedback, automatically verifies the true class and adds one fixed major investigation-time debit. A correctly identified decoy adds the smaller fixed routine inspection-time debit. Both are deterministic additions to Resource and Active-Time Efficiency rather than clocks running during Evidence Tray reading; neither changes condition, custody, rank caps or completion. Co-op conflict, restore and repeat interaction cannot duplicate a mark or debit.

**Approved five-item custody and condition ledger:** Each genuine item independently records custody through Hostile Custody, Carried toward Exit, Dropped Candidate, Verified Genuine, Recovery Tagged and Council Custody. A visible carrier pickup channel precedes movement; interruption drops at a fixed safe marker. After verification, an interruptible contextual tag locks the item from retargeting/movement/damage/decoy confusion, and an authorized Council team performs the documented handoff only during the next safe transition. Heroes never inventory or weaponize cases. A carrier reaching an authored exit records Advanced to Next Interception plus one efficiency incident and reappears next time with identical identity/condition. Each item may advance at most twice; the final Station has no exit, and all five canonically enter Council Custody.

**Approved advancement-count clarification:** Only a hostile exit between major route links records one counted advance. Sensor A moving from *The False Consignment* to *Shutter Transfer* inside the Tram Maintenance Yard is a within-zone relocation and does not consume an advance. A counted transition is Carried toward Exit → Advance Recorded → Hostile Custody at Next Link. An item that arrives at the Incline already carrying two advances is routed to the Terminal Recovery Bay and cannot exit. An item arriving with zero or one advance may leave the Incline, increment once and remain at the Station even when that produces count two. No item can record a third advance. Within-zone relocation, cross-link advance count, next-link custody and Terminal Recovery Bay lock state restore independently and cannot be duplicated.

Condition is Intact, Scuffed or Damaged and never improves, becomes Destroyed or unusable. Only a warned completed Rough Transfer or Hook Runner drag across a tagged obstacle downgrades one step, at most twice. Ordinary attacks, guns, throws, drops, physics, carts, cranes and player mistakes are neutral; route escape affects efficiency, not condition. The fence-broker case has separate Guard, so boss attacks cannot harm the tablet. The manifest persists identity/evidence, custody/zone, condition, advance/history, downgrade cause, inspection/marking, tag ownership and handoff. Condition and efficiency grade performance, never canonical five-item recovery.

**Approved Tram Maintenance Yard encounters:** Mandatory **The False Consignment** presents Sensor A, two decoys and one unrelated container with a short pre-commit observation window, then Decoy Carriers, Brawlers and one guard teach pickup interruption, fixed drop, pressure inspection, decoy rejection and recovery tagging. One objective action approaches in solo and two staggered different-case actions in co-op. Sensor A may relocate to encounter two without consuming a cross-link advance. **Shutter Transfer** presents Sensor B, two decoys and any relocated Sensor A while Hook Runners operate deterministic tram-service shutters. Shutters may redirect but never trap, seal, crush or alter condition. Genuine exits advance unchanged to the Market with an incident; unverified decoys may leave without penalty. Once verified, a decoy locks in place as noninteractive evidence scenery and cannot be carried, retargeted or moved again. The closed evacuated Yard uses safe pits/returns, stationary trams, deterministic doors/racks/carts/barriers and property grading. The first Surveyor's Tripod appears in an authorized rack. These are mandatory encounters one and two, with no extra wave for an advanced item.

**Approved Terraced Salvage Market:** Three public groups—lower vendors, central customers/stallholders and upper workers/travelers—move Occupied→Evacuating→Cleared after heroes open/defend short authored routes. They have no Health/death/grab/shield/environment vulnerability. Only marked intimidation or route-blocking can cause one Disrupted Evacuation per group, after which Council staff still clear them; Cleared groups never return. Mandatory encounter three, **Clear the Terraces**, introduces Sensor C, two decoys and any advanced A/B while Brawlers/Carriers contest evacuation. Solo permits one case action or disruption, co-op two staggered separate targets; advanced items replace slots and wait at holding markers. Mandatory encounter four, **Archive at the Switchback**, introduces the Hazard-Map Archive, two matching decoys, Hook Runner pallet routes, guards, any unrecovered sensors and the first Salvage Pry Bar. Genuine exits advance unchanged to the Incline. Legitimate stalls, goods, public stairs/rails/shelters/signs and untagged carts are protected; tagged ring-owned salvage objects remain legal. Only avoidable player damage affects collateral, never enemy/required/accessibility action.

**Approved Cableway Service Incline encounters:** The Tablet was already moved to the final Station by fixed tracking-established action before hero arrival, with no player incident or condition change. Mandatory encounter five, **Freight on the Rise**, combines any Market-advanced genuine items, three cableway decoys, Carriers, Hook Runners and guards. Items below two advances may still move; capped items enter a Terminal Recovery Bay where warned handling/drag condition threats remain but no exit exists, followed by verification/tagging. Mandatory encounter six, **Counterweight Crossing**, uses the upper platform, cart-loading, tagged obstacle drags and guarded stairs. Solo permits one genuine threat, co-op two staggered different-item threats. An eligible item entering the outbound cart records its next advance and reaches the final Station unchanged without a wave; all others transfer safely after clear. Decoys fill unused slots without penalties. The public-closed Incline always has an accessible route, safe rails/returns and no falling/stuck/lost cases. If earlier items are all recovered, normal formations use decoys without artificial penalties.

**Approved cableway machinery contract:** Cable carts use fixed Parked→Loading→Departure Warning→Moving→Destination Lock states and cannot be steered or weaponized. They may carry candidate cases, including a genuine item, only through this declared state machine. A visible channel precedes enemy loading; interruption drops safely, while host-authoritative movement alone commits departure and an item's advance. Players cannot freely load, unload, steer or weaponize a cart. Optional marked-platform boarding has equivalent on-foot routes, shared-camera co-op and regroup before objectives. Cranes move only among declared previewed positions through tagged controls, with fair solo interaction and optional co-op role division; cranes cannot handle people, genuine items or indispensable structures. Tagged ring-owned suspended cargo may be stopped/redirected/lowered/released against eligible enemies under ground silhouette, directional sound, caption, non-color icon and optional vibration, but cannot harm civilians/custody/genuine cases, block sole routes, cause lethal falls or remain over required interactions. Counterweights are deterministic route/presentation elements with capped displacement, rails/catch platforms and reduced-motion equivalents. All machinery/item/collision states restore exactly, and prediction cannot commit departure, advance or release.

**Approved Abandoned Cableway Station finale:** Mandatory encounter seven, **The Sorting Hall**, contains all Incline-advanced genuine sensor/map cases, three station decoys, the Tablet tracking signal and the standard ring roster; the Tablet remains inside the broker's upper-office reinforced case. Carriers reaching the upper platform create a Station Transfer Incident, never another route advance or loss. Mandatory encounter eight, **Seal the Lift**, defeats the transfer team, resolves candidates, tags every sensor/map and secures controls. A genuine case reaching the cage becomes Secured in Lift Cage and automatically Recovered after Station Transfer at arena clear with one retained incident and no extra interaction/wave. Every item except the Tablet enters Council Custody before the boss. The broker then emerges with the genuine Tablet case, whose Guard is separate; it cannot be transferred/destroyed/substituted/off-screen. The stationary emergency lift cannot depart until the declared boss attempt, cause a lethal fall or trap. No adds join the boss. Live capture leads to controlled Tablet verification/tag/handoff.

**Approved salvage-ring roster and difficulty roles:** Core **Decoy Carrier** visibly channels one candidate pickup, cannot fight while carrying, drops safely and alone may perform tagged Rough Transfer without changing identity/history. **Salvage Brawler** is a punishable close fighter with no objective authority. **Hook Runner** uses warned short combat pulls plus visible tagged shutter/cart/crane/obstacle-drag controls, never off-screen or against civilians, custody, protected interactions or through walls. Finite-Guard **Equipment Guard** protects routes/carriers and can pressure inspection/tagging but always has flank/throw/Exact Block/impact/co-op counters. Extreme **Case Rigger** makes one long local interruptible quick-haul harness attempt per selected encounter, granting only one readable faster route and never changing evidence/condition/limits; ineligible secured/capped items cannot be targeted. Ultimate **Manifest Forger** makes one visible local counterfeit sleeve that obscures one field, adds one false match, labels authoritative data Obscured and leaves two independent decoy inconsistencies; it is removable and cannot touch verified/tagged cases. Rigger/Forger replace slots, cannot channel together on one case or simultaneously. Easy separates, Medium completes core, Hard coordinates, Extreme/Ultimate add selected specialists. The workwear/improvised faction has no firearms, grenades, military/sponsor/Compact gear; defeat ends in withdrawal/surrender/custody.

**Approved Manifest Forger evidence clarification:** A counterfeit sleeve may target only an authored eligible Unknown decoy that retains at least two other visible inconsistencies. Its overlay lifecycle is Unknown+Unsleeved → Unknown+Sleeved → Inspected+Sleeved. Inspection records the obscured field and false match exactly as presented. Interrupting or removing the sleeve updates that Evidence Tray field and reveals the original clue without counting as repeat inspection. Genuine, inspected, marked, verified, tagged and custody-secured cases are ineligible.

**Approved specialist-difficulty clarification:** Specialist progression is cumulative but never simultaneous at the objective level. Extreme introduces the Case Rigger in selected authored encounters. Ultimate retains the Rigger and additionally introduces the Manifest Forger. Both replace ordinary slots, never target the same case and never overlap objective channels; if both occur in one encounter, authored sequential opportunities include a fair neutral interval. Active caps do not increase.

**Approved formation and objective-pressure contract:** Active solo/co-op caps are Easy 3/5, Medium 4/6, Hard 5/7, Extreme 5/7 and Ultimate 6/8; reserves may position but not act. Authored False Escort, Hooked Route, Decoy Split, Rigged Transfer and Counterfeit Screen formations use specialists as replacements. Solo permits one total genuine pickup/exit, transfer/drag, evacuation disruption, route-machine action, harness or sleeve; co-op at most two staggered actions against separate reachable targets, with specialist actions inside the same cap. Warnings identify target/direction/consequence/countdown and stagger; visible entrants cannot act immediately, ambush inspectors or appear beside tagged items. Off-screen objective actions are forbidden. A co-op down extends the later channel. Inspection/tagging interruption requires visible/directional warning, preserves prior evidence, gives solo recovery space and cannot chain without punishable neutral. Difficulty tightens validated windows and coordination, never waves, hidden evidence, extra inconsistencies, simultaneous specialists or primary Health inflation.

**Approved Two-Tool Survey Recovery Arsenal:** The temporary **Surveyor's Tripod** is a medium-durability long control weapon with neutral three-part brace string, forward thrust, retreating brace, low sweep, committed downward aerial, grounded wake-up sweep and repeat-protected grounded hook/roll; it may optionally brace tagged lightweight shutters, a cable control or empty cart, with mandatory unarmed alternatives. It trades damage/recovery for reach/spacing. The temporary high-durability **Salvage Pry Bar** is a short high-Guard/environment tool with three-hit string, committed step strike, retreat hook, low interrupt, heavy downward aerial, grounded defensive roll and route-ending pry-lift; it may optionally open ring tool crates, barriers or pallet locks but never genuine/protected objects. Shared `T`/directional/aerial/ground/`J`, ownership, handoff, durability, disarm, affinity, checkpoint and anti-infinite rules apply. Neither tool affects civilians, custody, genuine cases/seals/tags or identity/condition. Medium baseline supplies two of each solo/three co-op; Easy may add one Tripod, higher difficulties retain an accessible first instance. They cannot be pre-equipped, stored or purchased.

**Approved environmental-impact and Property Care contract:** Yard, Market, Incline and Station use authored Intact→Damaged→Broken lightweight barriers, carts, cabinets, panels, racks, packing frames, crates, cargo, gates, partitions, furniture and secured housings. Tagged breakage may yield one safe panel/bar/lid/frame throwable, never glass, live cable/electronics, counterweights, records, batteries or safety equipment. Tagged impacts add damage/stun/bounce/knockdown/Resolve under collision cooldown, diminishing control, launch budgets and boss vulnerability; genuine item state is immune. The three ledgers are Civil-Works Equipment, Market/Public Property and Cableway Safety Property, graded Protected at zero avoidable player incidents, Disturbed at one/two, Damaged at three-plus; an authored cluster counts once. Enemy damage, enemy-thrown heroes, required/accessibility actions, ring-owned objects, scripts/boss transitions and protection actions are exempt, while deliberate player throws into protected property attribute once. Genuine/custody items, occupied civilian property, required rails/machinery/records/controls are immune and redundantly identified. Property affects collateral/results/archive only, never canon/recovery/survival. State/attribution restore exactly; reduced effects preserve simulation.

**Approved fence-broker boss:** **Ivo Serrin — The Fence Broker / 伊沃·塞林——销赃掮客 / Ivo Serrin – der Hehler**, age 42, is an independent former freight appraiser/auction intermediary turned obscured-goods reseller with no Tomas/sponsor/Compact link. **Reinforced Inventory** uses the genuine Tablet case as finite separate Case Guard for standing/low/aerial/throw/grounded pressure; the Tablet condition is immune, phase exit requires Guard break plus Resolve threshold, and the case moves to a protected cradle. **Appraiser's Counter** uses Pry Bar close counters, ground/aerial/throws and one warned exact-durability player-tool disarm/pickup that cannot delete or leave the arena. **The Last Cable** is one warned three-step gate/platform/cage sequence; each success records one Escape Pressure incident and position/cover advantage without resource reset. Full cage entry triggers a safety-brake return, never escape. No adds, lethal falls, hidden life bars or new difficulty phases exist; shared Defense/counter/breaker/anti-infinite/co-op rescue applies. Zero Resolve produces non-QTE controlled live custody, medical assessment and Tablet verification/tag/Council handoff.

**Approved Ivo transition clarification:** Appraiser's Counter ends when Ivo crosses an authored low-Resolve threshold after the minimum readable Phase 2 exposure. A protected transition begins the one-time Last Cable sequence. Interruption before cage entry causes a short punishable stagger; completed cage entry records the third Escape Pressure incident and the safety brake returns him. Either result enters **Final Appraisal**, a no-reset final-combat state using shortened Phase 2 Pry Bar or unarmed routes according to exact weapon ownership. Ivo cannot retry the lift, recover Case Guard, summon enemies or restore resources; zero Resolve produces the approved live capture.

**Approved optional branches and Museum:** **The Warning Bench / 预警工作台 / Die Warnwerkbank** follows the Yard and clears a bounded Brawler/Guard group, interrupts one Rigger harness and tags indestructible retired calibration plates/reference housing/notes. It adds calibration art, engineer history commentary and an atlas calibration layer, never gameplay advantage. **Routes in the Margin / 图册边注 / Randnotizen der Wege** precedes the Station sorting fight and opens a protected-shelf route, defeats a bounded Hook Runner/Guard group and secures three indestructible annotation folios through one custody interaction. It adds original annotations, community commentary and restored physical atlas presentation, never campaign evidence. Any clear unlocks the controlled localized digital **Regional Hazard Atlas**; each branch layers its material, and both create the complete **Regional Hazard Atlas Restoration** plus **Every Route Remembered / 每条道路皆被铭记 / Jeder Weg bleibt bewahrt**. Branches are destruct/loss/weapon/confusion-proof, time-compensated, replayable and unnecessary for S, five-item recovery, Ivo, base Museum or access. Cheat-Assisted reveals content/clues without branch competitive/economy/prestige rewards.

**Approved solo/co-op equivalence:** Solo uses one of four heroes and one active pressure event with sequential interactions, recovery space and travel-aware timers; co-op uses two unique heroes and at most two staggered different-target events, may divide inspection/defense/machinery within one map, and never adds items, encounters or duplicate completion. Every hero can perform every objective. Civilian, evidence, custody, condition, route, machinery and handoff are shared once-only authority; case credit is not personal. Co-op scales formation coverage/responsibility rather than doubled Health, keeps friendly collision/fire off, and retains shared combo/rescue/anti-infinite rules. Same-device camera and regroup prevent splits. Downed-partner revive extends only the later channel and preserves evidence. Ivo's grabs permit rescue and all boss resources/steps are shared. Scores normalize into separate solo/co-op categories with informational contributions; no content/rank/reward requires co-op. Local-Wi-Fi host owns authoritative cases, civilians, machinery, enemies, boss, score and rewards.

**Approved five-anchor Survey Snapshot:** **Depot Departure**, **Yard Cleared**, **Market Cleared**, **Incline Cleared** and **Station Sealed** commit the tutorial/configuration, Yard/branch-one, Market/civilians, Incline/machinery and all non-Tablet Station custody respectively; no mid-boss checkpoint exists and Ivo restarts Phase 1 from exact Station Sealed resources. Payload includes heroes/loadouts/resources/weapons/provisions; all genuine identity/evidence/custody/condition/advance/incidents/tag/handoff; every decoy/mark/sleeve; Evidence Tray/debits; civilians/property; branches/Museum; all machinery/environment; enemies/formations/channels; Ivo/Tablet/case/boss/lift; score/time/difficulty/player/integrity/Highlight/network. Restore never improves, rerolls, erases, replenishes, duplicates, reverses or reopens custody.

Validated technical recovery and one consumed-on-resume anchor suspension preserve eligibility. Defeat/voluntary Continue preserves completion/content but removes competitive/economy/achievement/Highlight-prestige rewards; restart is fresh. Disconnect standby plus validated reconnect/migration before continued play preserves co-op class. Confirmed solo continuation marks Party Changed, adjusts only future safe-boundary rules, withdraws absent inventory without conversion, and grants access/mandatory records but no competitive/economy/achievement/Highlight-prestige result. Anchor rejoin cannot restore eligibility. Highlight facts reconstruct locally and add no network authority.

**Approved 100-point Survey Recovery Grade:** Survey Item Integrity 25 awards each genuine item Intact 5/Scuffed 3/Damaged 1; Custody and Recovery Efficiency 20 evaluates once-only route/Station/handling/tag/handoff results; Civilian and Property Care 20 splits 12 for three groups and 8 for three property categories; Combat and Nonlethal Mastery 25 rewards varied legal hero/tool/environment/co-op/defense/live-capture play under repetition decay; Resource and Active-Time Efficiency 10 excludes pause, Evidence Tray/access output, language, cinematics/loading/readiness/recovery and compensated branch time. Valid clear requires all five Council-held, Ivo live, all groups Cleared and validated completion. Thresholds are C valid below 60, B 60, A 75, S 90. S permits either one resolved route/Station incident or one Scuffed item, not both. One Damaged caps A, two-plus or any Damaged property category caps B. Wrong marks debit time only. Each optional branch adds five leaderboard points after rank, never gates S. Separate compatible records apply; ineligible runs keep completion only.

**Approved achievements and rewards:** Eight eligible achievements are mission clear **The Vanishing Survey / 消失的勘测资料 / Die verschwundene Vermessung**; both localized branch titles; combined **Every Route Remembered**; five Intact **Every Case Intact / 每只箱体完好 / Jeder Koffer unversehrt**; no wrong classification **No False Trail / 不入假途 / Keine falsche Spur**; undisrupted civilians plus Protected Market **Market without Harm / 市集安然 / Markt ohne Schaden**; and no completed boss lift step plus live capture **The Last Lift Stopped / 最后一趟缆车止步 / Die letzte Seilbahn gestoppt**. None requires co-op/purchase/hero. Any valid clear grants Chronicle, base Atlas, media/content entries and shared replay/remix access; first eligible clear adds balance-defined Credits and records. First A gives a text-free Survey Triangle emblem, first S all four heroes' cosmetic-only **Survey Line Patrol / 勘测线巡护装 / Patrouillenfarbset der Vermessungslinie**, and first Ultimate **Keeper of the Safe Routes / 安全路线守望者 / Hüter der sicheren Wege**. Profile-once/bounded replay/difference-only anti-farming applies. Highlights grant nothing; ineligible clears receive base narrative/media/Museum only.

**Approved Survey Recovery Provision Set:** Health **Terrace Vegetable Bun / 梯田蔬菜包 / Terrassen-Gemüsebrötchen**, Defense **Mineral Barley Tea / 矿物大麦茶 / Mineral-Gerstentee**, Attack **Dried-Fruit Seed Bar / 果干籽仁棒 / Trockenfrucht-Kernriegel**, and one moderate-all **Survey Crew Lunchbox / 勘测队餐盒 / Lunchpaket des Vermessungsteams** use established localized descriptions. Medium solo places Bun+Tea at Yard, Bar+Bun at Market, Tea+Bar at Incline and Lunchbox after Seal the Lift; co-op adds one smaller common portion of each type, never another Lunchbox. Easy adds one each common; Hard/Extreme/Ultimate remove one/two/three authored common items; Lunchbox remains. Branches add no resource. Only safe authorized containers/handoffs provide food, never cases/evidence/protected stalls/objective zones. Shared `J`/ownership/handoff/consume/drop/throw/checkpoint is deterministic. `FULLPANTRY` affects ordinary containers only, never Lunchbox, branches, tools, genuine cases or rewards.

**Approved Automatic Highlight profile:** The Curated-only six families map as follows: Perfect Combo retains universal legal hero routing; Two Threats One Move requires one legal action against two active hostiles and excludes cases/protected targets/repeated contact; Guardian Intervention recognizes prevention of committed carrier exit, Rough Transfer, Hook drag, Rigger harness, Forger sleeve, evacuation disruption or Ivo lift step before state worsens; Defensive Mastery requires Exact Block/Counter against designated Guard/Runner/Rigger-defense/Ivo action into legal damage-free punish; Team Harmony requires one hero completing inspection/tag/evacuation/machinery/branch custody while the partner interrupts its threat and the state secures; Environmental Ingenuity uses approved Yard/Market/cableway/Station objects to interrupt an objective actor and affect another hostile without protected harm. Maximum three/one per family and 24 candidates apply. Delay, repeated warnings, item harm, wrong-mark farming, custody/helpless targets, harmless damage and boss extension are excluded; screenshots avoid distress/damage/down/custody. Localized reasons, attribution/ruleset labels, default-on opt-out, local replay reconstruction, no network traffic, privacy/export/delete/Museum pin and zero rewards follow shared policy.

**Approved Guide and Academy package:** Searchable localized **Survey Recovery Operations / 勘测设备追回行动 / Bergung von Vermessungsausrüstung** documents manifest/evidence/inspection/custody/condition/advances/tagging, civilians/property, cableway, tools, Ivo, score/branches/rewards, Highlights, cheats and access without revealing mission answers or embedding generated text. Optional **Survey Recovery Drill / 勘测追回训练 / Übung zur Vermessungsbergung** offers five spoiler-safe lessons at mission access—Read the Case, Stop the Carrier, Protect the Condition, Clear the Market, Work the Cableway—and post-clear Break the Broker's Escape. Synthetic cases differ from mission assets. Demo/guided/test/free practice, tool/unarmed alternatives, configurable solo partner, optional unique-hero co-op and My Combos tool routes use unchanged systems; resets never transfer. Academy grants no access/power/economy/score/rank/achievement and is not required for Highlights. EN/zh-Hans/DE narration/captions/transcripts/screen readers/remapped German keyboard/active gamepad are required.

**Approved cheat profile:** No mission code is added. `SOFTERBLOWS`/`SAFEHEART` affect ordinary Health, `STEADYFEET` eligible knockdowns not cableway returns, `IRONGUARD` hero Defense only, `BRIGHTSPIRIT` legal Attack, `STRONGHANDS` enemy Health/Ivo Resolve but not item/Case Guard/Guard durability/property/machinery/objectives/civilians, and `TRUSTYTOOLS` only carried Tripod/Pry Bar durability without preventing ownership actions/Ivo pickup. `QUIETCROWD` lowers ordinary pressure but retains selected Riggers/Forgers/routes/civilians/Ivo/discoveries; `FULLPANTRY` ordinary food only, excluding Lunchbox/branches/tools/cases/decoys/rewards/Museum; `HOMECOMING` restores exact state. `STEADYRIDE` is Not Applicable because carts are environment; `SAFEPASSAGE` is Not Applicable because mandatory targets already cannot completion-fail and it cannot preserve condition/efficiency/disruption/property/branches/lift. Presets mark only invalid components. Any code marks Cheat-Assisted, retaining base clear/content/encountered records/Museum but no competitive/economy/achievement/cosmetic/title/Highlight prestige result. Localized all-input cards and co-op consent apply.

**Approved presentation/localization/accessibility:** Skippable **Lines That Protect** uses 16–24 seconds of silent small-motion Flow/stills showing theft aftermath, manifest comparison and route trace, not complex theft. In-engine briefing covers purpose/manifest/evidence/condition/custody/safety/configuration. Safe-transition authoritative updates report Yard, Market, Incline and Station. In-engine Ivo/Tablet evidence contains no generated labels. **Every Line Restored** shows live custody, manifest, earned conditions/results, Council transfer, monitoring restart and branch-shaped Atlas without implying destruction/danger. Flow uses approved start/end frames, one focal action/axis, explicit gaze/hand/ownership, no speech/text/logos/watermarks, two-attempt simplification and still fallback.

English defaults with full zh-Hans/DE dialogue/narration/subtitles/speakers, manifest/Evidence Tray/serial-seal descriptions, objectives/results/Guide/Academy/Museum/transcripts/SFX captions/Highlight reasons; readable evidence is controlled UI, optional Windows narration is separate. Evidence uses text/shape/screen reader with color supplemental; reading/magnification/narration/language time is excluded. Warnings include target/direction/consequence/countdown; machinery uses silhouette/audio/caption/icon/vibration; object classes are redundant. Hold/toggle/reduced input, subtitle/audio controls, reduced motion/shake/flash/particles/sway/height/background/debris preserve timing/hitboxes. Remapped German keys/active gamepad apply. Every sequence is independently skippable with recap, and presentation/access output never affects active time.

**Approved audio suite — Lines That Must Remain / 必须留存的路线 / Linien, die bleiben müssen:** Cues are restrained depot **The Missing Measure**, uncertainty-rhythm Yard **False Consignment**, humane public-space Market **Market of Many Hands**, airy/mechanical Incline **Above the Old Cable**, three-layer Case Guard/counter/lift boss **The Broker's Last Lift**, and resolved civic ending **Every Line Restored**. Authored exploration/inspection/combat/item/civilian/critical/boss/recovery stems adapt without revealing authenticity. Carrier/handling/specialist/civilian/cart/cargo/lift/speech warnings duck music. Zone ambience covers depot/Yard tools/tram, Market commerce then evacuation reduction, Incline cable/wind/counterweight and Station resonance/lift. Inspection, Tray, post-mark verification/correction, tag/custody, handling/specialists/advance/Station incidents have distinct cues but no pre-inspection authenticity leak. Tools/roles/Case Guard/machinery/brake/lift use captioned directional families; reduced audio retains warnings. Generated candidates require human editing/loops/provenance/creation-date commercial verification and no lyrics/language vocals.

**Approved package, Archive and replay contract:** Stable `curated.vanishing_survey` is a canon-compatible regional side story after Stage 6/before Stage 7, independently gated by any canonical Stage 6 clear and never another Curated Mission; late install opens immediately when eligible. Package metadata declares package/combat/localization/evidence/scoring/Highlight/replay versions plus asset/rules hashes, and local-Wi-Fi mismatch blocks with localized correction. Competitive archive stores complete configuration, score/rank/bonus, five-item/decoy/evidence/civilian/property/branch/Museum, resources/machinery/environment/enemies/Ivo, checkpoint/network/integrity and Highlight facts; ineligible history is separate. Personal best requires compatible content/scoring/difficulty/player/heroes/loadout/integrity; older is Legacy.

Compact replay stores inputs, deterministic case/enemy/evidence/custody/machinery/objective/score/checkpoint/network/Highlight events and hashes, never video. Compatible replays simulate; incompatible show localized timeline/manifest/results/stored cards. Replay grants/changes/recovers nothing and creates no competitive Highlight. Uninstall preserves completion/rewards/archive/Museum/Highlight pins/exports/Guide/discovery while disabling play/replay until compatible reinstall. Profile export/import preserves all records/capsules/labels/integrity/Museum/rewards and clearly labels missing packages.

**Approved Vanishing Survey release acceptance matrix:** Coverage includes all four solo heroes/six pairs, five difficulties, Standard/Open/Assisted/Cheat-Assisted/Continued/Party Changed, remapped keyboard including German physical layout, gamepads, same-device/local-Wi-Fi and supported browsers at 60-FPS target/stable 30 minimum. It spans five links, eight encounters, branches, all Ivo outcomes, every reachable five-item/decoy/civilian/property/enemy/specialist/tool/provision/machinery/environment/score/reward/Museum/Chronicle/Highlight state; all five checkpoints/recovery/network/package/replay/Legacy/uninstall/reinstall/export/import paths; and full EN/zh-Hans/DE/media/accessibility/performance peak.

Negative proof forbids item destruction/loss/substitution/identity change/permanent wrong classification; single-channel evidence; access time scoring; more than two advances; escape-generated waves; farming; civilian death/followers; false property attribution; cableway lethal/blocked/softlocked states; missing/duplicated content/rewards/Highlights; Ivo escape/death/Tablet damage/phase skip/weapon loss; co-op gates; campaign-conspiracy contamination; Cheat rewards; Highlight simulation/network/reward/protected-image violation; persistence/package corruption; or settings/frame rate changing outcomes. Release requires zero unresolved Critical/High defects. Exact frames, values, economy, geometry, final assets and browser budgets remain production validation unless a fundamental conflict emerges.

**Vanishing Survey status:** Feature refinement and the full product/content/technical specification at `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-02-fight-for-peace-the-vanishing-survey-specification.md` were approved on 2026-11-02. Fresh builder review passed after ten approved consistency clarifications, and the final follow-up returned nothing important left. Implementation planning remains separate.

Cable carts, shutters, pallet racks, cranes, and suspended cargo use deterministic environmental profiles. Pickable **Surveyor's Tripod** and **Salvage Pry Bar** profiles use shared weapon rules. Decoy Carriers, Salvage Brawlers, Hook Runners, and equipment guards use existing enemy resources and legality. A fence-broker boss uses a reinforced case as a temporary shield and attempts to reach the cable lift through an authored, readable sequence.

Mountain Guardian, River Scout, Wind Sentinel, and Fire Warden are selectable. A civil engineer coordinates the operation but is not playable. The Museum discovery is the restored regional hazard atlas.

#### Curated Mission 4 No Escape at Reedwater

**Confirmed:** **No Escape at Reedwater / 芦水渡口：无处可逃 / Kein Entkommen in Schilfwasser** occurs approximately four weeks after Stage 3, following *Storm over Qinglan* but before Stage 4. An independent extortion group controls the **Reedwater Ferry District / 芦水渡 / Fährbezirk Schilfwasser**, imposes illegal tolls, and attacks travelers who refuse to pay. The Regional Civil Protection Council issues a lawful live-capture order for the commander. The incident contains no Tomas or sponsor evidence.

The primary objective is **Nonlethal Apprehension**. The heroes enter the ferry market, dismantle the commander's prepared escape network, and secure a motorbike, cargo-winch route, and reinforcement signal relay. Missing an interruption moves the commander to the next authored encounter rather than failing the mission. Key lieutenants are restrained, and the final commander is defeated through Resolve break, controlled incapacitation, restraint, and live custody.

The mission uses the Reedwater market, ferry warehouse, crane yard, loading platform, and ferry deck. Pickable **Mooring Hook** and **Short Restraint Baton** profiles use existing weapon rules. Toll Enforcers, Signal Runners, Cargo Controllers, and Bike Interceptors use shared combat schemas. The Tollmaster boss has a mounted opening, dismounted counter-fighting, readable escape transitions, and a final defensive phase. The player never controls a boat, and the mission adds no vehicle-simulation rule.

The mission introduces the optional 27-year-old heroine **Lin Lan / 林澜**, titled **Reed Warden / 芦水卫 / Wächterin von Schilfwasser**. **Lin / 林** is the family name and **Lan / 澜** is the given name, evoking broad or powerful ripples. She is originally from the village and now serves as a regional civil-protection liaison at Reedwater. Lin Lan uses circular footwork, redirection, short strikes, and Qinna-derived restraint through a complete solo-capable data-defined kit. She is available as a mission-specific guest and unlocks after completion for Academy, Curated Missions, completed Mission Remixes, and Random Operations. She never becomes required for main progression and is unavailable in canonical Story Mode and Chapter Challenge. Players without the mission lose no essential story, technique, or progression.

**Confirmed guest-playable model:** Lin Lan is immediately selectable inside *No Escape at Reedwater*, including the first attempt. Solo selects one of Mountain Guardian, River Scout, Wind Sentinel, Fire Warden, or Lin; co-op selects any two different heroes from those five, and duplicates remain prohibited. Every objective and rank is achievable by every eligible solo hero, so Lin is never compulsory. If she is not selected, she coordinates from the safe command post and appears in briefing, radio, and custody scenes. If selected, she operates in the field and a neutral Council dispatcher carries command-post messages so she is never depicted in two places. Completion, including Cheat-Assisted completion, grants her approved permanent noncompetitive content-access unlock; Story Mode and Chapter Challenge exclusions remain unchanged.

**Confirmed route structure:** Reedwater uses a **Five-Link Closing Net** targeting approximately 30-40 minutes for a first clear and 20-28 minutes for an experienced replay, with an initial baseline of about eight mandatory ordinary encounters plus the multi-part Tollmaster confrontation. **Ferry Market Entry** protects travelers, breaks the illegal toll line, introduces nonlethal restraint and escape markers, and forces the commander toward the warehouse. **Ferry Warehouse** captures a key lieutenant and targets the reinforcement relay; an incomplete interruption may add one bounded later reinforcement group but never fails the mission. **Crane Yard** targets the cargo-winch route, introduces the Mooring Hook and deterministic cargo interactions, and may leave the commander a bounded cover advantage. **Loading Platform** contains the mounted confrontation and motorbike interception; if the bike is not disabled, the commander reaches the ferry deck but must abandon it at a secured vehicle barrier. **Ferry Deck** uses a stationary ferry already lawfully secured at berth and ends with dismounted counter-fighting, a final defensive phase, Resolve break, restraint, medical assessment, and documented custody.

A visible **Escape-Network Ledger** tracks the relay, winch, and motorbike. Successful interruption disables the corresponding asset. A missed interruption reduces route-control grading and creates only its authored bounded later pressure; it cannot fail the mission, create an infinite chase, or prevent canonical live capture. Checkpoints follow the relay, winch, and motorbike resolutions and immediately precede the final deck phase. The player never controls the ferry or another boat.

**Confirmed One-Way Escape Budget:** Each asset starts **Active** and resolves exactly once as **Disabled** or **Used**; it never resets through checkpoint restore, continue, reconnect, or host migration. A Used relay creates exactly one non-replenishing reinforcement group at the next eligible encounter. A Used winch gives the commander one advantageous loading-platform position and one additional destructible cover piece without an unavoidable cargo attack. A Used motorbike creates one clearly warned mounted escape and a counterable ferry-deck dismount entry, never extra Health, damage, or permanent armour. Disabled assets improve route-control score and reduce later pressure without free boss damage or early capture. Consequences cannot combine into an unavoidable formation, and missing all three remains fair and completable. Difficulty changes interception pressure and window length without hiding warnings or changing consequences; solo and co-op use the same three assets.

**Confirmed Civic Safety and Custody Contract:** Reedwater tracks ferry-market vendors and travelers, warehouse/crane-yard workers, and ferry waiting-area passengers as public safety zones moving visibly from **Occupied** to **Evacuating** to **Cleared**. The heroes open and briefly defend a safe route; civilians then use short authored movement rather than becoming followers. Civilians have no Health bars, cannot die, and face no hidden timer. Only a marked intimidation or route-blocking action may create one bounded **Disrupted Evacuation** penalty, after which Council personnel still complete the evacuation. Cleared zones cannot be attacked or reopened, and the final deck is cleared before the boss.

The relay lieutenant, cargo-winch lieutenant, bike captain, and Tollmaster require live custody through Resolve break, controlled incapacitation, a visible restraint interaction, medical assessment where appropriate, and documentation. Solo receives a protected restraint window; co-op may divide restraint and defense. Key targets cannot die, vanish off-screen, fall into the river, or be struck during custody presentation. Ordinary enemies may surrender, withdraw, or enter group restraint without an individual interaction for every fighter. Safety rails, catch nets, and authored arena returns prevent drowning and lethal falls. Passenger shelters, ferry safety equipment, ordinary commerce cargo, and mooring controls are protected scenery; unnecessary damage reduces collateral grading, while required route actions, designated breakables, and unavoidable boss transitions never penalize the player.

**Confirmed optional branches:** **The Right to Passage / 通行之权 / Recht auf freie Passage** leaves the route after the Ferry Market and before the warehouse. The heroes recover confiscated travel permits, extortion receipts, and ordinary personal property from the toll office while protecting two voluntary witnesses during a short Council handoff. The records expose only the relay channel and runner route, never Tomas or sponsor evidence. Completion lengthens the signal-relay warning and grants a bounded civilian-protection bonus; the standard warning remains fair when skipped.

**Secure Every Line / 缆索全安 / Jede Leine sichern** leaves the route after the Crane Yard and before the Loading Platform. The heroes recover emergency mooring lines, restore opened safety cabinets, and defeat a bounded Cargo Controller group without damaging protected ferry equipment. Completion grants each active hero one ownership-aware Life, Defense, or Momentum recovery choice before the final confrontation and does not automatically disable an escape asset.

Either branch expands the **Reedwater Civic Passage Archive / 芦水渡通行档案 / Schilfwasser-Archiv der freien Passage** and has an achievement using its localized title. Completing both in one reward-eligible run grants **Reedwater Stands Free / 芦水无阻 / Schilfwasser bleibt frei**. Neither is required for S, Lin's unlock, live capture, base Museum completion, or fair interception. Cheat-Assisted completion may reveal branch Museum material but grants no branch mastery achievement.

**Confirmed enemy roster:** Core roles are **Toll Enforcer**, a close baton-and-shield fighter whose marked intimidation action alone may disrupt evacuation; **Signal Runner**, a fast evasive relay activator; **Cargo Controller**, a heavier hook-and-rope fighter operating tagged barriers and crane controls; and **Bike Interceptor**, an ordinary mid-class rider using warned loading-platform lane pressure and safely dismounting into a close fighter at zero Stability. Only Signal Runners and their lieutenant may resolve the relay as Used, and only Cargo Controllers and their lieutenant may resolve the winch as Used. Bike Interceptors and the authored bike captain control loading-platform support but cannot resolve Cassian's motorbike asset; Cassian alone resolves it during Mounted Toll Run.

Extreme adds **Ferry Feinter**, whose delayed baton attacks, depth steps, and counter bait cannot imitate civilian, custody, or escape warnings. Ultimate adds **Lockdown Captain**, which deploys one destructible route barrier and coordinates one authored two-lane formation inside the existing encounter budget without sealing the only route or summoning unlimited enemies. Solo permits one designated objective action at a time; co-op permits at most two with staggered warnings, never against one target or during protected custody. Cleared zones and resolved assets remain untargetable. Easy delays Cargo Controllers and confines bikes to their teaching area; Medium presents the full core; Hard strengthens combinations; Extreme and Ultimate add their specialists. Lockdown Captains never accompany the final Tollmaster deck phase.

The extortion faction uses mismatched ferry workwear, toll batons, hooks, ropes, ordinary motorbikes, and improvised barriers. It has no firearms, grenades, military armour, sponsor equipment, or campaign-faction marks. Defeated enemies surrender, withdraw, or enter custody without execution or lethal crashes.

**Confirmed weapon and mount contract:** Reedwater uses a **Two-Tool Custody Arsenal**. The **Mooring Hook** is a long control weapon with unique standing, aerial, and ground routes emphasizing spacing, pulls, sweeps, Resolve pressure, and tagged light-barrier, loose-line, or eligible-cargo interactions rather than high damage. It cannot pull civilians, restrained targets, the Tollmaster, motorbikes, the ferry, protected equipment, or indispensable structures. Fixed tools and ordinary interactions provide every mandatory alternative. The **Short Restraint Baton** is a compact weapon with unique standing, aerial, and ground routes emphasizing quick recovery, interruption, guard pressure, and Resolve damage while trading reach and raw Health damage. It never captures automatically, bypasses Resolve, or makes custody exclusive to its holder.

Both weapons use `T`/directional-`T` graphs and shared `J` pickup/throw-discard, durability, disarm, ownership, handoff, checkpoint, affinity, and anti-infinite rules. A held weapon must be set aside before the default restraint interaction unless the optional separate Interact binding is used. The Medium baseline is two Hooks and two Batons in solo; co-op adds one Baton without duplicating every weapon. Easy may add one Baton; higher difficulties may contest placement but retain an accessible instance of each class. They come only from authored racks or enemy drops and cannot be pre-equipped, stored, or purchased permanently.

Only the Tollmaster and Bike Interceptors ride ordinary mid-class motorbikes. Players may interrupt, damage, destabilize, and secure them as evidence but cannot ride or commandeer them. Zero Stability produces a safe dismount. The ferry remains stationary environment rather than a controllable boat. `TRUSTYTOOLS` applies only to carried weapon integrity, and `STEADYRIDE` displays **Not Applicable in This Mission**.

**Confirmed environmental contract:** Reedwater uses a **Deterministic Working-Waterfront Environment**. Ferry Market awnings, toll barriers, handcarts, and selected stalls create lane control; ordinary barriers may yield one safe improvised fragment, while vendor property is protected from unnecessary player damage. Ferry Warehouse shutters, pallet racks, rope bundles, and handcarts provide cover and redirection, but shutters cannot trap a hero or seal the only route. Crane Yard suspended cargo follows fixed seeded movement with ground silhouettes, priority audio, captions, and non-colour warnings; tagged cargo may be stopped, redirected, or released against enemies but cannot crush civilians, restrained targets, or indispensable equipment. The separately tracked winch remains the escape asset.

Loading Platform uses deterministic motorbike lanes, retractable safety barriers, cargo cover, and the Lockdown Captain's single destructible barricade; collisions are warned and capped, and no broken barrier opens a lethal fall. Ferry Deck bollards, mooring ropes, secured crates, cabin corners, rails, and catch nets create wall/environment conversions. Rail impacts become capped reactions or safe arena returns. Ferry sway and water movement are presentation only and never change hitboxes or departure state.

All environmental events use authored deterministic states and identical solo/co-op schedules. Damage remains inside shared scaling, stun, bounce, Resolve, and anti-infinite limits; bosses receive full reactions only in declared vulnerable states. Protected, expendable, rescue-sensitive, and required scenery use redundant identification. Reduced effects may lower sway, water, reflections, particles, and camera shake without changing timing. Enemy-caused and mandatory damage is never charged falsely to the player.

**Confirmed boss:** Reedwater's commander is **Cassian Rook - The Tollmaster / 卡西安·鲁克——渡口霸主 / Cassian Rook - der Fährzollherr**, age 43. A former ferry traffic and cargo-flow supervisor, he was dismissed for falsifying fees, confiscating cargo, and intimidating complainants, then built an independent extortion network while calling coercion "order and protection." His knowledge of schedules, cargo routes, winches, barriers, and motorbike access comes from legitimate former work. He has no Tomas, Garran, sponsor, or Meridian connection. His dark navy and weathered-rust ferry coat, forearm guard, gloves, boots, plain mounted helmet, and reinforced toll baton carry no military, Council, sponsor, or futuristic identity.

**Phase 1 - Mounted Toll Run** begins at the Loading Platform with warned bike lanes, braking sweeps, baton passes, and readable dismount actions. Bike Stability is separate from Cassian's Health, Defense, and Resolve. Reducing Stability to zero before the escape threshold resolves the asset as Disabled and causes a safe dismount plus punish window. If Cassian reaches the ferry barrier first, the asset resolves as Used and he performs the approved counterable deck entry. His combat resources carry between areas and never reset.

**Phase 2 - Deck Counter-Control** uses baton checks, guard pressure, body counters, low sweeps, clinches, trips, and short throws. Failed counters and grapples have clear recovery. Cassian may disarm a held weapon but cannot remove it permanently or throw it into the river. **Phase 3 - The Last Tollgate** begins at low Resolve near a portable fare barrier by the wheelhouse threshold. He uses braced defense, short counter strings, and controlled barrier repositioning, then makes one telegraphed emergency-gate attempt. Interruption staggers him; success narrows one lane and adds destructible cover but cannot trap a hero, seal the only path, or prevent capture. No final-phase reinforcements appear.

Cassian obeys shared Defense, Exact Block, Counter, defense-consuming Combo Breaker, combo scaling, anti-infinite, throw, environmental-vulnerability, and co-op pressure rules. Mounted actions target one readable lane, eligible grabs allow partner rescue, and he cannot lock both heroes into one unavoidable sequence. Defeat produces Resolve break, controlled incapacitation, restraint, medical assessment, and documented live custody.

**Confirmed encounter baseline:** Reedwater uses an **Eight-Encounter Closing Net** before Cassian. Market Toll Line introduces Enforcers and restraint without an objective action. Market Evacuation adds one Signal Runner and marked intimidation. Warehouse Approach combines Enforcers and Runners. Relay Control Room uses the relay lieutenant with limited support, resolves the relay, and completes custody. Crane Yard Entry combines Cargo Controllers and Enforcers while teaching cargo and Mooring Hook opportunities. Winch Control uses the cargo-winch lieutenant, Controllers, and one mobile support role, then resolves the winch and custody. Loading-Platform Approach teaches Bike Interceptor warnings with Enforcers. **Bike Captain and Platform Control** removes mounted support and completes the third custody while leaving Cassian's motorbike Active. Cassian's continuous three-part confrontation follows and resolves the motorbike during Mounted Toll Run, with no ordinary final-deck reinforcements. The two optional branch encounters remain outside this baseline.

**Approved motorbike-resolution clarification:** The checkpoint after Bike Captain custody stores the motorbike as Active. Cassian alone changes it to Disabled or Used during Mounted Toll Run. The following deck-transition checkpoint stores that outcome plus his exact Health, Defense, Resolve, resources, and transition state. Restoring the deck checkpoint cannot replay the mounted phase, improve the asset outcome, or restore Cassian. Achievement, grade, encounter-count, and canonical-capture rules remain unchanged.

Initial mandatory non-boss population targets, including the three lieutenants, are approximately 17 on Easy, 23 on Medium, 26 on Hard, 28 on Extreme with Ferry Feinter, and 31 on Ultimate with Feinter and Lockdown Captain. A Used relay adds only two ordinary Enforcers on Easy/Medium or three on Hard and above, with no specialist or objective action. Co-op adds approximately 35-40 percent ordinary pressure through complementary lanes without duplicating lieutenants, specialists, assets, hazards, custody targets, or Cassian support. No encounter activates before relevant civilians reach their protection boundary, and no spawn may interrupt protected custody. All counts remain playtest baselines.

**Confirmed recovery catalogue:** Reedwater's **Ferry Worker Provision Set** uses **Steamed Fish Rice Bowl / 清蒸鱼饭 / Gedämpfter Fisch mit Reis** for Life, **Citrus Barley Tea / 柑橘麦茶 / Gersten-Zitrus-Tee** for Defense, **Peanut-Sesame Crisp / 花生芝麻酥 / Erdnuss-Sesam-Krokant** for Momentum, and the balanced **Reedwater Work Lunchbox / 芦水渡工餐盒 / Schilfwasser-Arbeitermahlzeit**. Effects are gameplay abstractions and make no medical claims.

The Medium baseline is two of each common category and one lunchbox in solo, three of each common and one lunchbox in co-op. Easy adds one of each common; Hard removes one authored common, Extreme two, and Ultimate three. The lunchbox remains after Bike Captain custody and before Cassian on every difficulty. The Secure Every Line choice is separate. Supplies appear only at cleared zones, documented custody handoffs, and safe transitions, never during active evacuation or restraint. Shared `J`, ownership, handoff, consume, throw, and checkpoint rules apply. Objective property and safety equipment are never consumable. `FULLPANTRY` affects only eligible ordinary containers and cannot duplicate the lunchbox, branch choice, weapons, or objectives.

**Confirmed grading model:** Reedwater uses a 100-point **Apprehension-First Civic Duty Grade**: Live Apprehension and Escape-Network Control 45; Civilian Safety and Collateral Restraint 25; Combat and Restraint Mastery 20; Resource and Active-Time Efficiency 10. A valid clear requires Cassian and all three lieutenants in documented live custody. Candidate thresholds are C for an authenticated clear, B at 60, A at 75, and S at 90 with the eligibility rules below; final thresholds remain validated per difficulty and solo/co-op profile.

S requires at least two of three assets Disabled, at least two of three civilian zones cleared without disruption, and collateral within the validated S band, so one Used asset and one Disrupted Evacuation may coexist with S when the rest is excellent. Two or more Used assets cap rank at A; all three Used cap it at B. Two Disrupted Evacuations cap at A; all three cap at B. Major avoidable protected-infrastructure damage caps at B. Cassian's successful emergency-gate action reduces points without automatically barring S. Optional branches are not required and compensate their added active time.

Active time excludes pause, cinematics, loading, synchronized readiness, protected custody, and validated network recovery. Surrendered or restrained enemies, civilians, protected presentations, repeatable barriers, harmless destruction, and phase transitions cannot be farmed.
