Evidence-seal and chain-of-custody confirmations, civilian-danger alerts, motorbike Stability warnings, Exact Block/Counter/Combo Breaker feedback, co-op partner-rescue alerts, and Kael's readable grapple and phase cues always take priority through bounded music ducking. Every essential cue has visual and optional vibration equivalents, audio categories are independently adjustable, and music never controls gameplay. Generated Flow Music is candidate material requiring human loop editing, provenance, and commercial-use verification.

**Confirmed Stage 4 persistence contract — Five-Anchor Evidence Snapshot:** CP1 follows Transfer Yard security and convoy release; CP2 follows Soren's withdrawal and protection of the trade-road travelers; CP3 follows the mandatory waystation evacuation and Chen Yue's withdrawal while preserving annex-branch state; CP4 follows the motorbike pursuit while preserving recorder, vehicle, and forced-dismount state; CP5 follows the evidence-facility approach immediately before Kael. Mission start is the fallback before CP1, and canonical completion is written transactionally after live custody and evidence delivery.

Each snapshot preserves hero resources, lives, loadouts, weapons, provisions and ownership; evidence, archivist, civilian, branch and recorder states; motorbike Stability and pursuit facts; broken scenery and collateral attribution; restrained enemies; score, active time, grade caps, achievement and anti-farm facts; deterministic encounter state; and co-op players, unique heroes, reconnect and authority state. Restore cannot duplicate supplies, repair equipment or bikes, improve evidence, reroll encounters, erase collateral, reopen resolved branches, repeat rewards, or improve any objective state.

Validated crash, browser reload, disconnect, host migration, and compatible transactional suspension restore the exact snapshot without harming eligibility. One **Suspend Stage** record may be created only at an authored checkpoint and is consumed transactionally when resumed. Voluntary or defeat-based **Continue from Checkpoint** preserves canonical completion, mandatory records, and Stage 5 access but marks the attempt **Continued Run**, with no competitive score, rank, Credits, performance achievement, emblem, or title. A new eligible attempt begins from stage start; Chapter Challenge retains its already approved equivalent rule.

**Confirmed Stage 4 encounter baseline — Ten-Encounter Convoy Pressure Curve:** Stage 4 targets approximately 35–45 minutes for a first Story clear and 25–30 minutes for an experienced replay. Transfer Yard, Changling Trade Road, Damaged Waystation, Broken-Road Pursuit, and Evidence-Facility Approach each contain two mandatory ordinary encounters. Soren's and Chen Yue's Resolve-limited appearances and Kael's boss battle are additional authored confrontations; each optional branch may add one brief engagement without becoming necessary for completion or S rank.

Initial solo population targets are approximately 20 ordinary enemies on Easy, 28 on Medium, 32 on Hard with stronger mixed-role formations, 35 on Extreme including two Convoy Breakers, and 38 on Ultimate including Convoy Breakers and two Counter Grapplers. Co-op adds approximately 35–40 percent ordinary pressure through complementary lanes rather than duplicating confrontations, rivals, specialists, objectives, hazards, or Kael. Simultaneous ordinary threat is capped at four in solo and six in co-op; only one marked objective action may approach completion at once in solo and two in co-op.

No enemy may change evidence or civilian state off-screen, spawn inside a protected evacuation boundary, or begin an objective action without readable visual, audio, and caption warnings. Higher difficulty improves coordination, defense, counter use, formation composition, and recovery-window pressure without removing telegraphs, changing canonical facts, permitting unfair simultaneous grapples, or bypassing shared anti-infinite rules. Counts are authored playtest baselines and may be tuned without changing the route, encounter classes, or difficulty identities.

**Confirmed Stage 4 learning package — Protect the Chain / 守护证据链 / Schützt die Beweiskette:** The localized Guide card remains readable reference material from briefing, pause, and Help. It explains evidence, protected-actor and recorder states; optional branches; rival Resolve; marked objective warnings; motorbike Stability and safe dismounts; Kael's mandatory live capture; and the competitive consequences of checkpoint continuation, assistance, and cheats.

Academy provides three optional spoiler-safe pre-stage lessons: **Convoy Interruption** for reading and stopping marked objective actions; **Road Control** for steering, dodging, striking, and safe motorbike dismount recovery; and **Break the Hold** for telegraphed grapple escape and partner rescue, using an Academy-controlled training partner in solo. First Stage 4 clear unlocks the advanced **Kael Matchup Lab** for clinch recognition, takedown defense, ground recovery, Exact Block conversion, Defense-Bar Combo Breakers, and safe punish windows using the authentic data-defined matchup.

No lesson gates Story, achievements, S rank, hero power, or technique ownership. Briefing links may recommend but never force training. Lessons support EN, zh-Hans, and DE; current remapped keyboard bindings; gamepads; captions; non-colour icons; and replayable demonstrations. Guide remains explanatory and Academy remains learn-by-doing.

**Confirmed Stage 4 cheat profile:** No new stage-specific code is added. All hero-scoped codes retain their shared behavior. `TRUSTYTOOLS` protects Folding Road Baton and Tire Iron integrity but not throws, drops, disarms, handoffs, or authored stow/removal boundaries. `STEADYRIDE` prevents player-motorbike Stability loss while preserving steering, obstacles, reactions, route limits, required inputs, and authored transition dismounts. `QUIETCROWD` lowers commitment and objective concurrency without removing selected-difficulty enemy variants, Soren, Chen Yue, or Kael. `FULLPANTRY` affects eligible ordinary recovery containers only and cannot duplicate the Convoy Lunchbox, branch rewards, weapons, evidence, or recorder. `HOMECOMING` restores the latest exact Five-Anchor snapshot without improving or rerolling it.

`SAFEPASSAGE` displays **Not Applicable in This Stage** because sealed mandatory evidence and protected actors already cannot cross into permanent destruction, death, or a completion-blocking failed state; it never preserves Secure status or cancels grading consequences. Any active code marks the full run Cheat-Assisted. Such a clear retains Stage 5, higher-difficulty access, canonical presentation, mandatory Chronicle/Museum/bestiary entries, and relevant Store visibility, but grants no competitive score, rank, Credits, performance achievement, emblem, title, or record.

**Confirmed Stage 4 accessibility — Evidence-Road Accessibility Profile:** Standard-eligible accommodations present every evidence, civilian, recorder, motorbike, and objective state through redundant symbols, shape, localized text, sound, captions, and optional screen-reader announcements. Marked attackers receive visible target lines and directional indicators. Players may independently reduce shake, pursuit-camera motion, flashes, particles, reflections, debris, and vibration without changing timing; configure steering sensitivity, dead zone, lane indicators, and camera centering; use hold/toggle alternatives for acceleration, braking, evidence interaction, restraint, and grapple escape; and see current remapped keyboard or gamepad inputs. Co-op danger and rescue indicators never depend on colour or audio alone.

Three explicit simulation-changing aids create an **Assisted** result: **Extended Protection Window** lengthens marked evidence/civilian interruption windows; **Guided Road Control** strengthens lane correction and slightly reduces pursuit speed; and **Extended Grapple Response** lengthens escape and partner-rescue windows against Restraint Controllers and Kael. Assisted play retains Story, checkpoints, ordinary Credits, purchases, Hero Mastery, Museum progress, and its own separate grade/record category, while Standard/Open competitive records and achievements explicitly testing original riding or response timing remain unassisted. Both co-op players approve shared aids before readiness; presentation and device settings remain per-player. No objective requires hearing, colour recognition, rapid repeated tapping, pointer input, camera motion, or vibration.

**Confirmed Stage 4 Stage Service outfits — Road and Convoy Guardian family:** Mountain Guardian receives **Stoneway Escort / 石道护运装 / Steinweg-Geleitschutz**, a charcoal and weathered-olive road jacket with reinforced shoulder mantle, divided riding hem, heavy bracers, fitted utility trousers, knee protection, sturdy brown convoy boots, and muted ochre stitching. River Scout receives **Currentline Courier / 流线信使装 / Strömungskurier-Outfit**, a short deep-teal asymmetric courier jacket, flexible blue-grey riding trousers, slim guards, compact document satchel, lightweight road boots, and silver water-line seams without loose cloth.

Wind Sentinel receives **Crosswind Interceptor / 侧风截卫装 / Seitenwind-Abfangschutz**, a fitted slate-blue road jacket with diagonal storm flap, pale-grey impact panels, narrow cyan reflective seams, streamlined guards, tapered trousers, high-grip riding boots, and a tightly secured braid. Fire Warden receives **Ember Convoy Guard / 烬火护运装 / Glut-Geleitschutz**, a deep-red wrap-front convoy jacket over compact charcoal rib protection, asymmetric bronze-rimmed forearm guards, reinforced grappling gloves, articulated trousers, thick-soled road boots, and restrained amber safety tabs.

All four are meaningful visual redesigns rather than palette swaps, carry only the text-free civil-protection emblem, preserve each hero's silhouette, animation anchors, and combat readability, and grant no statistics or mechanics. After catalogue reveal, each is purchased individually with earned Credits and may be equipped with any legal combat loadout.

**Confirmed Stage 4 Village Heritage outfits — Traveler's Homecoming family:** Mountain Guardian receives **Homeward Stone / 归途磐石装 / Stein der Heimkehr**, using a midnight-indigo structured jacket, stone-grey woven side panels, antique-copper fasteners, narrow cream inner collar, tailored dark trousers, polished protective boots, and restrained text-free bridge-and-road patterns. River Scout receives **Returning Current / 归流礼装 / Heimkehrende Strömung**, using a pale-jade and ink-blue short layered jacket, pearl lining, fitted divided side panels, silver river-path embroidery, slim formal trousers, decorative wrist guards, and flexible dark-blue boots.

Wind Sentinel receives **Clear-Wind Homecoming / 晴风归乡装 / Heimkehr im klaren Wind**, using a fitted cloud-grey and muted-violet diagonal jacket, narrow silver-cyan braided seams, secured side pleats, tapered trousers, refined lightweight guards, slate boots, and a silver braid clasp. Fire Warden receives **Hearthfire Return / 暖炉归乡装 / Heimkehr zum Herdfeuer**, using a warm-ivory tailored jacket, brick-red shoulder yoke, dark-umber fitted trousers, fixed copper-red waist detailing, refined grappling gloves, bronze-trimmed guards, polished brown protective boots, and small geometric hearth patterns without literal flame imagery.

The family represents the modern village welcoming travelers home after the convoy crisis. These are contemporary heritage designs rather than historical costumes, military uniforms, or palette variants. They remain visual-only, preserve combat silhouettes and animation anchors, are purchased individually with Credits after catalogue reveal, and support every legal loadout.

**Confirmed Kael default visual direction — Independent Road Fighter:** Kael is approximately 24–25 with a compact athletic pressure-boxing and wrestling build: broader through the shoulders and back than Fire Warden but mobile rather than bulky. His disciplined, guarded expression retains visible concern for his companions. He wears a short fitted dark-indigo road jacket with charcoal shoulder/rib stretch panels, muted-rust inner collar, pale-sand seams, concealed low-profile rib padding, close readable-finger grappling gloves, reinforced charcoal articulated-knee trousers, and flexible stable-soled road-wrestling boots. A small text-free three-path knot is a personal training token, never a village, military, sponsor, Compact, or already-established Wayfarer Watch insignia.

Four conditions use one consistent model: **Traveler Protector** is clean and calmly ready in the opening; **Convoy Interceptor** secures the jacket and gloves for combat; **Burdened Resolve** adds controlled scuffing, loosened collar, and fatigue without glow, rage transformation, or armour; and **Live Custody** removes the gloves, presents non-graphic treatment, and shows safe restraints. The design communicates a capable, misled, responsible opponent rather than an inherently cruel villain and forms the stable base for later playable use without implying that he has joined the village.

**Confirmed Kael delayed Stage 4 Store alternatives:** **Wayfarer Roadguard / 行旅守望装 / Wegwächter-Outfit** is a weathered blue-grey road-service jacket with forest-green flexible panels, burnt-copper fasteners, stronger forearm protection, reinforced grappling gloves, durable route trousers, all-weather boots, and three converging text-free seam lines. **Chosen Road Attire / 自择之路礼装 / Gewand des selbst gewählten Weges** is a tailored deep-forest personal-heritage jacket with warm-stone panels, pale-ivory inner collar, restrained copper edging, fitted dark trousers, formalized wrist protection, polished flexible boots, and three separated paths joining through its seam pattern.

The first expresses independent traveler protection; the second expresses Kael's voluntary choice of what the Rell name will mean without declaring the village his home or preselecting his relationship with Xu Lian. Kael's default is granted with his approved free-mode unlock. Both alternatives remain hidden until that same Wayfarer Watch milestone, then become individually Credit-purchasable, visual-only, compatible with every legal Kael loadout, and free of village, military, sponsor, or Compact disclosure.

**Confirmed Soren Beck and Chen Yue visual direction — Independent Training Companions:** Soren has a tall, lean, long-legged defensive-savate build and wears a short ash-blue road jacket with flexible cream side panels, muted-mustard seams, tapered charcoal trousers, compact shin/ankle protection, light gloves, and reinforced savate-style road boots. His fitted hip and knee silhouette keeps precision kicks readable; his expression remains observant and skeptical rather than aggressive.

Chen has a compact balanced judo and emergency-response build and wears a muted-plum wrap-front jacket with smoke-grey flexible panels, restrained sage accents, low-profile shoulder/forearm protection, fitted trousers, rounded knee guards, grappling gloves, soft-soled protective boots, a practical low bun, and a secured ordinary first-response pouch without a real-world medical emblem. Both carry the same text-free three-path training token as Kael in different positions, expressing a personal bond rather than an organization.

Each uses **Traveler Protector**, **Misdirected Interceptor**, and lightly scuffed **Civilian Withdrawal** conditions. In the last, Soren guards the evacuation route while Chen treats civilians. Neither receives boss armour, villain markings, sponsor equipment, supernatural effects, or a custody state. These are their stable later-playable bases without disclosing the Compact or prematurely formalizing the Wayfarer Watch.

**Confirmed Stage 4 result evaluation — Chain-of-Custody Result Ledger:** The result surface has five localized sections. **Evidence Integrity** reports sealed-original state, every breach/recovery, recorder state, evidence points, and caps. **People Protected** reports archivist, mandatory civilians, annex, disruptions, protected property, and solo/co-op attribution. **Combat and Restraint** reports hostile outcomes, defensive conversions, partner rescues, canonical rival withdrawals, and Kael's genuine defeat, treatment, and live custody. **Road Performance** reports Stability, forced dismount, pursuit and recorder timing, weapon use, resources, and active time. **Recognition and Records** itemizes four grade components, final grade, branches, achievements, Credits, Museum/catalogue changes, configuration, eligibility, recovery, and compatible-category personal-best comparison.

Cheat-Assisted and Continued Runs receive the complete narrative ledger without competitive score or rewards; Assisted results remain separately comparable. Stage 4 does **not** expand the Curated-Mission-only Automatic Highlight system. It may store lightweight replay bookmarks for authored events such as evidence recovery, civilian rescue, pursuit completion, and Kael custody, but performs no real-time highlight analysis or automatic screenshot capture.

**Confirmed Stage 4 release validation — Evidence-to-Archive Acceptance Matrix:** Coverage spans all four solo heroes and all six unique co-op pairs; Easy through Ultimate; Standard Loadout, Open Loadout, Assisted, Cheat-Assisted, and Continued classifications; keyboard, supported gamepads, same-device and local-Wi-Fi co-op; supported browsers at 60-FPS target and stable 30-FPS minimum without simulation differences; and every valid evidence, protected-actor, recorder, branch, motorbike, weapon, environment, resource, checkpoint, collateral, enemy, specialist, rival, Kael, grade, reward, ledger, bookmark, recovery, save/version, localization, input, Guide, Academy, Store, cinematic, transcript, music, SFX, and accessibility path.

Release requires explicit negative proof against permanent mandatory-evidence loss, civilian death, hidden objective change, route/branch/vehicle/custody/checkpoint/co-op softlocks, infinite combos, unavoidable simultaneous grapples, unreadable Kael actions, lethal crashes, protected-target weapon/environment damage, restore improvement, rerolls, duplication, repeated rewards, score farming, duplicate heroes, incorrect co-op attribution, premature sponsor/Compact disclosure, scripted voluntary Kael defeat, Story-mode Automatic Highlight activation, and language/frame-rate/quality/input-dependent simulation. Zero unresolved Critical or High-severity defects are permitted. Exact frame data, final tuning, artwork, media, and performance budgets remain production validation rather than feature gaps.

**Confirmed Stage 4 Deterministic Objective Contract:** The sealed originals are one aggregate protected evidence case containing multiple authenticated packets; breach events are logged individually while the case owns one Secure/Disturbed/Recovered state. Protected units are tracked separately as the archivist, trade-road travelers, main waystation group, and optional annex group. Each unit may suffer at most one disruption per run, and only one disruption may be active globally. Exactly two predetermined evidence-breach opportunities exist, each as an embedded phase of an existing encounter; no breach inserts another encounter, and a second recovered breach caps rank at A.

Soren's and Chen Yue's departures use a dual gate: the player first reaches each rival's protected Resolve threshold, after which the relevant civilian-danger event becomes active and produces the voluntary canonical withdrawal. Their Resolve cannot fall below the exit floor. In co-op pursuit, a forced-dismounted hero continues on foot in the same bounded arena while the other remains mounted under camera-tethered speed limits; both regroup on foot at the next secure anchor without forcing the successful rider to dismount.

Assisted completion may earn the clear, branch, combined-branch, ordinary first-clear Credits, separate grade, emblem, and title. First-clear Credits pay only once across Standard, Open, and Assisted categories. **Extended Protection Window** disables Unbroken Chain and Safe Testimony; **Guided Road Control** disables Broken Road, Unbroken Ride; and **Extended Grapple Response** disables Together on the Road. Other achievements remain available when their own conditions are met. Continued Runs retain progression, mandatory records, and catalogue visibility but no achievement, Credits, grade, emblem, title, or competitive record.

**Stage 4 documentation status:** Refinement is complete. `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-02-fight-for-peace-stage-4-specification.md` passed fresh builder review, received user approval on 2026-11-02, and is the controlling Stage 4 content specification. Exact production values remain governed by its validation gates; implementation planning has not begun.

#### Stage 5 detailed refinement

**Confirmed Stage 5 framing — Evidence-Anchored Historical Reconstruction:** *The Bell Before Dawn / 黎明前的钟声 / Die Glocke vor der Morgendämmerung* begins in the present at the regional evidence facility immediately after Stage 4. Sheng Guo, Kael, and the neutral archivist compare contradictory authenticated records. Illustrated documents, Sheng Guo's firsthand memory, and corroborating witness accounts transition into a dramatized playable past; the framing is neither time travel, virtual reality, nor supernatural memory. Confirmed facts are canonical, while connective staging may not invent unsupported guilt, motive, or sponsor knowledge.

The canonical first Story completion permits only 26-year-old **Sheng Guo — Civic Apprentice** and 31-year-old **Liang Zhen — Field Protector**. Solo chooses one primary hero while the other operates through authored parallel support and handoffs rather than continuous companion AI; co-op assigns one unique hero to each player. Modern heroes are unavailable in the first canonical reconstruction. Performance may affect grade, optional protection, property, Museum records, and bounded inserts but cannot alter Oren's betrayal, Rovan's assault, Qiao Lan's non-graphic sacrifice, the family-wide exile, or Sheng Guo's regret. The chapter returns to the present and compares played facts with the later ruling before unlocking its canonical Character Chronicle and non-canonical Mission Remix.

**Confirmed Stage 5 route — Five-Act Warning Bell Defense:** **Ledger Before Dawn** establishes Sheng Guo's comparison of purchase order, reserve ledger, merchant seal and wagon weight, Su Yan's duplicate receipts, and the first attack when discovery becomes imminent. **False Convoy at the Old Trade Entrance** exposes concealed weapons while Qiao Lan detects secondary-gate sabotage, sounds the manual warning, and holds the mechanism; her sacrifice is conveyed non-graphically through reaction and aftermath. **Communal Reserve Defense** protects evacuees and essential stores while Oren issues misleading civilian instructions as a noncombatant whose guilt remains suggested rather than omnisciently declared.

**Broken Wagon at the Storehouse Platforms** presents the sabotaged wagon: Liang Zhen redirects it long enough for villagers to escape, suffers the canonical permanent left-leg injury, and is rescued when Sheng Guo intercepts Rovan. **The Road-Cutter at the Warning Gate** stops Rovan's withdrawal with selected records and reserve goods, requires a complete boss victory, and ends in live capture. Brann Holt, concealed weapons, recovered goods and route records complete the evidence chain. A noncombat historical aftermath covers the month-long investigation, mourning, Sheng Guo's individual-judgment proposal, Zhao Heng's rejection, and collective exile before returning to the present.

**Confirmed Stage 5 objective state — Four-Ledger Historical Defense Model:** The **People Ledger** separately tracks trade-entrance workers, reserve staff and storehouse evacuees through Secure, Strained and Cleared; only warned objective actions or hazards may cause Strained, and no civilian may die. Qiao Lan is excluded because her fixed sacrifice is not a target the player failed to save. The aggregate **Emergency Reserve Ledger** uses Protected, Contested and Secured across grain, medicine, seed, tools and trade strongbox; one canonical wagon loss is excluded from grading, while additional avoidable loss affects recognition without contradicting survival.

The **Warning and Perimeter Ledger** commits Warning Raised through Qiao's action while player performance determines Contained or Strained remaining perimeter; the mechanism stays canonically damaged. The **Historical Evidence Ledger** commits four mandatory Identified → Authenticated → Committed anchors: Su Yan's receipts, concealed convoy weapons, Brann Holt's testimony, and route/depot records. None may be missed or destroyed, and Oren's guilt is formally established only after comparison following Rovan's capture. Liang's injury commits only after storehouse evacuees reach safety; players cannot prevent, cause or worsen it, and it is neither hero defeat nor objective failure.

**Confirmed Stage 5 control transition — Three-Phase Reciprocal Rescue Handoff:** Rovan's encounter begins with **Storehouse Interception**, allowing Liang and Sheng to confront him before the wagon crisis. **The Broken Wagon** integrates evacuation, Liang's successful redirection and injury, Sheng's interception and rescue, and Rovan's withdrawal. **Measured Hand Stand** is the final Warning-Gate phase with Sheng as the only mobile fighter and represents approximately the last third of the encounter.

Solo Sheng remains under player control; solo Liang transfers seamlessly to Sheng using a deterministic canonical resource state. Liang's eligible held items enter a single-use support cache without duplication. In co-op, the Liang player enters bounded **Field Protector Support** and may once warn one marked lane, once brace the damaged gate, once redirect one rolling-debris hazard and once transfer one carried provision. These actions cannot damage Rovan, replace Sheng's victory or become mandatory; the final phase remains solo-complete. The successful rescue, injury, support state, cache and boss transition are checkpoint-authoritative.

**Confirmed solo handoff resolution — Deterministic Ratio Handoff Contract:** Before the injury transition, a non-selected solo hero is an authored support actor only and owns no hidden Health, Defense, Attack Bar, inventory, collision, damage or checkpoint state. In a solo Liang route, Sheng receives Liang's exact remaining Health, Defense and Attack Bar percentages applied to Sheng's corresponding maxima, with no percentage increase or refill. The protected transition ends combo, hitstun, cancel and temporary-status state for both solo routes, while score, damage history, eligibility, ledgers and boss state continue unchanged.

Liang's one-use support cache contains only his equipped stage weapon at exact durability, if any, and one carried provision at exact type, if any. Dropped items remain in the world; objective objects and Rovan evidence never enter it. The cache may be claimed once after handoff, provides no substitute when empty, and stores exact contents plus claimed/unclaimed state in checkpoints. Co-op performs no conversion: Sheng and Liang keep their real resources until Liang enters support, and each of Liang's four support actions remains usable once.

**Confirmed Stage 5 historical-hero combat scope — Complete Historical Hero Kit Contract:** Young Sheng Guo and Liang Zhen each receive fifteen character-owned normal actions: seven standing, three aerial, three ground-route, one dash and one distinct low attack. Throws, Guard, Exact Block, Counter, Combo Breaker, weapon actions, three initial Specials on `U`/`Forward + U`/`Backward + U`, and one fixed `H` Ultimate are separate. `Up + U`, `Down + U` and directional Ultimate slots remain available for later independently authored techniques without changing the permanent engine.

Measured Hand emphasizes precise interruption, disarming, redirection, flank control, defensive conversion, safe restraint and protection with lower raw damage. Stone-and-Current balances stronger structure, leverage, clinch control, safe takedowns, angled pursuit, momentum redirection and front-line crowd control. Both are solo-complete before the injury transition; Liang's injury never weakens his reusable historical character data. First Story completion uses fixed historical loadouts without purchased moves or costumes. Canonical Replay defaults to those loadouts, while unlocked Mission Remix and eligible Academy use legal configurable loadouts without rewriting historical canon.

**Confirmed Sheng Guo normal-action catalogue — Measured Hand:** His seven standing actions are **Listening Palm**, **Balanced Elbow**, and separating **Peaceful Release** as the neutral chain; advancing **Intercession Step**; retreating **Reserved Reply**; depth-up redirection **North Court Turn**; and depth-down cross-through **South Court Pass**. His three aerial actions are quick **Rafter Check**, committed descending **Descending Measure**, and depth-correcting **Courtyard Turn**.

His three ground-route actions are close-keeping **Settling Hand**, route-closing non-submission **Quiet Restraint**, and relaunch-budget-limited **Measured Rise**. **Mediator's Entry** is his modest-damage dash intercept with conditional side switch. **Threshold Reap** is his dash-state `T + G` or bound Low Attack sweep, gaining stagger/trip only under shared counter-hit and anti-loop rules. The catalogue has no armoured rush and favors objective protection, positional redirection, safe separation and counter conversion; final timing, damage, cancel and localization values remain Combat Lab data.

**Confirmed Sheng Guo initial Special and Ultimate set:** **Civic Arc** (`U`) is a short broad rising open-hand sequence for close ground/aerial coverage and rescue space, with Resolve-weighted output and no projectile or automatic invulnerability. **Intervening Passage** (`Forward + U`) advances along an angled lane, redirects and carries one target away from protected space, and has committed punishable recovery without armour. **Measured Reversal** (`Backward + U`) is a brief eligible-strike counter that turns the attacker, opens a route and may disarm a held weapon; grabs, True Unblockables and failed timing defeat it.

**Dawn Bell Accord** (`H`) is his fixed full-meter Ultimate: precise interceptions against one primary target, optional legal disarm, and a broad non-supernatural separation arc creating rescue or co-op continuation space. It favors high Resolve and moderate Health damage rather than raw-damage supremacy. Dust, cloth motion, controlled impact lines and restrained bell resonance provide presentation without magical energy. All four moves retain shared scaling, cancel, boss resistance, anti-infinite and protected-transition rules.

**Confirmed Liang Zhen normal-action catalogue — Stone-and-Current Method:** His seven standing actions are **Foundation Palm**, **Turning Forearm**, and guard/Resolve-focused **Stonecourse Press** as the neutral chain; deliberate advancing **Advancing Bank**; retreating momentum conversion **Receding Current**; depth-up launcher **Skyward Leverage**; and depth-down side-switching **Low Channel Turn**. His aerial actions are compact **Eave Guard**, committed descending-knee **Falling Buttress**, and crowd-repositioning depth arc **Crossing Current**.

His ground route uses close/stable **Settling Weight**, controlled takedown-and-release **Anchor Fold**, and relaunch-budget-limited side-switching **Returning Surge**. **Bridge-Line Shoulder** is a committed push and guard-pressure dash action without invulnerability. **Riverbed Reap** is a broader, slower dash-state `T + G` or bound Low Attack sweep with stronger conditional trip pressure and longer recovery. Liang has more direct damage and guard pressure than Sheng while remaining a balanced source tradition; the moves do not duplicate either later student's animation, naming, timing or route.

**Confirmed Liang Zhen initial Special and Ultimate set:** **Confluence Guard** (`U`) is a rooted circular forearm-and-palm sequence ending in a rising check for close ground/aerial coverage, recentering, and guard/Resolve pressure; legal defensive-cancel routes do not grant automatic armour or invulnerability. **Channel-Break Advance** (`Forward + U`) is a committed stance drive converting momentum into a controlled trip or separating push, with lane carry and punishable failure. **Returning Anchor** (`Backward + U`) retreats briefly then catches an eligible grounded advance into a directional safe throw; aerials, projectiles, True Unblockables, late timing and resistant boss states defeat it.

**Stone Guides the Current** (`H`) is his fixed full-meter Ultimate: brace against one primary target, redirect through a legal depth lane, controlled takedown, and broad separating finish. It supplies balanced Health, high guard/Resolve pressure and rescue space without automatic crowd defeat. Grounded footwork, dust, cloth tension and sweeping motion lines communicate Stone and Current without literal elements, magic or supernatural armour; all shared scaling, resistance, anti-infinite, co-op, boss and boundary rules apply.

**Confirmed Stage 5 enemy family — False-Convoy Raider Roster:** **False Merchants** discard civilian trade layers permanently when combat begins and use concealed cudgels with simple flanks. **Torch Saboteurs** perform warned actions only against tagged empty structures, reserve exteriors or warning equipment, with visible interrupt/extinguish windows and no people or indispensable evidence targets. **Wagon Guards** use convoy poles, short shields or heavy tools for slow-turning front-line lane control and protection of another enemy's objective action. **Mounted Raiders** use ordinary horses for bounded charges and withdrawal pressure; zero Stability produces safe dismount and reduced ordinary on-foot combat without horse death.

Extreme adds the feinting, angled, route-denial **Route-Cutter Veteran** without route closure or off-screen actions. Ultimate adds the signal-coordinating **Field Captain**; interrupting a readable hand/whistle signal cancels its formation, which cannot spawn hidden forces or unavoidable simultaneous failure. Only Saboteurs, designated Wagon Guards, Veterans and Captains receive objective actions; solo permits one approaching consequence and co-op two staggered consequences against different targets. Historical merchant/work clothing, cudgels, poles, short sabers, axes, torches, horses and wagons replace firearms, grenades, modern armour or sponsor identity. Surrender and individual live restraint remain available.

**Confirmed Stage 5 boss — Three-Phase Road-Cutter:** **Mounted Road-Cutter** gives Rovan bounded hooked-pole charges, angled passes and withdrawal feints on an ordinary horse whose zero Stability causes safe retreat without civilian collision or lethal crash. **Convoy Weapon Control** moves him on foot with short saber and shortened pole handle, lane cuts, legal clashes, tagged-scenery throws and warned nearby-raider commands; it contains the Broken Wagon sequence, Liang's injury, Sheng's rescue and Rovan's withdrawal. **Road-Cutter's Last Stand** is the Sheng-only mobile phase at the Warning Gate while Liang supports, using compact strikes, elbows, trips, rough clinches, escape attempts, Exact Blocks, Counters and one Defense-consuming Combo Breaker.

Rovan's weapons become secured evidence, never player pickups. Earlier objective results do not alter his statistics. He gains no armour transformation, rage immunity, reinforcements, hidden escape timer or voluntary loss. Legal Health/Defense/Resolve victory is mandatory before disarm, restraint, assessment and live custody. Higher difficulties add readable feints, formation timing, defense and punish routes without removing tells or creating unavoidable mounted pressure; his testimony corroborates evidence without immunity.

**Confirmed Stage 5 pickup weapons — Historical Defender and Raider Tool Set:** **Village Gate Staff** is a balanced long rack weapon for reach, broad rescue arcs, controlled thrusts and modest guard pressure. **Carpenter's Work Axe** is a slower heavy tool for guard and tagged wood damage but cannot harm evidence, indispensable reserve containers or protected structures. **Raider Short Saber** is a fast enemy drop for precision, advance and controlled disarm with non-gory presentation and no bonus against restrained targets. **Wagon Hook Pole** provides spacing, eligible loose-cargo pull, one tagged hazard redirect and separation without pulling civilians, bosses, horses, evidence or fixed structures.

Both historical heroes receive character-owned standing, aerial, low, ground, dash, pickup, throw and finisher interpretations for all four families: Sheng emphasizes interruption/disarm/redirection/separation, while Liang emphasizes structure/leverage/sweeps/crowd repositioning. Shared `J`, ownership, handoff, durability, drop, disarm, throw, boundary, anti-infinite, checkpoint and recovery rules apply. They come only from authored racks/tools/drops and cannot be pre-equipped, stored or purchased. Rovan's unique weapons remain evidence; Qiao Lan's personal carpenter tool remains a Museum artifact rather than an ordinary pickup.

**Confirmed Stage 5 historical transport — Two-Part Historical Transport Contract:** **Reserve Relay Ride** is a two-to-three-minute Chase segment from the Old Trade Entrance to the Communal Reserve. Solo gives the selected defender one ordinary horse; co-op gives one horse to each historical hero. Sheng Guo and Liang Zhen receive compact mounted interpretations of direction, `T`, `Z`, `G`, `U`, and eligible `H` actions through the unchanged combat engine. Each horse uses Stability and withdraws safely at zero without death or graphic injury. A complete on-foot fallback preserves completion and regroups the heroes at the reserve. Carried weapons are stowed and restored with exact ownership, durability, and checkpoint state.

**Reserve Wagon Passage** is a sixty-to-ninety-second authored moving-platform defense that carries protected supplies toward the storehouse. Players may fight from the wagon bed or alongside it but never steer it; co-op may board together or split without making either arrangement mandatory. The wagon cannot run over civilians, leave its authored route, or enter a permanently destroyed state. Warned exterior damage affects the Emergency Reserve Ledger without contradicting canonical delivery. The later **Broken Wagon** is a separate fixed, warned hazard and is never controllable; false-convoy wagons become stationary evidence or scenery after exposure. Rovan and ordinary Mounted Raiders share the same safe horse-Stability rules. All transport is stage-local, non-persistent, excluded from the Store, and provides no free roaming, animal collision weapon, lethal crash, or random-physics outcome. `STEADYRIDE` applies only to player horses, not the supply wagon or scripted Broken Wagon event.

**Confirmed Stage 5 environmental combat — Deterministic Historical Infrastructure Network:** The **Ledger Annex** uses damageable desks, shelves, shutters and loose crates while mandatory records remain indestructible. The **Old Trade Entrance** uses gate beams, movable barriers, wagon covers and warning-mechanism components without altering Qiao Lan's canonical warning. The **Communal Reserve** uses handcarts, sacks, empty containers, shutters, fire buckets and tagged wooden partitions. The **Storehouse Platforms** use pulleys, cargo stacks, workshop timber, ramps and the damaged wagon. The **Warning Gate** uses barricades, bell-frame supports, cargo and route stones without replacing Rovan's required direct defeat.

Eligible infrastructure follows readable **Intact → Damaged → Critical → Broken** states with tagged interactions, deterministic debris and strict scene quotas. Environmental attacks may damage, stun, knock down or separate eligible fighters, but cannot kill or permanently injure civilians, destroy mandatory evidence or essential reserves, alter Qiao Lan's sacrifice or Liang Zhen's injury, block the only valid route, defeat Rovan without completing his combat contract, or create graphic bodily injury or uncontrolled physics. Enemy sabotage, unavoidable canonical destruction and player collateral are recorded separately, so scripted history never penalizes the player. Solo and co-op use equivalent warned hazard schedules; reduced-effects settings preserve timing, warnings and collision legality while simplifying presentation.

**Confirmed Stage 5 recovery resources — Historical Provision Recovery Set:** **Steamed Grain Bun / 蒸杂粮包 / Gedämpftes Getreidebrötchen** restores Health, **Warm Tea / 热茶 / Warmer Tee** restores the Defense Bar, **Sesame Cake / 芝麻饼 / Sesamkuchen** restores the Attack Bar, and the rare **Communal Stew / 村社炖汤 / Gemeinschaftseintopf** moderately restores all three. Effects are gameplay abstractions rather than medical or historical-health claims.

Provisions come from personal food baskets, approved defender supplies, safe handovers or tagged clean containers rather than damaged or contaminated food. Their use never reduces the Emergency Reserve Ledger because defender portions are accounted for separately from protected communal stores. Shared `J` pickup, carrying, handover and eligible throwing rules apply. Solo and co-op receive equivalent total recovery value, with co-op placements preventing one player from being forced to consume both heroes' allocations. Ordinary provisions do not persist between acts or duplicate through checkpoints. The Communal Stew appears once, normally after Reserve Defense or behind a demanding optional objective. `FULLPANTRY` affects eligible ordinary provision containers only; it cannot create extra stew, improve the Reserve Ledger, refill Liang's support cache or alter boss resources.

**Confirmed Stage 5 optional content — Two Historical Preservation Branches:** **The Ledger Holds / 账册得存 / Das Hauptbuch bleibt** opens during Communal Reserve Defense when Torch Saboteurs attack the reserve-ledger annex as clerks evacuate. The optional branch asks the heroes to protect departing clerks, interrupt two warned sabotage actions, use fire buckets before the annex reaches Broken state, and secure supplementary trade and reserve records. Mandatory evidence remains indestructible in every outcome. Success preserves the supplementary records and upgrades the historical trade-ledger Museum exhibit; a partially scorched result remains canon-compatible and never blocks completion.

**The Warning Endures / 警钟长鸣 / Die Warnung bleibt** opens during the Warning Gate approach when raiders loot the small maintenance workshop connected to the warning mechanism. A short optional route lets the heroes defeat the looters, protect the workshop cabinet, recover Qiao Lan's personal carpenter's tool, and preserve an original warning-mechanism component. These become Museum artifacts explaining Qiao's practical work; the branch can neither prevent nor replay her sacrifice.

Both branches may be completed in one eligible run. Neither gates canonical completion, Rovan's capture, mandatory truth, Stage 6, or the highest attainable rank. Each grants optional score and a dedicated achievement; completing both grants a combined preservation achievement. Solo and co-op use equivalent pressure. Cheat-Assisted play may reveal the Museum material and localized unlock clues but grants no branch achievement, competitive score, or preservation record. Locked entries explain how to discover them without prematurely revealing Qiao's fate.

**Confirmed Stage 5 recognition — Eight-Achievement Historical Recognition Set:** The stage achievements are **The Bell Before Dawn / 黎明前的钟声 / Die Glocke vor der Morgendämmerung** for an eligible clear; **The Ledger Holds / 账册得存 / Das Hauptbuch bleibt** for fully preserving the ledger annex; **The Warning Endures / 警钟长鸣 / Die Warnung bleibt** for preserving Qiao Lan's tool and the warning component; **Memory Defended / 记忆得守 / Bewahrte Erinnerung** for both branches in one eligible run; **Every Evacuee Safe / 撤离者皆安 / Alle Evakuierten sicher** for all three tracked People Ledger groups ending Secure or Cleared, with Qiao excluded as fixed canon; **Reserve for Tomorrow / 为明日留粮 / Vorrat für morgen** for securing every avoidable reserve category; **Steady Through the Alarm / 警钟声中稳行 / Sicher durch den Alarm** for no forced dismount in Reserve Relay Ride and no Critical-state supply wagon; and **The Road-Cutter Bound / 截路者伏法 / Der Straßenräuber gefasst** for capturing Rovan after at least one Exact Block and one Counter in his encounter.

Any canonical clear unlocks Stage 6, mandatory Chronicle/Museum records, and the approved noncanonical Mission access for young Sheng Guo and Liang Zhen. First eligible clear grants Credits and Store visibility for their **Historical Civic-Defender Interpretation** and **Village Archive Commemoration Attire**. First A grants a text-free warning-bell profile emblem; first S grants **Keeper of the First Warning / 初警守望者 / Hüter der ersten Warnung**. No achievement requires co-op and neither branch gates S. Cheat-Assisted completion preserves story progression, mandatory records, hero access and catalogue visibility but grants no achievement, Credits, rank, emblem, title or competitive record.

**Confirmed Stage 5 evaluation — Protection-First Historical Scoring Model:** The 100-point base score consists of **Combat Mastery** 30, **People Protection** 25, **Reserve Stewardship** 20, **Infrastructure Discipline** 10, **Transport Control** 10, and **Boss Discipline** 5. Combat rewards legal variety, advanced defense, safe co-op extension and controlled restraint; protection and stewardship read the approved ledgers; infrastructure distinguishes interrupted sabotage from avoidable collateral; transport reads horse Stability and supply-wagon condition; boss discipline requires Rovan's legal defeat and custody transition. The two optional branches add up to ten leaderboard bonus points, five each, but never change S/A/B/C/D thresholds, so S remains attainable without either branch.

Qiao Lan's sacrifice, Liang Zhen's injury, the canonical wagon loss, gate damage and every other unavoidable historical event are excluded from penalties. Mandatory evidence gives no performance bonus because it cannot be lost. There is no general speed category that could encourage abandoning civilians or objectives. Solo and co-op use normalized separate leaderboards; co-op receives one shared rank plus individual contribution summaries rather than teammate competition. Difficulty applies its approved multiplier only after base evaluation, while exact thresholds remain Combat Lab data. Continued, Cheat-Assisted, invalidated or unverified-recovery runs retain canonical progression but receive no competitive score, rank, Credits or records.

**Confirmed Stage 5 persistence — Five-Anchor Historical State Contract:** The authored anchors are **Ledger Discovery** after the annex investigation and first attack; **Warning Raised** after false-convoy exposure and Qiao Lan's fixed warning event; **Reserve Secured** after reserve defense, branch outcomes and relay-ride state commit; **Storehouse Interception** after the supply-wagon passage and before Rovan's wagon-crisis sequence; and **Measured Hand Stand** after Liang Zhen's injury, Sheng Guo's rescue and the deterministic final-phase handoff.

Each snapshot stores active hero and exact Health/Defense/Attack/status, weapons and their ownership/durability/stowed or dropped state, all four ledgers, branch and Museum-artifact results, horse and wagon outcomes, infrastructure and collateral, evidence, enemy/boss/surrender state, Liang's support cache and remaining actions, score, difficulty, player count, eligibility and co-op authority. Restore never rerolls encounters, repairs objects, improves ledgers, duplicates provisions, restores consumed support or changes a committed branch. Verified crash/reload/disconnect/host-migration recovery preserves eligibility only from an exact validated snapshot. One transactional **Suspend Stage** record may be created at an authored anchor and is consumed on resume. Voluntary or defeat-based **Continue from Checkpoint** preserves canon and Stage 6 access but marks the run Continued and removes competitive score, rank, Credits, achievements, emblems, titles and records. The Liang-to-Sheng transition cannot be reversed or duplicated; a fresh eligible attempt always begins at the stage opening.

**Confirmed Stage 5 storytelling presentation — Hybrid Historical Presentation Contract:** A short skippable present-day opening shows Sheng Guo, Kael and the neutral archivist examining sealed records, while detailed evidence remains pauseable and reviewable in-engine. Illustrated documents, old-village views and portraits of young Sheng and Liang introduce a clearly labelled corroborated historical dramatization rather than time travel or supernatural memory. Qiao Lan's discovery, warning mechanism, village reaction, respectful cutaway and aftermath remain in-engine and non-graphic. Liang's wagon redirection, injury, rescue and control transition remain fully in-engine because they alter authoritative gameplay state.

A skippable illustrated **Month of Reckoning** montage covers mourning, investigation, Rovan's testimony, evidence against Oren, Sheng's individual-judgment proposal, Zhao Heng's rejection and the collective exile. The present-day return lets Sheng acknowledge both the real betrayal and unjust collective punishment, confirms Kael's separation from Tomas and advances the sponsor trail toward Stage 6. Google Flow footage uses supplied start/end frames, one focal action, small existing-frame motion, stable camera direction and minimal character movement; complex fights, groups, handovers and state changes stay in-engine or are assembled in editing. Flow assets contain no speech or embedded text. English, Simplified Chinese and German subtitles, transcripts, speaker labels, optional Windows-generated narration, music and SFX are added afterward. Every clip is independently skippable, Museum-replayable and backed by a still-image fallback. Document writing is controlled localized UI, never AI-generated text inside imagery.

**Confirmed Stage 5 audio — The Warning Remembered Audio Suite:** Seven original cues define the chapter: **Records under Lamplight** for restrained present-day evidence review; **Before the Bell** for quiet historical investigation; **False Colours at the Gate** for the exposed deception; **The Bell Carries** for the warning, relay ride and reserve defense; **Timber and Duty** for wagon passage, Liang's stand and Sheng's rescue; **Cut the Road No More** for Rovan's encounter; and **What Judgment Left Behind** for investigation, exile and present return. The warning-bell interval is the shared musical/narrative motif. Historical cues use restrained acoustic plucked and bowed strings, bamboo flute, hand drums, wooden percussion and low winds; present framing adds a subtle modern ambient layer. Generated music has no vocals, lyrics, recognizable melody or artist imitation, and combat cues provide loopable exploration, pressure, climax and resolution stems.

Key SFX cover ledgers/seals, concealed weapons, torches, water buckets, gate machinery, hoofbeats, wagon wheels, strained timber, pulleys and near/distant warning-bell layers. Animal audio avoids distress; Qiao's sacrifice and Liang's injury use environment, reactions and silence rather than graphic impact. Exact Block, Counter, Combo Breaker, mounted charge, sabotage and support cues remain mechanically distinct. Music ducks for narration, essential dialogue, warnings and accessibility announcements. English, Simplified Chinese and German receive localized captions for meaningful sounds. The seven cues and major SFX become Museum entries through approved discovery or completion gates.

**Confirmed Stage 5 difficulty — Five-Tier Historical Threat Matrix:** **Easy** uses core enemies, fewer simultaneous attackers, longer objective windows, slower mounted approaches and simpler Rovan feints while retaining all story, branches, evidence and Museum content. **Medium** is the canonical baseline. **Hard** improves core coordination: Wagon Guards protect Saboteurs, Mounted Raiders stagger passes, enemies punish repeated routes and Rovan adds delayed attacks and defensive conversions without hiding warnings. **Extreme** introduces authored **Route-Cutter Veterans** with visible feints, angled lane denial, weapon changes and pursuit control that cannot close the only route or act off-screen; Rovan adds one mounted redirect and advanced on-foot counter route. **Ultimate** introduces the **Field Captain**, sometimes with one Veteran; visible hand/whistle signals coordinate existing enemies and may be interrupted to cancel formation, with no hidden reinforcements. Rovan uses his complete legal feint, defense, breaker, escape-pressure and punish kit.

Difficulty grows through behavior, coordination, timing and pattern variety rather than excessive Health/damage inflation. Advanced roles never alter canon or imply a modern sponsor link. Solo/co-op compositions scale separately within objective-concurrency caps, optional branches remain achievable, recovery decreases without disappearing, and all dangerous actions keep accessible warnings. `QUIETCROWD` may reduce ordinary commitment but never removes selected-difficulty Veterans, Captains, boss phases or required pressure. Cheat-Assisted clears may unlock the next difficulty and encountered bestiary/Museum records so less-experienced players can experience advanced enemies, but grant no competitive or prestige rewards.

**Confirmed Stage 5 cheat profile — Shared-Cheat Interpretation Profile:** No stage-specific code is added. `SOFTERBLOWS` and `SAFEHEART` affect ordinary hero Health only; `STEADYFEET` changes eligible ordinary knockdowns; and neither can alter Qiao Lan's sacrifice, Liang Zhen's injury or the Broken Wagon transition. `IRONGUARD` protects eligible Defense cost but not horse Stability, wagon condition, reserves or infrastructure. `BRIGHTSPIRIT` fills the ordinary Attack Bar without bypassing Ultimate, boss or custody legality. `STRONGHANDS` doubles eligible Health/Resolve damage but not Stability, wagon, environment or objective effects. `TRUSTYTOOLS` protects the four carried pickup families only, not fixed mechanisms, wagons, structures or Rovan's evidence weapons. `STEADYRIDE` preserves player-horse Stability while retaining controls, reactions and route requirements; it excludes enemy horses and both wagons.

`QUIETCROWD` cannot remove selected Veterans, Captains, Rovan, branch threats or mandatory pressure. `FULLPANTRY` remains limited to ordinary provision containers. `HOMECOMING` restores the latest exact validated checkpoint without improvement or reroll. `SAFEPASSAGE` displays **Not Applicable in This Stage** because tracked people and indispensable evidence cannot enter completion-blocking dead, Lost or permanently destroyed states; it cannot preserve Secure/Protected grading or optional branch assets. Any active code marks the full run Cheat-Assisted. Such clears may unlock Stage 6, difficulties, canon, approved historical-hero Mission access, mandatory records and Store visibility, but grant no score, rank, Credits, achievements, preservation record, emblem, title or leaderboard entry.

**Confirmed Stage 5 learning — Answer the Bell Learning Package:** The localized **Answer the Bell / 应钟而行 / Dem Ruf der Glocke folgen** Guide card is available from briefing, pause and Help. Before first completion it explains the evidence-based reconstruction and fixed canon; four ledgers; horse Stability and wagon condition; historical weapons/provisions; environmental warnings; Liang-to-Sheng handoff; spoiler-safe branch symbols; and continuation, assistance and cheat consequences. After completion it expands with full character, evidence, branch and historical context.

The post-clear Academy curriculum contains **Measured Hand Foundations**, **Stone and Current Foundations**, **Protect before Pursuing**, **Ride, Dismount, Defend**, **Reciprocal Rescue**, and **Binding the Road-Cutter**. The fifth lesson provides a deterministic partner in solo. Lessons use the unchanged engine and never rewrite canon; Liang retains his complete reusable kit. Free Practice permits approved configurable replay loadouts, and My Combos records discovered Sheng/Liang freestyle routes. Academy never gates Stage 6, Story, heroes or standard moves. English, Simplified Chinese and German text, demonstrations, prompts, transcripts, captions and controller diagrams are supported; keyboard prompts honor remapping/German physical-key handling and gamepad prompts follow the active controller.

**Confirmed Stage 5 accessibility — Multimodal Historical Clarity Contract:** All four ledgers combine distinct icons, shapes, localized labels, patterns and screen-reader announcements. Sabotage, evacuation pressure, mounted charges, falling cargo, wagon hazards, boss signals and support opportunities combine target lines, direction markers, silhouettes, captions, distinct audio and optional vibration. Combat resources and environmental states remain distinguishable without colour. Evidence uses scalable high-contrast controlled text, narration, keyboard/gamepad navigation and complete transcripts.

Mounting, provisions, fire buckets, workshop protection, weapons, Liang support and restraint support hold/toggle/reduced-input forms, with no mandatory rapid repeated press. Riding offers steering sensitivity, camera-centering and route-guidance adjustments. Handoffs announce incoming hero, transferred resources, support cache and changed controls before resume; co-op shares warnings and accessible pings without requiring voice chat. Flash, shake, camera motion, particles, fire, debris and background crowds reduce independently while preserving simulation timing/collision/warnings. Story sequences may be skipped or replaced by localized still panels. Music, speech, bell, combat SFX and accessibility announcements have separate volumes and meaningful sounds receive localized captions. Non-simulation accessibility never harms eligibility; simulation-changing timing, steering, commitment, damage or pressure help is labelled Assisted under shared record rules, never a cheat. No option rewrites history, auto-completes an objective or removes Rovan's genuine defeat.

**Confirmed Stage 5 networking — Deterministic Co-op Authority Contract:** Same-device co-op uses one shared simulation; local-Wi-Fi uses the approved host-authoritative deterministic model. Sheng and Liang remain uniquely assigned, no new participant joins after launch, and only the original peer may reconnect. Weapons, provisions, horses, branches, fire buckets, ledgers, support and environment interactions use atomic ownership to prevent latency duplication. Each player owns one horse during the relay; wagon boarding never silently transfers items. Disconnect never despawns or rescales encounter pressure.

After a short grace, the established restricted survival proxy may follow, block, evade and use conservative basics with real resources, but cannot choose branches, collect/throw weapons, consume provisions, spend Ultimate meter, initiate co-op attacks or use Liang support. Rejoin validates the authoritative snapshot and returns control at a safe actionable moment. The canonical injury/handoff proceeds despite interruption. If Sheng's player disconnects during **Measured Hand Stand**, the connected Liang player may accept safe-boundary **Historical Role Reassignment** to control Sheng while optional Liang support becomes inactive; no resources or score are added. Liang disconnection never blocks Sheng because support is optional. Host migration restores exact authoritative state. Validated recovery preserves eligibility; severe desync, unverified correction or excessive proxy/reassignment may preserve completion as Continued. Authority, rollback, proxy, reassignment, reconnect and hashes are recorded.

**Confirmed Stage 5 wardrobe — Historical Identity Wardrobe Contract:** Young Sheng Guo's canonical default uses a lean 26-year-old silhouette, short indigo-grey cross-collar work jacket, warm-beige inner layer, dark practical trousers, cloth forearm wraps, flexible ankle boots, close records pouch, restrained bronze stitching and a text-free village-knot emblem; he has no elder robes, cane, loose sleeves, senior authority or modern protection. Liang Zhen's distinct broader 31-year-old default uses an earth-brown wrap jacket, muted moss protective overshirt, stone-grey trousers, reinforced cloth wraps and sturdy work boots, with practical field protection rather than armour and no pre-event limp, brace, cane or injury marker.

The visual-only Store **Historical Civic-Defender Interpretation** gives Sheng a fitted slate-blue/ivory/bronze training jacket and light guards, while Liang receives deep stone-blue/river-grey reinforced training layers, broad belt, segmented guards and heavier boots. **Village Archive Commemoration Attire** gives Sheng muted burgundy/navy with an abstract warning-bell seam motif and Liang pine/charcoal with river-and-stone panel shapes; all remain fitted and combat-readable without robes, capes, loose ornaments, text or exaggerated memorial imagery. First canonical completion fixes defaults. Alternatives are available only in approved replay, Chapter Challenge, Mission Remix, Random Operations and Academy contexts and change no statistics, moves, hitboxes or weapon behavior. Designs preserve animation, ground combat, weapon clearance, mounting and pre-injury state. Pricing/final artwork remain Store/art data.

**Confirmed Stage 5 records — Fact, Performance, and Memory Archive Contract:** Results separate **Fixed Historical Outcome** from **Player Performance** and **Run Integrity**. Fixed facts list Qiao's warning, Liang's injury, Rovan's capture, Oren's betrayal and collective exile without treating them as player failures. Performance shows score categories/bonus/rank, difficulty, heroes, player count, loadout, four ledgers, transport, collateral, branches, boss discipline, achievements and rewards. Integrity labels Standard, Assisted, Cheat-Assisted, Continued, validated recovery, proxy, Historical Role Reassignment and other eligibility facts.

The Campaign Chronicle provides present and reconstructed timelines plus a source matrix marking conclusions **Confirmed**, **Corroborated** or **Unresolved**, distinguishing Oren's guilt from collective punishment and linking Stage 4 contradictions to Stage 6; speculation never masquerades as fact. Mandatory Museum entries cover Su Yan records, concealed weapons, Brann testimony, Rovan routes/depots, four pickup families, village/transport/enemy/hero/boss/media/audio material. Branches upgrade the reserve-ledger exhibit and add Qiao's tool, warning component and maintenance commentary. Generated document art contains no readable invented text; localization is controlled UI. Run Archive categories separate difficulty, solo/co-op, hero/pair and ruleset/integrity class; eligible replay capsules retain deterministic inputs, version, authority/checkpoint history, ledgers, score and hashes. Canonical Replay, Chapter Challenge and Mission Remix remain visibly distinct. Locked entries expose localized spoiler-safe clues through every input and screen reader.

**Confirmed Stage 5 pacing — Eight-Encounter Historical Pressure Curve:** The first Story clear targets 40–50 minutes including evidence and optional exploration; experienced replay targets 28–35 minutes with presentation skipped. Eight mandatory ordinary encounters precede/surround Rovan's continuous three-phase confrontation: one in Ledger Before Dawn; two across convoy exposure and secondary-gate defense; two across reserve evacuation/protection; two across supply-wagon passage and storehouse interception; and one Warning Gate approach. Each preservation branch adds at most one short engagement.

Initial solo ordinary counts are approximately 18/24/30/34/38 from Easy through Ultimate and remain tuning baselines. Co-op adds about 35–40% complementary ordinary pressure rather than doubling enemies, Health or objective danger. Simultaneous ordinary pressure caps at four solo/six co-op; approaching objective actions cap at one/two staggered against different targets. Mounted lanes cap at one solo/two staggered co-op without unavoidable crossings. Veterans/Captains replace suitable slots. Surrendered, restrained, protected, off-screen or transition enemies neither score nor respawn. Branches, evidence, dialogue, cinematics, synchronized holds, loading and accessibility reading create no hidden pressure. Rovan carries exact resources across phases without refill.

**Confirmed Stage 5 briefing — Two-Layer Evidence and Deployment Briefing:** Present-day evidence orientation explains the Stage 4 contradictions, authenticated sources, unresolved questions and the non-rewritable corroborated reconstruction, with source inspection, localization, Guide access and direct continue. Historical deployment then presents Sheng and Liang; solo/co-op; difficulty/advanced roles; four ledgers; horse, wagon, weapon, provision and environment rules; spoiler-safe branch markers; checkpoint/continuation/assistance/cheat/scoring/reward consequences; current bindings and accessibility.

First-clear solo selects Sheng or Liang while the other uses authored support/handoff; co-op assigns both uniquely. Fixed historical defaults, initial Specials, fixed Ultimate and canonical clothing apply, players start unarmed, and purchased moves/Ultimates/costumes, modern heroes and modern equipment are unavailable. The briefing says only that historical circumstances may change active responsibilities. Post-clear Canonical Replay and Chapter Challenge remain Sheng/Liang-only with approved configurable historical loadouts/cosmetics, while Mission Remix is visibly noncanonical with its wider approved roster. Difficulty, hero, loadout, ruleset, assistance or cheat changes require renewed readiness; Wi-Fi peers validate matching content versions.

**Confirmed Stage 5 release validation — History-to-Archive Acceptance Matrix:** Release coverage includes both solo starts and unique co-op pair; all five difficulties and advanced roles; fixed first-clear and legal replay configurations; eight encounters, two branches, transport and all Rovan phases; every four-ledger transition; complete hero/weapon/provision/horse/wagon/environment/support behavior; injury, handoff, support, reassignment and reconnect; checkpoint/suspension/continue/recovery/rollback/migration/replay; scoring, rewards, unlocks, Chronicle/Museum/Stage 6; every integrity class; all approved inputs/co-op/networking; EN/zh-Hans/DE; accessibility and still fallbacks; browsers/quality/save/migration/export/import/replay/Legacy; and 60-FPS target/30-FPS minimum without simulation drift.

Negative proof forbids alternate/graphic/player-blamed Qiao outcomes; preventable/caused/worsened/duplicated Liang injury; missing/destroyed/duplicated/rewritten evidence; penalties for fixed damage; branches gating S/canon/Rovan/Stage 6; scripted/scenery/skipped/custody-shortcut Rovan defeat; horse death/collision weapons; civilian death, off-screen loss, unavoidable mounted crossing, infinite/unfair combat, softlocks or rerolls; duplication of items/scores/rewards/branches/support/Store state; first-clear modern heroes/technology/purchased alternatives; generated document text; or presentation/access settings changing authoritative simulation. Release requires zero unresolved Critical/High defects. Exact frame data, thresholds, final art/media, encounter tuning and performance budgets remain governed production validation rather than reopened feature decisions.

#### Approved canonical roster progression

- **Stage 3:** Mountain Guardian, River Scout, Wind Sentinel, and Fire Warden are selectable. No new hero debuts.
- **Stage 4:** The same four village heroes are selectable. Kael is a story opponent and cannot be selected during his confrontation.
- **Stage 5:** Only young Sheng Guo and Liang Zhen are canonical selections. Solo play chooses one primary hero while the other appears through scripted support and handoff sequences; co-op assigns one unique hero to each player. First completion unlocks both historical heroes for non-canonical Mission Remixes, never modern Story Mode.
- **Stage 6:** The four village heroes return. Kael remains independently occupied with the Wayfarer Watch. Early robot assistance may function as support but not as a selectable hero.
- **Stage 7:** Kael becomes a canonical guest selection when the Wayfarer Watch voluntarily joins the Compact investigation. Soren and Chen Yue appear as non-playable canonical allies while becoming playable in completed Mission Remixes and Random Operations through the Wayfarer Watch unlock path.
- **Stage 8:** Mountain Guardian, River Scout, Wind Sentinel, Fire Warden, and Kael are selectable. If Kael is not selected, he still participates through support scenes. Duplicate heroes remain prohibited.

This progression adds young Sheng Guo, Liang Zhen, and Kael as meaningful playable identities without introducing unrelated Story heroes before the established cast receives its narrative development. Soren and Chen Yue expand non-canonical mission variety without altering the approved Story roster.

#### Approved modernization and technology progression

- **Stage 3:** Improved patrol clothing, rugged communications, mapping tools, portable medical equipment, and safe herb-sample storage support mountain rescue. Robots and advanced combat wearables are not yet present.
- **Stage 4:** Authenticated evidence seals, body-worn incident recording, wearable health monitoring, and modern nonlethal restraint equipment enter field use. Village interludes show early printed construction, solar-roof pilots, and work beginning on the modern transport bridge.
- **Stage 5:** The historical battle deliberately removes modern technology. Manual warnings, traditional tools, old transport, physical ledgers, and human messengers contrast with the developing present-day village.
- **Stage 6:** Utility robots, the completed transport bridge, an expanding solar microgrid, organized charging and service infrastructure, and early protective-material clothing enter ordinary village life. A limited robot-support sequence remains under human supervision and is not yet a standard summon.
- **Stage 7:** Temporary robot assistance becomes an approved gameplay system. Protective wearables and advanced-material outfits become mature sidegrades rather than automatic upgrades. Support robots are regulated, interruptible, cooldown-limited, and unable to complete objectives independently.
- **Stage 8:** Mature systems assist evacuation, communication, medical response, and evidence preservation. Compact disruption makes manual overrides, human judgment, and direct protection essential; technology supports the victory but cannot produce it automatically.

The visual progression affects clothing, infrastructure, transport, and daily life together. It remains village-scaled and humane rather than becoming a sudden transition into generic science fiction.

#### Approved location and conflict progression

- **Stage 3 — mountain rescue:** The route crosses a mountain trail, cliffside gathering settlement, rare-herb basin, abandoned quarry passages, and the bandit camp. The self-contained objective is rescuing every captive and securing the surviving herb samples.
- **Stage 4 — evidence-road interception:** The action follows the Changling Trade Road, a rural waystation, damaged convoy routes, and the approach to a Regional Council evidence facility. Kael attacks an authenticated evidence transport after Tomas convinces him that it carries falsified material. The heroes protect civilians, stop the interception, and capture Kael alive.
- **Stage 5 — historical village defense:** The playable past includes the old trade entrance, communal reserve, storehouse platforms, warning-gate mechanism, and wagon route during Rovan's false-convoy assault. It ends with Rovan's capture and discovery of Oren's documented betrayal before returning to the present-day exile controversy.
- **Stage 6 — abandoned instrument:** The modern bridge, expanding logistics district, solar service area, robot-maintenance facility, and transformed village perimeter become Tomas's supposed decisive infrastructure target. The Compact withdraws transport and support after gathering defensive data. The heroes protect the district while Tomas discovers that the network has sacrificed him.
- **Stage 7 — Meridian exposure:** A Yunzhou shell-consultancy office, regional freight exchange, and Meridian Forum branch archive form one evidence-recovery operation. Compact cleanup teams attempt to destroy records and intimidate witnesses. The Wayfarer Watch protects evacuation routes while the heroes recover and match the Han, Rhea, and Damon records.
- **Stage 8 — Fight for Peace:** The final route crosses the public Forum facilities, logistics and data-control areas, operations yard, transport hub, and Compact command complex.

The six later stages therefore have distinct action identities: wilderness rescue, convoy conflict, historical defense, modern village protection, urban evidence recovery, and institutional finale.

#### Approved boss progression

- **Stage 3 — Mountain Bandit Chief:** An experienced terrain fighter and raider who accepted Tomas's money and planning. The chief uses improvised long weapons, throws, elevation changes, and environmental hazards. Tomas remains the strategist and withdraws rather than fighting. The chief is captured alive.
- **Stage 4 — Kael Rell:** Kael serves as the principal opponent through his pressure-boxing and chain-wrestling system. Soren and Chen Yue are not additional bosses. Their concern for civilians exposes problems in Tomas's account, while Kael's decision to cover their withdrawal and protect bystanders creates the circumstances for his live capture without making his defeat voluntary or trivial.
- **Stage 5 — Rovan Kest:** The historical commander combines mounted pressure, raider weapons, and close combat. His defeat and capture conclude the playable assault while Oren remains a noncombatant conspirator.
- **Stage 6 — Procurement Recovery Marshal:** A deniable specialist sent through the procurement cutout to recover surveillance data and equipment after the Compact abandons Tomas's force. The marshal prioritizes extraction, uses modern field equipment, and possesses neither Compact insignia nor knowledge of the steering circle. The heroes capture the marshal, while Tomas escapes with part of the recovery order.
- **Stage 7 — Compact Erasure Chief:** A direct cleanup commander responsible for destroying Forum-branch evidence and intimidating witnesses. The chief uses defensive technology, area denial, and coordinated archive-destruction hazards. Defeat permits recovery of the three records while preserving Rhea as the superior final combatant.
- **Stage 8 — Rhea Calder:** The unique final physical boss uses the approved Vector Command System, baton phase, breakable wearables, Defense Bar, counters, and fair co-op rules. Han and Damon remain noncombatant captures.

Every boss obeys the combat engine's resource, anti-infinite, Combo Breaker, and readable-counter rules. Canonical outcomes emphasize live capture and individual accountability rather than execution.

#### Approved mount, weapon, and environmental-damage progression

- **Stage 3:** Mountain horses provide short riding and pursuit sections and disengage safely when their stamina is depleted. Pickups include a hiking staff, wood axe, quarry hammer, and a bandit revolver with very limited ammunition. Quarry scaffolds, carts, loose stone, and timber barriers support impact damage and throwable debris.
- **Stage 4:** Mid-class motorbikes and utility bikes support the convoy pursuit. Pickups emphasize road equipment such as batons, tire irons, and emergency tools rather than heavy firearms. Vehicles, roadside barriers, crates, and waystation furniture provide environmental impacts.
- **Stage 5:** Horses and wagons function as historical mounts and hazards. Pickups include staffs, work axes, short sabers, and convoy tools; modern grenades and automatic weapons are absent. Storehouse platforms, damaged wagons, gates, and cargo can be broken graphically while bodily injury remains non-graphic.
- **Stage 6:** Hostile equipment expands to revolvers, finite-ammunition automatic weapons, and limited fictional concussion grenades. A heavy machine gun has one restricted ammunition supply, slows movement, prevents jumping and grappling, and is discarded when empty. Motorbikes and electric utility vehicles support the bridge and logistics areas.
- **Stage 7:** Short electric cargo-vehicle sections replace a full mount route. Cleanup teams may drop sidearms, compact automatic weapons, control batons, and single-use concussion grenades. Office partitions, shutters, freight pallets, and cargo containers become environmental combat elements.
- **Stage 8:** One short transport-hub vehicle sequence appears without unrestricted indoor riding. Security weapons remain finite and disposable. Reinforced barriers, cargo systems, control panels, and data-area structures support environmental attacks, while careless destruction lowers preservation rank and Museum recovery without changing the canonical ending.

All weapons are stage pickups rather than pre-stage loadout equipment and obey durability, ammunition, pickup, throw, and replacement rules. Environmental destruction creates tactical opportunities but never requires harm to civilians or destruction of indispensable evidence.

#### Approved canonical endings and transitions

- **Stage 3:** Every captive is rescued alive, enough viable herb material is recovered for controlled research, and the Mountain Bandit Chief is captured. Injuries, damaged supplies, and optional rescues affect presentation and rank. Tomas escapes through a prepared mountain route. The damaged invoice begins the Stage 4 investigation.
- **Stage 4:** The evidence convoy and civilians survive. Kael is defeated and captured after covering Soren and Chen Yue's withdrawal. Authenticated records disprove key parts of Tomas's story, and Kael voluntarily suspends his allegiance, accepts responsibility for the interception, and chooses neutrality. Disputed historical records trigger the mandatory Stage 5 reconstruction.
- **Stage 5:** Rovan is captured, Oren's betrayal is proven, and the family-wide exile is shown to have exceeded the evidence. Returning to the present, Sheng Guo acknowledges both his resistance and his later failure to repair the injustice. Kael confirms his separation from Tomas, while the historical freight evidence advances the sponsor investigation.
- **Stage 6:** The logistics district and critical infrastructure remain operational, although optional assets may require repair. The Recovery Marshal is captured, while Tomas escapes with part of the abandonment order.
