He proves that the network used him but keeps the evidence as leverage rather than immediately helping the village.
- **Stage 7:** Witnesses survive, the three decisive records are recovered, and the Compact's identity and leaders are established. Tomas voluntarily surrenders his retained evidence and enters lawful custody. Kael and the Wayfarer Watch cooperate openly without joining the village patrol. The Council begins the immediate Stage 8 intervention.
- **Stage 8:** Civilians survive, sufficient archives are preserved, Rhea is defeated alive, and all three steering leaders are captured. Performance changes rank, Museum recovery, achievements, and epilogue inserts rather than the peaceful canonical resolution.

Every stage therefore delivers a complete local victory while preserving consequences and an unbroken transition into the larger campaign.

#### Approved optional-branch and Museum progression

Each later stage contains two optional branches and one Museum discovery. Completing both branches grants a stage-specific achievement and Museum presentation upgrade. Discoveries unlock art, music, video, historical context, or character commentary rather than combat power. Missed discoveries remain recoverable through replay, and no optional route contains a mandatory story fact.

- **Stage 3:** Restore a cliffside signal hut and rescue its trapped operator; search an abandoned herbalist shelter for missing gathering equipment. The Museum receives a non-actionable illustrated record of mountain ecology.
- **Stage 4:** Evacuate civilians trapped at the damaged waystation; recover a convoy recorder before environmental damage destroys it. The Museum receives an early Wayfarer Watch training token connected to Kael's group.
- **Stage 5:** Protect the reserve-ledger annex from fire and looting; recover Qiao Lan's carpenter tool and part of the original warning mechanism without altering her canonical sacrifice. The Museum receives the preserved historical warning-bell mechanism collection.
- **Stage 6:** Stabilize the solar microgrid before cascading shutdown; evacuate technicians and protect civilian utility robots in the maintenance bay. The Museum receives a component from the village's first retired service robot.
- **Stage 7:** Protect witnesses isolated inside a consultancy office; recover supplemental freight records identifying additional victim communities. The Museum receives a lawful Forum public-history exhibit showing how legitimate work was exploited by the Compact.
- **Stage 8:** Evacuate the coordination complex's medical and employee-support wing; secure a contingency vault containing supplemental victim and asset records. The Museum receives the authenticated three-record set that exposed the Compact.

Optional performance may enrich epilogue inserts, recognition, and replay goals but never changes hostage survival, decisive evidence recovery, leader capture, or the peaceful canonical ending.

Locked achievement and Museum entries never appear as unexplained blanks. Hovering with a pointer, focusing with keyboard or gamepad, or selecting by click, confirmation button, or touch displays a concise localized unlock clue in English, Simplified Chinese, or German according to the active language. The same clue is exposed through accessibility labels and screen readers. Hints identify the relevant stage and type of action without revealing unreached mandatory plot facts; after the associated stage is cleared, they may become more explicit. If cheats or another eligibility rule affect an achievement, the hint states that requirement clearly.

#### Approved enemy and difficulty progression

Easy, Medium, and Hard use each stage's core roster with increasing coordination and pattern complexity. Extreme introduces one advanced specialist; Ultimate adds a second elite and more demanding mixed formations.

- **Stage 3:** Trail Ambusher, Quarry Bruiser, Rope Trapper, and Mounted Raider form the core. Extreme adds the visibly feinting Ridge Stalker; Ultimate adds the Pack Captain with readable coordinated flanks.
- **Stage 4:** Road Blocker, Baton Interceptor, Bike Pursuer, and Restraint Controller form the core. Extreme adds the objective-pressuring Convoy Breaker; Ultimate adds the Counter Grappler.
- **Stage 5:** False Merchant, Torch Saboteur, Wagon Guard, and Mounted Raider form the core. Extreme adds the Route-Cutter Veteran; Ultimate adds Rovan's formation-coordinating Field Captain.
- **Stage 6:** Mercenary Striker, Retrieval Shield, Hijack Technician, and Finite-Ammunition Gunner form the core. Extreme adds the Recovery Specialist; Ultimate adds the Systems Breacher, whose temporary utility-device hijacks are telegraphed and interruptible.
- **Stage 7:** Archive Suppressor, Witness Grabber, Shield Escort, and Technology Disabler form the core. Extreme adds the Erasure Specialist; Ultimate adds the Counter-Intelligence Captain.
- **Stage 8:** Formation Guard, Baton Interceptor, Shield Controller, and Systems Jammer form the core. Extreme adds the Vector Lieutenant; Ultimate adds a complementary Command Pair whose coordination can be broken by separating or countering them.

Higher difficulty changes composition, cooperation, timing, and objective pressure rather than only health and damage. Every specialist retains readable tells, finite defensive resources, defense-consuming Combo Breakers, anti-infinite protection, and punishable recovery.

#### Approved recovery-food progression

Every later stage uses three standard recovery categories: Life food restores Health, Defense food restores the Defense Bar, and Momentum food restores the Attack Bar. One scarce balanced meal per stage moderately restores all three resources and normally appears only once or within a demanding optional route.

- **Stage 3:** Trail rice parcel, warm vegetable broth, spiced seed cake, and a rare mountain camp meal.
- **Stage 4:** Waystation noodle box, electrolyte drink, roasted-nut bar, and a rare convoy lunch.
- **Stage 5:** Steamed grain bun, warm tea, sesame cake, and a rare communal stew.
- **Stage 6:** Greenhouse vegetable wrap, aquaponic fish broth, fruit-energy gel, and a rare balanced village meal.
- **Stage 7:** Yunzhou rice bowl, hydration bottle, cocoa-nut bar, and a rare cooperative meal box.
- **Stage 8:** Employee meal pack, emergency hydration pouch, response-energy chew, and a rare coordination-center meal.

All recovery effects are gameplay abstractions rather than medical claims. The rare research herb and experimental formula never appear as consumable power-ups. Food retains the established `J` pickup, carry, consume, and throw interactions, while exact recovery amounts and difficulty-specific placements remain balance data.

#### Approved costume and Store progression

The confirmed Store rule remains active: every new stage contributes one character-specific **Stage Service Outfit** and one **Village Heritage Outfit** for every hero available through that stage. Each is a meaningful visual redesign rather than a palette swap, remains visual-only and independent of combat styles, is eligible in Standard Loadouts, and stays permanently purchasable after its reveal condition.

- **Stage 3:** Mountain Rescue Outfit and Highland Gathering Festival Attire.
- **Stage 4:** Road and Convoy Guardian Outfit and Traveler's Homecoming Attire.
- **Stage 5:** Historical Civic-Defender Interpretation and Village Archive Commemoration Attire.
- **Stage 6:** Infrastructure Emergency-Response Outfit and Village Renewal Festival Attire.
- **Stage 7:** Evidence and Witness-Protection Outfit and Yunzhou Cooperative Formal Attire.
- **Stage 8:** Regional Peacekeeping Coordination Outfit and Peace and Reconciliation Festival Attire.

Canonical default clothing also evolves freely at Stages 3, 6, and 8 so that story modernization is never locked behind Store purchases. These free default updates do not replace either purchasable outfit.

Young Sheng Guo and Liang Zhen use historically accurate defaults during the mandatory first completion of Stage 5; purchased alternatives become available only in replay and Mission Remix. Kael launches with a default outfit and two Stage 4 alternatives when his free-mode unlock becomes available. Soren and Chen Yue launch with defaults and two Stage 7 alternatives when their Wayfarer Watch mission content unlocks, and each receives the Stage 8 pair. No costume available before the Stage 7 reveal may expose Meridian Compact names or insignia. Locked entries follow the approved localized clue behavior.

Phase 2 is complete. Stages 3–8 now have approved titles, chronology, local conflicts, locations, playable rosters, boss roles, sponsor clues, canonical endings, technology progression, mounts, weapons, environmental systems, enemy and difficulty ladders, recovery food, optional branches, Museum discoveries, achievements, and costume families. Exact stage geometry, encounter counts, balance data, final assets, and dialogue remain for the individual stage specifications.

#### Approved campaign soundtrack progression

The authoritative music definition is `docs/production/campaign_music_direction.md`. Before this decision, only Stage 2 possessed a complete cue suite; Stage 1 had the shared adaptive architecture without stage-specific music, and Stages 3–8 plus Random Operations had no complete musical families.

The approved families are **Water Held Back** for Stage 1, the unchanged **Prosperity Under Siege** suite for Stage 2, **Mist and Resolve** for Stage 3, **Burden of Blood** for Stage 4, **The Warning Remembered** for Stage 5, **A Village Tested Again** for Stage 6, **Lines behind the Ledger** for Stage 7, and **Strength in Service of Peace** for Stage 8. Random Operations uses **Five Waves, One Stand** for Defeat and **Until Help Arrives** for Safeguard.

All use the Authored Adaptive Score architecture, separate music from gameplay SFX and ambience, protect essential warnings in the mix, require spoiler-safe unlock behavior, and treat generated music as candidate source material requiring human editing, provenance, and commercial-use verification.

### Phase 3 — Refine Stage 3 — completed

Once the campaign map is stable, refine:

1. Confirm the approved Stage 3 localized title and placement approximately 18 months after Stage 2 in the detailed specification.
2. Exact rare-herb fiction and the non-actionable scientific description of the preclinical discovery.
3. Mountain geography, settlements, gathering customs, and bandit camp.
4. Hostage roster, relationships, and rescue structure.
5. Playable roster and any new heroes.
6. Combat boss and Tomas's exact Stage 3 role.
7. Stage 3 ending and Tomas's immediate fate.
8. Mandatory Stage 3 clue demonstrating intermediary support without prematurely revealing the sponsor.

Phase 3 is complete. Stage 3 now has an approved title, chronology, scientific catalyst, research governance, geography, settlement culture, hostages, rescue structure, playable roster, antagonist roles, boss identity and combat direction, Tomas presentation, escape, sponsor clue, canonical outcome, optional branches, Museum content, music, enemies, difficulty progression, mounts, weapons, environmental systems, food, checkpoints, and initial encounter baselines. Exact frame data, final numerical tuning, final dialogue, artwork, animation, audio assets, and map dimensions remain production work.

### Phase 4 — Refine expanded game modes

1. **Mission Mode:** navigation, Chronicle catalogue, campaign framing, first-clear presentation, and Chronicle-to-Remix unlock feedback.
2. **Random Operations:** eligible arenas, heroes and enemies; objectives; difficulty budgets; wave pacing; Safeguard rules; co-op scaling; rewards; records; seed sharing; migration; anti-exploit rules; and validation.
3. **Chapter Challenge:** completion flag, eligible stages, expansion behavior, run and checkpoint rules, retry flow, rankings, solo and co-op records, replay retention, version migration, and any later online-leaderboard assessment.
4. **Academy curriculum audit:** complete the learning path from controls and fundamentals through defense, conversion, resources, hero/co-op specialization, advanced freestyle routes, recording, and mastery assessment without making Academy mandatory for Story completion.
5. **Guide and Academy boundary:** formalize text-reference ownership, interactive lesson ownership, cross-links, search and pause behavior, rewards, accessibility, and a single shared source for stable mechanical facts.
6. **Curated Mission automatic highlights:** deterministic candidate detection, six hero-normalized families, maximum-three diversified selection, default-on bounded capture, layered localized disclosure, co-op attribution, ephemeral result images, opt-in Museum pins, a 50-pin profile budget, export and backup behavior, accessibility, replay reconstruction, performance safeguards, privacy, and strict presentation-only behavior are confirmed. Tiered Replay Retention preserves every result's compact facts and explanations while retaining full capsules for category bests, five recent Curated runs, pinned replays, and Museum-pinned Highlights; older unprotected capsules may become clearly labelled text-only records. The Curated-Only Validation Gate requires all four missions to pass deterministic, performance, quality, localization, accessibility, persistence, and no-reward tests. No automatic analytics upload is permitted, failures may disable capture without losing records or pins, and expansion to another mode requires a separate approved decision. The feature model is complete for the Curated Mission scope.

**Phase 4 status:** Complete and approved. The controlling document is `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-01-fight-for-peace-expanded-game-modes-specification.md`. Exact production catalogues, numerical tuning, commercial pricing, final media, and implementation planning remain future work and do not reopen the approved mode models by default.

**Approved remaining-content refinement order:** Stage 5 feature refinement and specification are approved. Refine *The Medicine Road* next, then Stage 6, *The Vanishing Survey*, Stage 7, and Stage 8. Curated Mission feature rules remain locked while each case receives its route, encounters, objective states, boss phases, resources, optional content, presentation, audio, records, and acceptance specification. Implementation planning remains separate future work.

### Phase 5 — Refine Stage 7: The Hidden Ledger — completed

**Approved Stage 7 route architecture — Six-Link Ledger Exposure Route:** Stage 7 targets approximately **48–56 minutes** for a first clear and **33–41 minutes** for an experienced replay, with **nine mandatory encounters plus the boss**. **Yunzhou Council Forward Office** provides safe briefing, unique-hero/loadout setup, Tomas's controlled evidence handover and initial authentication of his Stage 6 annex. **Shell Consultancy Office** protects isolated witnesses and recovers Damon's acquisition records. **Freight Service Corridor** provides a short electric cargo-vehicle connection while cleanup teams try to intercept witnesses and evidence. **Regional Freight Exchange** recovers Rhea's operational records while protecting workers and shipment histories. **Meridian Forum Public Wing** separates innocent personnel from cleanup forces, evacuates civilians and locates Han's strategy records. **Forum Branch Archive** matches the three records, withstands final erasure pressure and ends with the Compact Erasure Chief captured alive.

Two optional branches reconnect forward without backtracking. Three short safe transitions provide breathing space and evidence review. Active links stream without conventional loading screens. Each major evidence location contributes one indispensable part of the chain. Missed interceptions may worsen evidence condition or ranking, but decisive records remain canonically recoverable. The route escalates from confined witness protection through freight interception and public evacuation to institutional evidence exposure while remaining smaller than Stage 8's final intervention.

**Approved Stage 7 opening — Converging Testimony:** A 25–35-second skippable sequence shows consultancy records being remotely locked/removed, freight cargo diverted by unfamiliar security teams and an unarmed Tomas voluntarily arriving at a Regional Council checkpoint with the Stage 6 annex. It preserves the Compact and steering-circle reveal. At the Forward Office, Tomas formally relinquishes the annex and enters documented lawful protective custody without pardon, automatic forgiveness or family reconciliation. The annex proves urgency but cannot identify the organization or leaders alone. Kael and the Wayfarer Watch arrive independently in response to witness-protection requests. Kael cooperates with village and Council without reconciling with Tomas or joining the patrol. Sheng Guo and the Council prioritize people, original records, live capture and proportionate force.

**Approved Stage 7 playable roster:** Mountain Guardian, River Scout, Wind Sentinel, Fire Warden and canonical guest Kael Rell are selectable. Solo chooses one and co-op two unique heroes. If unselected, Kael remains present through Wayfarer Watch support scenes. Soren Beck and Chen Yue are visible non-playable allies coordinating safe routes and emergency response; their playability remains limited to approved Mission Mode scopes. The briefing provides loadouts, difficulty, accessibility, evidence-condition rules, optional-branch indicators and the robot-support introduction without naming the hidden organization or steering circle.

**Approved Stage 7 evidence model — Authenticated Three-Ledger Chain:** The Shell Consultancy Office yields the **Damon Acquisition Ledger**, documenting shell contracts, concealed subsidies, procurement cutouts and planned seizure of Tomas's assets. The Regional Freight Exchange yields the **Rhea Operations Register**, connecting deployments, witness intimidation, cleanup teams and freight diversion to shared operation identifiers. The Forum Branch Archive yields the **Han Strategy Memorandum**, proving strategic intent, targeted communities and the Compact's two-of-three steering approval system. Each record progresses Unlocated → Identified → Under Erasure → Stabilized → Secured → Authenticated and has Preserved, Reconstructed or Essential Core integrity.

Visible interruptible erasure channels lower integrity one state. An attempted loss below Essential Core triggers a checkpoint-restoring critical preservation failure rather than permanent destruction. Safe-transition authentication compares operation IDs, timestamps, signatures, routing fragments and custody records through accessible controlled UI; reading/access time is excluded and no guess-based classification is required. At the final Archive, Damon proves acquisition/financing, Rhea operational command and Han strategy/approval. Their match reveals the **Meridian Compact**, identifies Han, Rhea and Damon as the steering circle and establishes lawful Stage 8 grounds. Tomas's annex and earlier clues corroborate but cannot replace any ledger. Integrity changes score, achievements, Museum completeness and ending detail, never the canonical reveal or Stage 8 access.

**Approved Stage 7 witness protection — Protected Transfer System:** Three mandatory groups are consultancy auditors/contract staff, freight dispatchers/maintenance workers and Forum archivists/public-service employees. Each progresses Sheltered → Route Authorized → Protected Transfer → Council Safe after heroes clear a short route, open safety access, defend authorization and confirm protected Wayfarer Watch/Council handoff. No follower escort is used. Conditions are Protected, Disrupted after one completed hostile action and alternate routing, or Emergency Extracted after repeated pressure and a slower protected exit. People always survive and have no Health, death, grab, shield or environmental-damage state; Witness Grabbers threaten route/access controls rather than physically carrying civilians.

Mandatory testimony remains available at every condition. Conditions affect commentary, score, achievements, Museum detail and acknowledgements, never ledger authentication. Solo permits at most one total evidence/civilian pressure channel; co-op at most two staggered channels against separate reachable objectives. Warnings identify group, route, direction, consequence and window. Council Safe groups cannot be retargeted or downgraded. A later optional consultancy-witness branch uses a separate supplemental group and cannot change the mandatory case.

**Approved Protected Transfer downgrade clarification:** Zero completed hostile route-control actions yields Protected. The first completed action yields Disrupted and the authored alternate route. The second yields Emergency Extracted and the slower protected emergency exit. Interrupted actions do not downgrade, and further completions cannot worsen the group or farm score. Council Safe permanently locks the earned condition. Solo/co-op thresholds are identical; co-op changes only simultaneous defensive responsibility.

**Approved Technology Disabler target clarification:** One visible authored channel may target only a currently eligible freight control, temporary cleanup/Council barrier or active Field Support Robot effect. Completion pauses a freight control until accessible manual reset, changes a temporary barrier's state once, or suppresses only the active robot effect's remaining duration without changing later allocations. Evidence, people, checkpoints, sole routes, permanent machinery, inactive allocations and future robot availability are ineligible. Every control has manual recovery and no channel begins off-screen.

**Approved Shell Consultancy Office sequence:** Mandatory encounter one, **Open the Safe Route**, covers reception, records corridor and staff safety lobby. Heroes defeat the controlling cleanup team, manually release two remotely locked safety doors, authorize the mandatory consultancy group's protected transfer and interrupt route-control disruption. It introduces Archive Suppressor, Witness Grabber and Shield Escort. Ordinary employees, desks, belongings, accessibility equipment and lawful records are protected; tagged cleanup equipment and barricades are legal combat objects.

Mandatory encounter two, **The Acquisition Ledger**, covers the upper contract suite and records room. Heroes locate the physical Damon Acquisition Ledger, stabilize its signed authentication token/protected mirror reference, defend visible Suppressor channels and secure the package for Council custody. Zero/one/two completed authored erasure actions yield Preserved/Reconstructed/Essential Core; another would restore the checkpoint. Security shutters always have manual release; tagged cleanup partitions may open paths; legitimate cabinets cannot be destroyed/weaponized; tagged cleanup consoles may be disabled. Reduced effects preserve timing. A safe review confirms a match with Tomas's routing fragment without revealing the organization, and the consultancy group reaches Council Safe under its earned condition.

**Approved Freight Service Corridor encounter — Intercept the Cleanup Convoy:** The evacuated commercial connector provides one Council-authorized electric cargo bike in solo and one per hero in co-op for approximately two–three minutes, using the existing mount system rather than a driving engine. Bikes have finite Stability, readable steering, normal attacks and safe/voluntary dismount; a parallel service path supports equivalent on-foot completion. Zero Stability causes safe section-only loss, never failure. Three Pressure Gates are Break the Screen against Shield Escorts, Clear the Barrier through marked release or dismount, and Stop the Credential Transfer before the exchange entrance.

Three successes deny cleanup access advantages; two permit one minor defensive setup; zero/one permit reinforced shutters and one additional ordinary formation slot within the active cap. The courier always reaches the perimeter, so performance changes opening conditions, score and commentary, never evidence integrity, encounter count or canon. The evacuated corridor forbids civilian ramming, explosion, evidence carriage and vehicle weaponization; automatic braking, shared-camera spacing and regroup prevent falls/softlocks. Vehicle state ends at the exchange.

**Approved Regional Freight Exchange sequence:** Mandatory encounter four, **Hold the Dispatch Floor**, clears three connected freight lanes, manually restores the worker-transfer platform, defends freight-worker authorization and prevents a false outbound-route lock. It introduces the Technology Disabler, whose warned local channel temporarily disables one freight control or hero support device but cannot affect civilians, evidence, checkpoints, sole routes or permanent state. The corridor result produces standard formation at three gates, one cleanup barrier at two, or reinforced shutters plus one ordinary slot within cap at zero/one.

Mandatory encounter five, **Orders in Transit**, covers the freight-control gallery and pallet-transfer chamber. Rhea's Operations Register combines a signed command terminal, physical dispatch seals and a protected route-history mirror. Heroes stop the terminal's transfer cage, reconnect the mirror, stabilize orders and defend Council custody. Zero/one/two completed erasures produce Preserved/Reconstructed/Essential Core; another restores checkpoint. Deterministic pallet lifts/carts provide cover or move tagged cleanup equipment, never people/evidence/indispensable controls; safety prevents crush/blockage, players cannot freely drive them, and warned tagged-pallet redirects obey collision cooldown. Safe review matches Damon operation IDs and proves centralized operational command without yet identifying the steering circle.

**Approved Meridian Forum Public Wing sequence:** Mandatory encounter six, **Separate the Public Wing**, covers reception, civic gallery and employee concourse. Council-issued non-color markers/behavior cues distinguish cleanup forces; heroes clear three evacuation routes, restore emergency access and prevent false redirection of Forum archivists/public-service employees. Only marked hostiles are fought. Ordinary Forum security withdraws, surrenders or assists once the Council order is authenticated and is never hostile merely through employment. The mandatory Forum group then uses Protected Transfer.

Mandatory encounter seven, **The Strategy Index**, covers the public research library and restricted archive lobby. Heroes protect the lawful catalogue, recover Han-record archive-location metadata, restore sealed-lift authorization and prevent corruption of the lift handshake. The Index is a non-graded locator/access record, not Han's Memorandum; it cannot be destroyed/downgraded. A completed cleanup action forces a longer manual route affecting time/score only. Exhibits, access systems, community records and belongings are protected; tagged cleanup barriers/false terminals/partitions are legal. Mandatory access always has manual release. Public Forum naming is allowed, but no scene identifies the Meridian Compact before ledger authentication.

**Approved Forum Branch Archive sequence:** Mandatory encounter eight, **The Hidden Ledger**, covers sealed policy stacks and the steering-record chamber. Han's Strategy Memorandum combines a signed policy folio, two-of-three approval-key record and protected meeting-history mirror. Heroes open stacks with the Strategy Index, locate/stabilize the set, interrupt warned Suppressor channels and secure Council custody. Zero/one/two completed erasures produce Preserved/Reconstructed/Essential Core; another restores checkpoint.

Mandatory encounter nine, **Hold the Three Keys**, covers the authentication chamber and emergency transmission floor. A protected Council session compares the three ledgers while physical records remain in documented custody. Heroes activate/defend three nodes, prevent isolation and secure the result. Secured ledger integrity cannot be lowered; completed hostile action pauses/resets only that node's current progress and records one efficiency incident. Completion reveals the **Meridian Compact**, Han/Rhea/Damon roles, two-of-three approval and lawful Stage 8 grounds. The Erasure Chief then locks the external route and enters immediately, with no ordinary wave. Original shelves/history/records are protected; tagged cleanup barriers/emitters/temporary controls are legal, evidence cannot become a weapon, and reduced effects preserve timing.

**Approved Stage 7 Council Field Support Robot:** One shared robot serves solo/co-op through marked Council signal points in selected encounters and a remappable contextual support command; it never permanently follows the heroes. **Mobile Cover** deploys a temporary non-damaging barrier, **Objective Interference** briefly pauses one visible hostile evidence/route/intimidation channel without cancelling it, and **Reserve Delay** delays the next ordinary reserve entry without deleting or changing the budget. One intervention allocation exists per supported encounter and only one effect may run.

Activation is visible/directional/interruptible. First pre-activation disruption retracts safely with one cooldown retry; second consumes the allocation; successful activation consumes immediately. Technology Disablers may temporarily suppress an active effect but cannot destroy the robot or alter later availability. Difficulty cannot weaken support or cues. Checkpoints store allocation, selection, approach, disruption, retry, cooldown, effect and consumption. The robot cannot damage/combo, handle evidence, open/finish mandatory objectives, revive, capture, automatically protect people, or collect items. Supported placements are Office encounter two, Freight encounter one, Public Wing encounter two, Archive encounter one and selected boss moments, each with a complete unassisted solution.

**Approved robot-map clarification:** Exact supported encounters are The Acquisition Ledger, Hold the Dispatch Floor, The Strategy Index, The Hidden Ledger and Silas, each with one independent allocation. Ordinary encounters offer all three interventions. Silas offers Mobile Cover and Objective Interference, which may pause one active Last Redaction step without cancelling/completing it; Reserve Delay is Not Applicable because there are no reserves. One boss allocation spans all phases, never refreshes, and exact pre-boss state restores. Silas may visibly suppress an active effect but cannot remove unused allocation.

**Approved Stage 7 Controlled Urban Equipment Set:** Temporary pickups are a medium-durability **Expandable Control Baton** with complete combat routes, finite single-load **Compact Sidearm**, one-magazine controlled-burst **Compact Automatic**, and one-carried single-use fictional **Concussion Device** with bounded knockback/stun and no protected-target harm. They come only from authorized stage lockers or disarmed cleanup personnel and cannot be pre-equipped, stored, purchased or exported. Firearms have no reload/refill, preserve exact ammo/ownership/drop/disposal state, and enemy finite warned fire ends in punishable empty states.

Authored Intact→Damaged→Broken objects include cleanup office partitions/terminals/barriers/cabinets; ring-controlled freight pallets/frames/barriers/carts; cleanup Forum shutters/checkpoints/kiosks; and Archive emitters/housings/frames. Selected breakage yields one safe lightweight panel/bar/lid/frame, never glass, batteries, live electronics, public records, lockers or safety equipment. Ledgers are **Consultancy and Public Property**, **Freight Safety Infrastructure**, and **Forum and Archive Preservation**, ending Protected at zero avoidable player incidents, Disturbed at one/two and Damaged at three-plus; one cluster counts once. Enemy/required/accessibility/cleanup-owned/scripted damage is exempt. People, evidence, indispensable controls/history/sole routes are immune. Environmental impacts obey collision cooldown, diminishing control, launch limits, elite resistance, Combo Breakers and boss vulnerability; friendly fire is off.

**Approved exclusive property attribution:** Consultancy/Public applies only to lawful Shell Office doors/desks/cabinets/belongings/access equipment/fixtures. Freight Safety applies only to Corridor/Exchange rails, lawful barriers, worker platforms, maintenance controls, non-cleanup pallets and automatic carts. Forum/Archive applies only to public exhibits/community records/access systems/archive shelves/protected controls/supplemental archive. Every legitimate asset has exactly one ledger; cleanup-tagged objects have none. One authored damage cluster counts once, and a cross-object impact attributes once to its originating cluster ledger. Enemy/enemy-thrown/required/accessibility/scripted/protective actions are exempt. No asset can affect two ledgers; attribution and consumed-cluster state persist.

**Approved Stage 7 enemy roster and difficulty:** Core **Archive Suppressor** channels only tagged evidence/authentication nodes and cannot fire a finite sidearm while suppressing; **Witness Grabber** targets evacuation doors/authorization/false-transfer controls, never bodies; finite-Guard **Shield Escort** always exposes flank/throw/Guard-break/impact/co-op counters; and **Technology Disabler** locally targets an eligible freight control, temporary barrier or active support robot without affecting evidence, people, checkpoints, sole routes or later support. Extreme **Erasure Specialist** deploys one portable accelerator for an already eligible Suppressor channel but cannot create targets, hide warning or pass Essential Core. Ultimate **Counter-Intelligence Captain** uses one interruptible finite coordination command, never fabricates evidence/warnings, adds budget or overlaps an Erasure Specialist channel.

Easy separates roles/extends warnings; Medium uses the core; Hard combines it; Extreme cumulatively adds selected Specialists; Ultimate retains them and adds selected Captains. Solo/co-op active caps are Easy 4/6, Medium 5/7, and Hard/Extreme/Ultimate 6/8. Formations are Shielded Erasure, Split Custody, False Transfer, Blackout Corridor, Two-Key Lock and Withdrawal Screen. Solo permits at most one total evidence/civilian/objective channel, one ranged aim and one specialist action; co-op at most two staggered separate reachable objectives. Specialist channels never overlap; no unavoidable crossfire, off-screen action, hidden warning or active-combo spawn. Visible reserves cannot act. Difficulty changes coordination/timing/defense/positioning, not encounter count, primary Health or waves. Boss has no adds.

**Approved Stage 7 boss:** **Silas Kern — The Erasure Chief / 西拉斯·克恩——抹除主管 / Silas Kern – Leiter der Spurenvernichtung**, age 44, is a former corporate compliance/records-security investigator who made truth an asset to control and became Rhea's direct cleanup commander. He is not a steering leader and knows only assignment-relevant operations. **Controlled Access** uses a reinforced expandable baton and finite forearm projection-shield Guard with complete combat routes and legal Exact Block/throw/flank/environment/pressure counters. Robot Mobile Cover/Reserve Delay remains usable without damaging him.

After shield break plus Resolve threshold, **Suppression Geometry** activates three fixed emitters with one warned denial lane solo or at most two staggered safe-path lanes co-op. Silas may visibly suppress an active robot effect. Heroes disable emitters through combat/context; emitters cannot damage evidence/critical controls. At low Resolve, one **Last Redaction** sequence attempts Seal Supplemental Archive → Divert Emergency Power → Trigger Purge. Each success records one Archive Preservation Incident affecting only supplemental Museum/archive condition; the three-ledger chain is immune. Interrupted steps stagger him; full completion triggers emergency isolation after supplemental loss. Either result enters no-reset **Closed File** baton/unarmed combat with no retry, shield/emitter recovery, adds or replenishment. Zero Resolve produces non-QTE disarm, medical assessment, restraint and live Council custody. Silas confirms Rhea's operational authority without replacing proof or revealing Stage 8 tactics.

**Approved Tomas two-step voluntary surrender:** At the opening, an unarmed Tomas voluntarily hands over the original Stage 6 authorization annex, portable abandonment-order copy, retained routing fragment and authenticating device/custody metadata. Multiple witnesses document free handover; Council conducts safety search/medical assessment, states rights/pending allegations and places him in protective lawful custody. He receives no immunity, pardon, sentence reduction, family contact or financial protection. His material corroborates but cannot identify the Compact or replace a ledger.

After independent three-ledger proof, Tomas records responsibility for raids, manipulation, kidnapping conspiracy, fraud and revenge exploitation; withdraws collective punishment of the village; relinquishes claims over seized Compact-linked assets pending lawful restitution; and cooperates without demanding forgiveness or claiming childhood injustice excuses adult choices. Ordinary process may consider cooperation but promises no outcome. Kael is informed but need not meet/speak; his alliance remains independent. Xu Lian is notified lawfully after evidence security. Neither must forgive, and future contact does not affect Stage 8. Tomas is never playable/boss/hostage/objective, his evidence cannot be lost, he learns Compact identity only after independent proof, and surrender is fixed across results/rules.

**Approved Tomas admission clarification:** His recorded statement explicitly accepts financing/planning armed village raids; manipulating Marek, Garran, Kael and other intermediaries through incomplete/false accounts; participating in kidnapping/ransom/leverage conspiracy; defrauding Xu Lian's family and exploiting concealed custody procedure to control Kael's upbringing; building a revenge network that endangered unrelated people; and withholding the Stage 6 annex as leverage after abandonment. He ends collective punishment, submits Compact-linked claims to lawful restitution review, cooperates with individual investigations and states childhood injustice does not excuse adult choices. No pardon, reduction, restored wealth, forgiveness or family contact is guaranteed.

**Approved Stage 7 optional branches and Museum:** **Voices Behind the Door / 门后证言 / Stimmen hinter der Tür** follows Open the Safe Route and precedes The Acquisition Ledger. Heroes open maintenance access, defeat one bounded Grabber/Escort formation, restore an annex relay and authorize a separate protected transfer for supplemental consultancy witnesses who refused altered statements. They always survive and add localized recollections, office-history art and legitimate-employee commentary, never mandatory evidence.

**Routes of the Forgotten / 被遗忘者的路线 / Wege der Vergessenen** follows Orders in Transit before exchange departure. Heroes safely open a dispatch bay, defeat one bounded Suppressor/Disabler formation, stabilize three indestructible victim-route folios through one custody interaction and transfer them. They identify other exploited communities but contain no mandatory steering evidence or gameplay advantage. Any clear grants **Forum Public History Exhibit / 论坛公共历史展 / Ausstellung zur öffentlichen Geschichte des Forums**; both layers create **Forum Accountability Gallery / 论坛问责展廊 / Forumsgalerie der Verantwortung** and eligible **Truth Has Many Witnesses / 真相有众多见证 / Die Wahrheit hat viele Zeugen**. Branches reconnect forward, are active-time compensated, loss-proof/replayable and never gate S/reveal/Stage 8/boss/base Museum. Cheat-Assisted reveals encountered content only; locked entries give localized spoiler-safe clues.

**Approved branch-state clarification:** Voices Behind the Door is Accepted → Maintenance Access Opened → bounded Grabber/Escort Cleared → Annex Relay Restored → Supplemental Protected Transfer Completed → Rejoined. Routes of the Forgotten is Accepted → Dispatch Bay Opened → bounded Suppressor/Disabler Cleared → Three Folios Stabilized → Single Council Custody Transfer Completed → Rejoined. Decline/exit records Skipped without blocking. Checkpoints preserve each accepted step; people/folios are loss-proof; rejoin cannot respawn, duplicate custody/provisions/score/Museum rewards; active time receives approved compensation.

**Approved Stage 7 solo/co-op equivalence — One Investigation, Shared Authority:** Both modes use the same route, nine encounters/boss, three ledgers, witnesses, branches, evidence, vehicle gates, robot allocations, provisions and outcome. Solo uses one hero/one objective channel, one bike, sequential travel-aware timing and compensating interaction speed. Co-op uses two unique heroes, at most two staggered different reachable objective channels, divided roles and two independently tracked bikes without extra gates; scaling adds coverage/roles rather than Health/objective duplication.

Evidence, witnesses, collateral, robot, machinery, gates, branches and boss exist once. Any hero completes any objective; Kael is never required. Host-authoritative first valid interaction resolves conflict; friendly fire/harmful collision stay off; shared camera/regroup prevents splits. Partner down extends only the later objective window without automatic downgrade; Silas permits partner rescue. Host owns all authoritative state. Separate normalized solo/co-op records and informational contribution details apply; rewards are shared and nothing requires co-op. Unselected Kael's scene role grants no advantage.

**Approved Stage 7 Five-Anchor Ledger Snapshot:** Anchors are **Forward Office Departure**, storing configuration/Tomas handover; **Consultancy Secured**, storing Damon/witness/branch/property/robot state; **Freight Exchange Secured**, storing corridor gates/bikes/freight group/Rhea/branch/machinery/equipment; **Public Wing Cleared**, storing Forum group/Strategy Index/property/archive access; and **Three Keys Authenticated**, storing Han/reveal/nodes/supplemental archive/exact pre-Silas state. No mid-boss checkpoint exists; Silas restarts Phase 1 from exact final-anchor resources.

Defeat/voluntary Continue keeps story/Stage 8 but marks Continued with no competitive/economy/achievement/prestige result; Restart is fresh. Disconnect standby and validated same-player reconnect/migration before play preserve class. Solo continuation marks Party Changed at a safe boundary, withdraws absent hero/bike/inventory without transfer, keeps story/Museum/Chronicle/Stage 8 only and cannot regain eligibility through rejoin.

**Approved Stage 7 100-point Evidence and Protection Grade:** Evidence Preservation/Authentication 35 awards each ledger Preserved 10, Reconstructed 7 or Essential Core 4 plus five for clean nodes/custody; Witness/Civilian Protection 25 awards each mandatory group Protected 8, Disrupted 5 or Emergency Extracted 2 plus one for all first routes; Combat/Nonlethal Mastery 20; Property/Operational Discipline 10; Resource/Active-Time 10. Valid clear requires three authenticated ledgers, all groups Council Safe, live Silas and validated completion. Thresholds are C valid below 60, B60, A75, S90.

S requires two Preserved ledgers, third at least Reconstructed, all groups Protected and no Damaged property. One Essential Core caps A; two-plus cap B. One Emergency Extracted caps B. One Damaged property category caps A, two-plus B. At most one Silas purge step may coexist with S if other conditions hold. Each optional branch adds five leaderboard points after rank with compensated time and never gates S. Active time excludes pause/presentation/loading/evidence/access/language/protected dialogue/readiness/recovery/branch compensation. Repetition, harmless damage, surrendered targets, completed routes, robot retries and repeated impacts decay or score zero. Continued/Party Changed/Cheat-Assisted/invalid-integrity runs keep completion/access only, not competitive/economy/achievement/prestige results.

**Approved Stage 7 achievements and rewards:** Eight eligible achievements are stage clear **The Hidden Ledger / 隐秘账簿 / Das verborgene Hauptbuch**; both localized branch titles; combined **Truth Has Many Witnesses**; all ledgers Preserved **Nothing Erased / 一页未失 / Keine Seite ausgelöscht**; all mandatory groups Protected **Every Voice Safe / 众声安然 / Jede Stimme in Sicherheit**; all property categories Protected **Public Trust Preserved / 公信无损 / Öffentliches Vertrauen bewahrt**; and zero Silas purge steps plus live capture **The Last Redaction Stopped / 最后的抹除被阻止 / Die letzte Tilgung gestoppt**. **Stage 7 status:** Feature refinement and the full product/content/technical specification at `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-02-fight-for-peace-stage-7-specification.md` were approved on 2026-11-02. Fresh builder review passed after seven approved consistency clarifications, and the final follow-up returned nothing important left. Implementation planning remains separate.

### Phase 6 — Refine Stage 8: Fight for Peace — completed

**Approved Stage 8 route architecture — Seven-Link Peacekeeping Intervention:** Stage 8 targets approximately **58–68 minutes** for a first clear and **40–50 minutes** for an experienced replay, with **eleven mandatory encounters plus Rhea**. **Regional Council Intervention Staging** provides lawful authority, final briefing, unique-hero/loadout setup, evacuation priorities and evidence-preservation rules. **Forum Public Atrium** enters through public facilities, distinguishes uninvolved employees from hostile formations and opens evacuation routes. **Employee Services Concourse** protects staff, medical-support personnel and accessible routes; the optional medical-wing evacuation branches here. **Logistics and Data-Control Spine** preserves physical archives, backup devices and transmission nodes, stops Damon's deletion and captures him as a noncombatant; the contingency-vault branch occurs here. **Compact Operations Yard** breaks Rhea's organized force. **Transport Hub** provides one bounded vehicle sequence and stops controlled withdrawal. **Steering Command Complex** defeats/captures Rhea alive and prevents Han's public-denial withdrawal, leading to his noncombatant capture.

Two optional branches add approximately seven–ten minutes. Four protected transitions review evacuation, archives, leader custody and epilogue setup. Active links stream without conventional loading and require no backtracking. The vehicle section is not a full mount route. Canon always preserves sufficient evidence, protects civilians and captures Han, Rhea and Damon. Performance changes archive completeness, property, score, achievements, Museum content and epilogue inserts, never the peaceful resolution. The route implements the approved four acts and remains the largest campaign stage without becoming a driving game.

**Approved four-transition contract:** **Evacuation Review** follows Employee Services/its branch, with Employee Services Secured committed before playback and Data-Control Spine afterward. **Archive Review** follows Data Spine/its branch, with Data Spine Secured committed before playback and Damon afterward. **Leader Custody Review** follows Damon's live custody, with Damon in Custody committed before playback and Operations Yard afterward. **Final Accountability Review** follows live Rhea/Han custody; Command Complex Contained remains the pre-Rhea recovery anchor, while both captures and Campaign Completed commit transactionally before playback and the ending follows. All four are skippable/pausable/recap-accessible/active-time-excluded; alter no resources/score/incidents/eligibility/enemies/condition ledgers; resume at the protected boundary after interruption without objective replay/reward duplication. Release validates exact count/order, full/partial skip, interruption recovery, and gameplay-state hash identity except declared commits.

**Approved Stage 8 opening — Mandate to Protect:** A 30–40-second skippable sequence shows the three authenticated ledgers entering off-site Council custody, a judicial intervention order through controlled UI, ordinary Forum operations, Council/civil-protection approach and Rhea activating a controlled lockdown as evacuation indicators appear. It is not framed as a military assault on the whole institution. At Intervention Staging, Council defines protection of uninvolved people, evidence preservation, stopping active coercion and live capture of Han/Rhea/Damon. Sheng Guo rejects revenge/destruction. Kael/Wayfarer voluntarily coordinate external evacuation without joining the patrol. Stage 7 ledgers remain off-site and invulnerable; ordinary employees/contractors are distinguished from confirmed leadership.

**Approved Stage 8 roster:** Mountain Guardian, River Scout, Wind Sentinel, Fire Warden and Kael are selectable; solo chooses one/co-op two unique heroes. Unselected Kael appears only in support scenes without advantage. Soren/Chen Yue remain non-playable Story allies on external routes/emergency assistance. Briefing covers loadouts, difficulty/accessibility, evacuation/archive rules, branches, mature robot support, temporary equipment/vehicle safety, live capture, scoring and fixed canon.

**Approved Forum Public Atrium sequence:** Mandatory encounter one, **Open the Forum Doors**, authenticates the Council order through controlled UI, identifies hostile lockdown personnel via non-color markers/behavior, manually releases two exits and clears routes for visitors/reception/contractors. Ordinary Forum security may surrender, withdraw or assist and is never hostile by uniform/employment. Mandatory encounter two, **Hold the Public Atrium**, defends the exits, stops three route-control pylons, protects the public cohort to Council Safe and prevents a Systems Jammer from disabling both indicators simultaneously. Only completed pylon actions are condition incidents; Jammer suppression is tactical pressure only and never changes cohort condition.

The public cohort progresses Sheltered → Routes Open → Evacuating → Council Safe. Zero completed hostile route-control actions yields Secure; first yields Delayed plus alternate protected route; second yields Emergency Assisted plus slower Council extraction. Interrupted/further actions do not count/worsen/farm, and Council Safe locks condition. People always survive with no Health/death/grab/shield/environment state. Solo permits one evacuation/system channel, co-op two staggered different reachable controls; warnings state route/direction/consequence/window and never begin off-screen. Access systems/civic exhibits/reception/belongings are protected; tagged lockdown pylons/barriers/coercive controls are legal.

**Approved Employee Services encounter:** Mandatory encounter three, **Protect the Support Corridor**, covers rest areas, accessibility transit, first-aid reception and transfer bay. Heroes manually restore three safety partitions, clear marked hostiles and protect the employee-support/first-aid/assisted-worker cohort to Council Safe. Two distinct warned, once-only hostile actions determine condition: **Isolation Lock** separates one protected care zone and **Emergency Re-seal** closes one restored safe partition. Zero completed actions yields Secure, either one yields Delayed, and both yield Emergency Assisted. Interrupted attempts do not count, completed actions cannot repeat or be farmed, and neither can injure/kill/trap/remove a civilian. Formation Guards and Baton Interceptors debut. Partitions cannot crush/trap/block and manual access always exists; marked robot support has an unassisted solution. The encounter ends before the medical-wing branch decision.

First-aid equipment, access devices, employee records/belongings, emergency supplies and consultation spaces are protected and cannot become weapons/provisions/explosives/scenery; tagged lockdown barriers/control units are legal. Experimental formula, herbs and medical supplies never become recovery items.

**Approved Logistics and Data-Control sequence and Archive Completeness Ledger:** Three once-only degradation tokens govern Archive condition: **Physical Record** for completed hostile relocation/inventory corruption in Encounter 4, **Backup Set** for completed loss of authenticated backup redundancy in Encounter 5, and **Transmission Index** for completed victim/asset-index deletion in Encounter 5 or Damon's deletion action in Encounter 6. Damon can affect only Transmission Index. Zero spent tokens is Complete, one Reconstructed, two Essential Set; repeated incidents against a spent token affect efficiency/score only. Mandatory encounter four, **Preserve the Physical Record**, secures three physical archive groups, stops relocation/Jammer inventory actions and transfers custody; records cannot become weapons/environment. Mandatory encounter five, **Hold the Data Spine**, isolates three authenticated backup nodes, maintains one manual transmission path, stops deletion and preserves the victim/asset index. Every node has a slower accessible manual route. The contingency-vault branch decision follows.

If a third distinct token would be spent, a special eligibility-preserving recovery returns to the relevant encounter boundary at Essential Set with the first two tokens committed. It retains committed time, consumed resources and unrelated incidents, never rerolls enemies/restores provisions/enables farming, and is the sole narrow exception to ordinary non-improving restore semantics because it preserves the canonical evidence minimum rather than improving the pre-attempt Archive state.

Mandatory encounter six, **Damon's Last Transaction**, covers acquisition control and administrative suite. Noncombatant Damon visibly attempts Delete Acquisition Index, transfer Compact asset keys, and seal the suite. Index deletion belongs only to Archive Completeness, completed asset-key transfer records the once-only Damon Custody Incident and affects restitution detail, and suite sealing belongs only to efficiency. Heroes defeat protection, secure controls, record/interrupt actions, lawfully open the suite and perform non-QTE custody. Damon has no Health/Defense/Resolve/attacks/weapons/boss phase, cannot be attacked/shield/escape/destroy the case/permanently transfer assets, and receives assessment/rights/documented live custody.

**Approved Compact Operations Yard sequence:** Mandatory encounter seven, **Break the Command Line**, defeats three coordinated formations and disables three command platforms for formation timing, temporary defensive positioning and reserve-route guidance. Benefits are finite/readable and never raise Health, hide warnings or exceed budget. Platforms use combat/context, cannot be completed by robot, and cannot reactivate after secured. Mandatory encounter eight, **Open the Transport Route**, breaks the final yard formation, interrupts two route-seal attempts, secures manual override and protects hub credentials.

The leadership convoy reaches the Hub through fixed protected transition. Zero completed route-seal actions yields **Route Secured** with escort disabled/no barrier; one yields **Route Disrupted** with one active escort/no barrier; two yields **Route Forced** with one active escort/one route-pressure barrier. Tagged Compact barriers/empty frames/training shields/noncritical containers are legal; Council/captured equipment, structure, live power and routes are protected. Machinery safety prevents crush. Shared environmental anti-loop rules apply. The yard is evacuated of civilians.

**Approved Transport Hub encounter — Cut Off the Withdrawal:** A 2–3-minute evacuated section provides one Council electric response bike solo/per co-op hero, existing mount controls, finite Stability, safe/voluntary dismount and equivalent on-foot path; bikes park before Command Complex. Pressure Gates are Close the Departure Lane, Disable the Escort Relay and Secure the Transfer Bridge and can only improve, never reactivate/worsen, the inherited Yard state. Three successes always yield no escort/clear barrier. Two keep Route Secured at none and turn Route Disrupted/Forced into one disabled neutral-cover vehicle while clearing barrier. Zero/one preserve Route Secured at none, Route Disrupted at one active finite position, and Route Forced at that position plus barrier. The barrier is warned alternate-approach/active-time pressure only and cannot block a gate, automatically damage heroes, create a wave, alter canon or affect Rhea.

Han/Rhea reach the Complex through fixed protected route and cannot be attacked here. Bikes cannot ram people/explode/carry evidence/become weapons; escort Stability ends safely, automatic braking prevents falls, shared-camera/regroup applies, on-foot opportunities are equivalent, and all vehicle/gate/bridge/result state restores exactly.

**Approved Steering Command Complex sequence:** Mandatory encounter ten, **Secure the Command Floor**, defeats the command formation, secures three facility stations, opens exits for noncombatant analysts/support staff and resolves the inherited Yard × Hub escort state. Mandatory encounter eleven, **End the Withdrawal**, stops two custody-record destruction attempts, each of which records its own once-only Han Custody Incident if completed, secures transport controls, defeats Rhea's final ordinary formation and closes Han's withdrawal.

Han remains noncombatant and moves by fixed protected transition into a leadership chamber, untargetable. Completion locks external vehicles, places Han under Council containment, removes all ordinary enemies, opens the arena and triggers Rhea. After her live capture, Council opens the chamber and takes Han into non-QTE live custody. He cannot escape/fight/shield/destroy Stage 7 proof/add a boss. Emergency systems/custody records/noncombatant workstations/property/structure/evacuation controls are protected; tagged tactical barriers/emitters/frames are legal.

**Approved final boss — Rhea Calder / 瑞娅·卡尔德:** Approximately 48, Compact Operations Director and final physical boss, she uses the fictional Vector Command System under visible Health/Defense/Resolve, defense-consuming Combo Breakers, anti-infinite rules and live capture. **Vector Authority** is complete empty-hand standing/low/clinch/throw/ground/aerial/Exact Block/Counter combat. Separate finite Command Bracer, Shoulder Deflector and Leg Stabilizer Integrity strengthens selected counters, aerial defense and dash/low resistance; legal targeted strings/throws/environment/counters may break each for one stagger and permanently remove only its benefit, but breakage is optional.

At Resolve threshold, **Baton Directive** adds a finite-Integrity command baton with complete routes, reach/Guard pressure, committed sweeps and one warned player-weapon disarm. It cannot be picked up, leave arena or reset resources; disarm/break uses defined unarmed fallback without endless retrieval. At low Resolve, **Last Order** permanently removes baton and safely disconnects surviving wearable functions; earlier breakage rewards score/shorter transition but intact pieces do not punish. Pure Vector combat uses shorter readable strings, finite-cost conversions and one warned desperation grab with co-op rescue, no tech/heal/reset/adds/escape. Co-op forbids unavoidable dual pressure and bounds carryover. Zero Resolve yields non-QTE disarm/restraint/assessment/live custody; Rhea orders remaining personnel to stand down without exculpation and is removed from command/accountable. No death/escape/secret phase. The three ordinary readable designated achievement actions are Phase-1 **Cross-Axis Palm**, Phase-2 **Directive Arc**, and Phase-3 **Final Vector Strike**; each accepts Exact Block/approved Counter and is never required to win.

**Approved Stage 8 Integrated Civil-Protection Support Network:** One shared robot has six independent encounter allocations: one in Hold the Public Atrium, Protect the Support Corridor, Hold the Data Spine, Break the Command Line, Secure the Command Floor and Rhea. Unused allocation never carries between encounters; consumption/disruption affects only its encounter, and checkpoints preserve each completed encounter result without duplication. Rhea's single allocation spans all three phases, never refreshes on phase transition/mid-attempt retry, and returns to its exact pre-attempt available state only on complete Phase-1 restart. **Mobile Cover** deploys non-damaging cover; **Objective Interference** pauses but never cancels/completes one hostile channel; **Evacuation Guidance** highlights manual route and extends one current interruption window without opening/moving; **Recovery Relay** shortens one hero-performed revive/protected interaction without healing/reviving/completing.

One effect runs. First pre-activation disruption retracts with one cooldown retry; second or success consumes. Jammers suppress current effect only; unassisted solution always exists; difficulty cannot weaken/cue/retry; full state persists. Rhea permits Mobile Cover/Recovery Relay only; other options are Not Applicable. She has one warned suppression of active effect, never unused allocation/resources. Robot cannot attack/stun/carry/open/preserve/capture/drive/complete objectives or affect Rhea Integrity/combos/final hit.

**Approved Stage 8 Final Intervention Equipment Set:** Temporary pickups are medium-durability long-control **Telescoping Response Staff** with complete combat routes, finite single-load **Security Sidearm**, one-magazine controlled-burst **Compact Security Automatic**, and one-carried fictional **Concussion Capsule** with no protected-target harm. They come only from authorized lockers/disarmed hostiles and cannot be pre-equipped/stored/purchased/exported. Rhea's baton is boss evidence only. Firearms have no reload/refill, exact ownership/ammo/disposal and warned finite enemy fire.

Authored Intact→Damaged→Broken objects are tagged lockdown/control equipment in public areas; empty frames/housings/covers/crates in data areas; tactical barriers/frames/shields/escort equipment in yard/hub; and emitters/frames/housings in command. Safe fragments exclude batteries/glass/live electronics/medical/evidence/records/lockers/safety. Exclusive ledgers are Public and Employee Services, Archive and Data Preservation, Operations and Transport Infrastructure, Command and Custody Integrity, each Protected at zero avoidable player incidents, Disturbed one/two, Damaged three-plus; one cluster counts once. Compact tactical objects have none; enemy/enemy-thrown/required/access/protective/scripted actions exempt. People/evidence/medical/custody/critical controls/routes are immune. Shared firearm/friendly-fire/environment anti-loop rules apply.

**Approved Stage 8 enemy roster/difficulty:** Core **Formation Guard** has no objective authority; **Baton Interceptor** uses committed readable aerial/recovery/ground-route counters; finite-Guard **Shield Controller** projects one short directional enemy-only cover field with flank/throw/impact/Guard-break/co-op counters; **Systems Jammer** visibly targets only eligible evacuation/manual control/active robot/noncritical support, never people/evidence/checkpoints/sole routes/permanent/future support. Extreme **Vector Lieutenant** uses a limited readable Counter/formation/ground-recovery derivative, never Rhea's full kit. Ultimate **Command Anchor** plus **Command Striker** occupy two ordinary slots and share a visible finite coordination link broken by separation, Anchor Guard break or Striker Counter; no revive/shared Health/hidden warning/simultaneous objective.

Easy separates/extends; Medium core; Hard combinations; Extreme adds selected Lieutenants; Ultimate retains them/adds selected Pair. Solo/co-op caps are Easy4/6, Medium5/7, Hard–Ultimate6/8. Formations are Controlled Entry, Shielded Intercept, Split Lockdown, Data Suppression, Command Wedge, Withdrawal Screen, Vector Formation. Solo permits one objective/aim/specialist command, co-op two staggered separate channels; advanced coordination cannot overlap critical channel. No crossfire/off-screen/hidden/combo spawn; reserves recover before action; challenge changes behavior not waves/Health. Rhea has no adds.

**Approved Stage 8 optional branches and Museum:** **Care Must Continue / 照护不可中断 / Die Versorgung muss weitergehen** follows Protect the Support Corridor and uses Accepted → Medical Wing Access → bounded Guard/Jammer clear → three care zones stabilized → protected evacuation → rejoin. It preserves people, medical equipment/confidential records and adds support history/response art/commentary; supplies never become items. **Accounts for Every Victim / 每一位受害者都有记录 / Aufzeichnungen für jedes Opfer** follows Hold the Data Spine before Damon and uses Accepted → dual-authorized Contingency Vault → bounded Shield/Jammer clear → victim index stabilized → asset records/three indestructible folios secured → Council custody → rejoin. It enriches restitution, never mandatory evidence/capture.

Any clear grants **Compact Accountability Archive / 联合体问责档案 / Archiv zur Rechenschaft des Kompakts** with exposure proof/leader roles/intervention. Both layers create **Peace and Restitution Collection / 和平与追偿典藏 / Sammlung für Frieden und Wiedergutmachung** and eligible **No One Left Unrecorded / 无人被遗忘 / Niemand bleibt unerfasst**. Branches reconnect/no backtrack, time-compensated, loss-proof, checkpointed and nonduplicating; skip never blocks. They never gate S/evidence/capture/ending/base Museum. Cheat reveals encountered content only; locked clues are localized/spoiler-safe.

**Approved Stage 8 solo/co-op equivalence — One Final Intervention, Shared Outcome:** Both modes use the same seven links, eleven encounters/Rhea, cohorts, Archive Completeness, branches, captures, robot, gates, resources, ledgers and ending. Solo uses one hero/one objective channel, one bike, sequential compensated timing/manual overrides. Co-op uses two unique heroes, at most two staggered separate reachable channels, divided roles and two bikes without extra gates; scaling adds coverage/roles rather than Health/evidence/people/objectives.

All civilian/archive/evidence/branch/property/machinery/robot/gate/Damon/Han/Rhea state exists once. Any hero completes objectives; Kael never required. Host first-valid interaction, no friendly fire/harmful collision, shared camera/regroup and later-window extension after partner down apply without automatic downgrade. Rhea permits rescue and shares all resources/transitions once. Separate normalized solo/co-op records with informational contributions and shared rewards apply; nothing requires co-op and unselected Kael grants no advantage. Host owns all authority.

**Approved Stage 8 Seven-Anchor Final Intervention Snapshot:** Anchors are **Intervention Begins** (configuration/order), **Employee Services Secured** (Atrium/Services cohorts/medical branch/property/robot/resources), **Data Spine Secured** (records/nodes/completeness/vault/systems), **Damon in Custody** (actions/assets/restitution/custody/equipment), **Operations Yard Secured** (platforms/seals/environment/formations/robot), **Transport Hub Secured** (bikes/gates/escort/bridge/Complex opening) and **Command Complex Contained** (evacuation/custody/Han/no ordinary enemies/exact pre-Rhea state). No mid-Rhea checkpoint; Phase 1 restarts exactly.

Snapshot includes complete heroes/resources; cohorts/routes; archives/records/nodes/custody/branches; ledgers/environment/machinery/platforms/bikes/gates; robot; enemies/specialists/links/warnings; Damon/Han; Rhea resources/equipment/phase/capture; score/time/rules/player/network/content integrity. Restore never improves/rerolls/replenishes/duplicates/reverses/reopens/changes conditions. Validated recovery and one consumed transactional Suspend preserve eligibility. Defeat/voluntary Continue keeps ending/postgame but marks Continued without competitive/economy/achievement/prestige; Restart is fresh. Disconnect standby plus reconnect/migration before play preserves; solo continuation marks Party Changed at safe boundary, withdraws absent hero/bike/inventory without transfer, retains story/Museum/ending/postgame only, and rejoin cannot restore eligibility.

**Approved Stage 8 100-point Peacekeeping and Accountability Grade:** Civilian Protection/Evacuation 25; Archive Preservation/Restitution 25; Leadership Apprehension/Custody 20; Combat/Nonlethal Mastery 15; Property/Operational Discipline 10; Resource/Active-Time 5. Mandatory cohort Secure/Delayed/Emergency Assisted and archive Complete/Reconstructed/Essential Set scale their categories; optional civilians are branch bonus only. Damon actions/node incidents count once. Valid clear requires all mandatory cohorts safe, at least Essential Set, live Damon/Rhea/Han and validated completion. Thresholds are C valid below60, B60, A75, S90.

S requires all cohorts Secure, Archive Complete, all four property ledgers no worse than Disturbed and clean documented leader custody. The three-entry **Custody Incident Ledger** records Damon's completed asset-key transfer and Han's two completed record-destruction actions once each; zero is clean. Each completed entry deducts two points from Leadership Apprehension/Custody to a minimum of 14 and adds no separate cap. Interrupted/repeated actions do not count/farm; index deletion is Archive-only, suite sealing efficiency-only, Han record actions custody-only, and no event double-counts. All leaders remain live canonical captures. One Delayed or Reconstructed caps A; Emergency Assisted or Essential Set caps B; one Damaged property caps A, two-plus B. Rhea wearable breakage never required. Each branch adds five post-rank leaderboard points with compensated time and never gates S. Active-time/access exclusions and anti-farming apply. Continued/Party Changed/Cheat-Assisted/invalid-integrity retain ending/postgame only, no competitive/economy/achievement/prestige result.

**Approved Stage 8 achievements and rewards:** Eight eligible achievements are stage clear **Fight for Peace / 为和平而战 / Kampf für den Frieden**; both localized branch titles; combined **No One Left Unrecorded**; all mandatory cohorts Secure **Everyone Returns Safely / 人人平安归来 / Alle kehren sicher zurück**; Archive Complete **The Full Account / 完整记录 / Die vollständige Akte**; all four property ledgers Protected **Peace without Ruin / 和平而不毁灭 / Frieden ohne Zerstörung**; and **Every Vector Answered / 向量皆有应对 / Jede Richtung beantwortet**. The last requires Exact Block/approved Counter against Cross-Axis Palm, Directive Arc and Final Vector Strike plus live Rhea capture in one uninterrupted attempt. Phase transitions preserve, full Phase-1 restart clears and mid-phase retry cannot preserve/duplicate progress. Co-op tracking is team-shared/either hero may answer. It never requires wearables/baton break/hero/co-op/difficulty and normal eligible-run rules apply.

Any canonical clear unlocks completed campaign, all Story Chapter Challenges, full Chronicle/completed-stage Remixes/final Museum/credits/postgame difficulties; ineligible clears retain access. First eligible clear grants Credits/outfit visibility; first A text-free Open Hands and Shield emblem; first S **Guardian of Peace / 和平守护者 / Hüter des Friedens**; first eligible Ultimate **Peacekeeper of the Eight Paths / 八程和平守望者 / Friedenswächter der acht Wege**. Higher difficulties add marks, rewards bounded/improvement-only. No non-branch achievement requires branch/co-op/hero/purchase/costume; nothing grants power/changes ending/uses Story Highlights. Ineligible clears get narrative/postgame/catalogue only, no competitive/economy/achievement/prestige.

**Approved Stage 8 Coordination Center Provision Set:** Health **Employee Meal Pack / 员工餐包 / Mitarbeiter-Mahlzeitpaket**; Defense **Emergency Hydration Pouch / 应急补水袋 / Notfall-Trinkbeutel**; Attack **Response Energy Chew / 应急能量软糖 / Einsatz-Energiekaubonbon**; balanced **Coordination Center Meal / 协调中心餐盒 / Mahlzeit der Koordinationszentrale**. Medium solo has two each common/one Meal, co-op three each/one shared Meal, placed in safe Atrium, Employee Services, Data transition, Yard staging and pre-Rhea. Easy adds one each; Hard/Extreme/Ultimate remove one/two/three commons; Meal remains.

Branches give none. Medical supplies/formula/herbs/medication/equipment never become items. Authorized kitchens/break areas/Council caches/handoffs and distinct containers prevent evidence/medical/custody/weapon confusion. Shared J/state applies; FULLPANTRY ordinary eligible containers only, never Meal/branches/weapons/evidence/medical/rewards. Effects make no medical/nutrition claims.

**Approved Stage 8 visual evolution and costumes:** Mountain Guardian, River Scout, Wind Sentinel, Fire Warden and Kael receive free final-campaign canonical defaults using mature practical protective fabrics, style-specific joint protection, improved boots/gloves/weather/communications and affiliation identity without stats/hitbox/automatic upgrade; village heroes remain civil-protection and Kael Wayfarer. Sheng Guo/key civilians receive consistent modern non-playable clothing. Canonical generated media uses these defaults.

Purchasable character-specific visual-only families are **Regional Peacekeeping Coordination Outfit / 区域和平维护协调装 / Regionale Friedensschutz-Koordinationsausrüstung**, professional flexible field attire with text-free Council/affiliation cues and no military camouflage/holsters/bulky armor/Compact marks; and **Peace and Reconciliation Festival Attire / 和平与和解庆典礼服 / Festkleidung für Frieden und Versöhnung**, contemporary celebration clothing with water/mountain/open-hand/repaired-bridge/shared-harvest motifs and no slogans/political/Compact glorification. Designs cover five Story heroes plus unlocked Soren/Chen Yue/Reed Warden. First eligible clear reveals per-hero Credit purchases; prior ownership persists. All are meaningful visual-only mode-eligible designs with no mechanics/story/eligibility changes. Ending generated media uses defaults; authored in-engine celebration/replay may use selections. Locked entries use localized spoiler-safe clues.

**Approved Stage 8 ending — Three-Horizon Peace Ending:** Immediate accountability gives live medical/documented custody to Rhea, noncombat arrest to Han, confirms Damon, secures archives/assets, individually reviews remaining personnel and places Forum services under independent administration. No leader escapes/dies/immunity/higher mastermind: Han/Rhea/Damon conclusively form the Compact steering circle. Weeks/months later, assets support lawful restitution; victim claims, legitimate Forum preservation, individual employee review, leadership/Tomas separate proceedings, no automatic pardon, historical family injustice acknowledgement with Oren guilt, and permanent anti-collective-punishment rule are shown. Performance changes completeness/repairs/recognition/Museum detail only.

The village return shows humane infrastructure/technology, heroes among rather than above people, Sheng Guo linking accountable strength to peace, independent cooperative Wayfarer Watch and continued protective training. Kael receives Xu Lian's neutral invitation and the localized profile-stored noncombat choice **Meet Now / Reply and Meet Later / Not Yet**, changing one still/Chronicle note only—no score/reward/gameplay/canon; Not Yet is not permanent rejection and Tomas is absent. Final image is the modern multipurpose bridge at sunrise over restored river, equipment at rest. Sheng says: **“Strength does not create peace. It protects the chance to build it.” / “力量并不能创造和平；它守护的是建设和平的机会。” / „Stärke schafft keinen Frieden. Sie schützt die Möglichkeit, ihn aufzubauen.“**

**Approved Stage 8 Guide and Academy:** Searchable localized **Final Intervention and Peacekeeping / 最终干预与和平维护 / Letzte Intervention und Friedenssicherung** covers lawful identification, cohorts, archives/manual paths, noncombat custody, support network, bike, equipment, ledgers, spoiler-safe Rhea, scoring/branches/cheats/checkpoints/postgame; full Compact/restitution/epilogue details unlock after clear.

Optional **Peacekeeping Mastery Course / 和平维护精通训练 / Meisterkurs Friedenssicherung** uses synthetic Identify before Acting, Open the Safe Route, Preserve the Archive, Coordinate Civil Support, Control the Transport Hub, Secure without Fighting and Answer the Vector lessons, plus post-clear configurable **Fight for Peace Trial** combining systems/full Rhea profile. Established demo/guided/test/free/reset/partner/unique-co-op/My Combos and assisted/unassisted paths apply. Synthetic assets prevent spoilers; full Rhea waits for clear. Training transfers/grants nothing and never gates ending. Full EN/zh-Hans/DE/access inputs required.

QUIETCROWD retains selected Lieutenants/Pairs/objective pressure/leader outcomes/discoveries/Rhea; FULLPANTRY ordinary food only, excluding Meal/branches/weapons/evidence/medical/rewards; HOMECOMING exact anchor. Archive stores configuration/eligibility; every cohort/archive/Damon/restitution/branch/property/robot/equipment/vehicle/enemy/leader/Rhea/score/checkpoint/network/ending/Museum/reward/Kael-choice fact; no Story Highlight data. Eligible/ineligible histories separate, compatible personal-best keys strict and older results Legacy.

**Approved Stage 8 presentation/localization/credits/accessibility:** Skippable 30–40-second silent small-motion **Mandate to Protect / 守护之令 / Mandat zum Schutz** shows safe evidence, lawful preparation, ordinary Forum, arrival and lockdown; legal detail is controlled UI. Skippable 30–45-second **The Last Order Ends / 最后的命令终结 / Der letzte Befehl endet** shows live leader custody, archives/assets, safe employees and independent administration with bounded condition stills. ## 17. Update procedure

**Permanent campaign rule:** `story_line.md` is the authoritative living continuity document for all story refinement from the next stage through the final ending. A future storyline decision is not fully logged until this file has been updated. Stage-specific notes and specifications may add implementation detail, but they must not replace this campaign-wide record.

When a new story decision is approved:

1. Add it to the relevant stage or long-term section.
2. Update the chronology and the character or knowledge ledger if the decision changes persistent state.
3. Remove the item from the open-decisions list or narrow the unresolved portion.
4. Check the change against both completed stages and every confirmed sponsor clue.
5. Record whether the decision is Canon, Planned, Concept, Unresolved, or a Production detail.
6. Update any affected stage-specific storyline or specification only after campaign continuity has been reconciled here.

The purpose of this process is not to prevent new twists. It is to ensure that every twist grows from what the campaign has already established.
